### [1] [AgentRoboCritic: Understanding the Capability of Frontier Agents on Direct Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26613)
*Yiyang Du,Yuxuan Kuang,Qitao Zhao,Yixuan Wang,Zhanqiu Guo,Yi Xu,Xin Li,Wenbin He,Liu Ren,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对前沿多模态语言智能体在12个机器人操作基准上的直接控制进行动作级分析，提出AgentRoboCritic批评框架，并发布标注数据集与微调的开源批评模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅报告回合级成功率，无法揭示智能体如何成功或失败，缺乏对直接机器人控制中动作级表现的系统理解。

Method: 提出AgentRoboCritic意图接地批评框架：策略智能体为每个动作声明局部意图，批评者评估动作结果与失败模式；在12个操作基准上分析前沿智能体，并在400动作人工标注基准上验证批评者，微调开源模型Muse-Glimmer-30B。

Result: 动作级分析显示前沿智能体具备闭环恢复、语义接地和零样本操作能力，但存在抓取与对齐等物理执行瓶颈及失败模式恢复不均衡；微调后Muse-Glimmer-30B失败模式准确率从32.58%提升至74.24%，并发布12,844条标注动作数据集。

Conclusion: 动作级批评框架能低成本揭示智能体直接机器人控制的优势与局限，为未来改进提供可解释的诊断工具。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal language agents such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated strong capabilities in controlling robots directly via low-level numerical actions without high-level primitives. However, existing benchmarks report only episode-level success rates, which show neither how these agents succeed nor where they fail. In this paper, we present a systematic action-level analysis of frontier agents on direct robot control across 12 manipulation benchmarks. We introduce AgentRoboCritic, an intent-grounded critique framework in which the policy agent states a local intent alongside each action, and a critic evaluates the action's outcome and failure mode against this intent. Our action-level analysis demonstrates the promising capabilities of frontier agents on direct robot control, including closed-loop recovery from local execution failures, reliable semantic grounding across diverse scenes, and strong zero-shot manipulation without task-specific adaptation. It also identifies their current limitations, including task-relevant physical execution bottlenecks such as grasping and alignment, and uneven recovery across failure modes. Finally, we validate our critic on a 400-action human-annotated benchmark and release a dataset of 12,844 annotated actions alongside a fine-tuned open-source critic, Muse-Glimmer-30B, whose failure-mode accuracy rises from 32.58% to 74.24%, enabling low-cost action-level analysis.

</details>


### [2] [A Tutorial on Complementarity-Based Contact Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26600)
*Onur Beker,A. René Geist,Michael Posa,Marc Toussaint,Justin Carpentier,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本教程填补了机器人领域缺乏自包含教学材料的空白，系统连接刚体动力学、锥优化与摩擦接触，涵盖互补条件、求解器比较及实际应用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多面向计算力学或计算机图形学，或仅记录单一模拟器，缺乏面向机器人领域的自包含教学材料，以统一符号和完整推导整合锥互补 formulations 与求解器。

Method: 通过将刚体动力学、锥优化与摩擦接触相结合，解释互补条件如何编码非穿透与摩擦耗散，区分等价转录与凸松弛，分析运动与接触力的存在唯一性，比较一阶与牛顿型方法，并讨论正则化与近端策略。

Result: 揭示了凸松弛可能引入抬升和蠕变等伪影，阐明了求解器在多解选择中的作用，并对比了不同方法在计算成本、鲁棒性与物理保真度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本教程帮助读者解释模拟结果、选择合适的 formulations 与求解器，并开发自己的接触动力学程序，连接了常用模拟器、最优控制与可微分模拟。

Abstract: Simulating rigid-body dynamics with contact is fundamental to robotics, from motion synthesis and control to mechanism design. Many widely used simulators rely on cone complementarity formulations, whose physical assumptions and numerical solution require substantial background. While excellent surveys, monographs, and course notes exist, they mostly address computational mechanics or computer graphics or document a single simulator. To the best of our knowledge, a self-contained pedagogical treatment for robotics, bringing these formulations and their solvers together with complete derivations and a unified notation, does not yet exist. This tutorial addresses this gap by connecting rigid-body dynamics, conic optimization, and frictional contact. In particular, we explain how complementarity conditions encode non-penetration and frictional dissipation, determining separation, sticking, and sliding without prescribed contact modes. We distinguish equivalent transcriptions from convex relaxations, showing how the latter can introduce artifacts such as lift-off and creep. We examine the existence and uniqueness of motion and contact forces, including the solver's role in selecting among multiple solutions. We then compare first-order and Newton-based methods, explain regularization and proximal strategies, and discuss trade-offs between computational cost, robustness, and physical fidelity. Connections to widely used simulators, optimal control, and differentiable simulation help readers interpret simulation results, choose suitable formulations and solvers, and develop their own contact dynamics routines.

</details>


### [3] [PGX: Physically Grounded Execution for Long-Horizon Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26596)
*Kai Lu,Chenyang Ma,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PGX框架，将长时程移动操作中的时序与精度控制交由两个免训练经典组件处理，使VLA模型专注阶段技能动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视觉-语言-动作模型能执行多样单一操作技能，但在时间结构、技能与精度紧密耦合的单体模型中难以完成长时程移动操作任务。

Method: PGX将长时程移动操作分解为三层：(i)基于谓词的状态机，用物理谓词控制阶段转换、触发恢复并协调导航与等待，共享谓词语义连接离线演示分割、在线执行和分阶段评估；(ii)VLA技能在主动操作阶段内运行，平面或分阶段训练的模型专注任务相关动作；(iii)世界坐标锁定（WCL）通过基站保持、物体相对定位和世界锚定接触目标维持空间一致性。

Result: 在三个仿真任务和一个使用Unitree G1人形机器人的真实任务上进行了大量实验，阶段推进和任务成功率均有所提升。

Conclusion: PGX通过将时序与精度控制卸载到免训练经典组件，有效提升了长时程移动操作任务的阶段推进与成功率。

Abstract: Robotic vision-language-action (VLA) models can execute diverse individual manipulation skills, yet they still struggle with long-horizon loco-manipulation tasks, where temporal structure, skills, and precision are tightly coupled within a monolithic model. We propose Physically Grounded Execution (PGX), a framework that offloads temporal and precision control to two classical training-free components, allowing the VLA to focus on stage-specific skill dynamics. PGX decomposes long-horizon loco-manipulation into three layers: (i) a predicate-based state machine governs task progression, using physical predicates to gate phase transitions, trigger recovery, and coordinate navigation and waiting. Shared predicate semantics connect offline demonstration segmentation, online execution, and stage-wise evaluation; (ii) VLA skills operate within active manipulation phases, with flat or stage-trained models concentrating on task-relevant actions; and (iii) world-coordinate locking (WCL) maintains spatial consistency through base station keeping, object-relative positioning, and world-anchored contact targets. We demonstrate the effectiveness of PGX with extensive experiments conducted on three simulated tasks and one real-world task using a Unitree G1 humanoid robot for long-horizon loco-manipulation, showing improvements in stage progression and task success rates. Project page is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [4] [PhyDEX: A Benchmark for Physical Reasoning in Vision-Language-Action Models via Dynamic Extrinsic Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26565)
*Hao Vo,Taisei Hanyu,Yuki Ikebe,Pham Tri Quang,Sreevenkata Anjani Tishita Godavarthi,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Daniel Sonntag,Chase Rainwater,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Anh Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhyDEX抛掷基准，揭示现有VLA模型在物理动态操作中成功率低于3%，主要受限于预训练鲁棒性不足与物理盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基准主要评估夹爪与物体的接触操作，忽略了重力、摩擦、惯性、质量分布等环境影响，无法判断策略是否真正理解物理规律。

Method: 构建PhyDEX抛掷基准，包含9任务、100场景、3.5万条轨迹（143小时），覆盖均匀/不规则/动态质量物体及静态/多/移动目标；数据由抓取生成、运动规划与闭环抛掷优化全自动流水线生成，在随机物理属性下评估GR00T N1.7、DynamicVLA、ForceVLA三个代表性模型。

Result: 三个VLA成功率均低于3%；失败主因是预训练模型鲁棒性有限导致抛掷前失败，以及物理盲区——提供真实物理属性可使成功率约翻倍；在人形机器人与真实世界中观察到相同失败模式。

Conclusion: PhyDEX有望推动具备物理基础的机器人策略发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly in language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks mainly evaluate gripper--object contact and largely ignore how objects interact with the environment through gravity, friction, inertia, and mass distribution. As a result, it remains unclear whether current policies truly understand physics or merely interpolate from their training data. We address this gap through the lens of dynamic extrinsic dexterity and introduce PhyDEX, a throwing benchmark in which a robot must infer an object's mass, center of mass, and friction to send it to a destination beyond its kinematic workspace. PhyDEX contains 35K trajectories (143 hours) across nine tasks and 100 scenes, covering uniform, irregular, and dynamic-mass objects with static, multiple, and moving destinations. All data are generated by a scalable, fully automatic pipeline that combines grasp generation, motion planning, and closed-loop throw optimization under randomized physical properties. We evaluate three representative VLAs, GR00T N1.7, DynamicVLA, and ForceVLA, and find that all achieve success rates below 3\%. Our analysis reveals two key bottlenecks: limited robustness of pretrained models, which causes most failures before the throw, and physical blindness, as providing ground-truth physical properties roughly doubles success. Experiments on a humanoid robot and in the real world show the same failure patterns. We hope PhyDEX will drive the development of physically grounded robot policies.

</details>


### [5] [ACE-Brain-0.7: Learning Execution-Grounded Reasoning for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26519)
*Yinhe Wang,Shijun Shi,Feng Pan,Tao Tao,Wei Mao,Jiangning Zhang,Zhiyao Ren,Yihang Jiao,Haoming Gu,Yunpeng Qing,Jiyu Guo,Zhi Hou,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出9B视觉语言模型ACE-Brain-0.7，融合空间定位、任务理解与进度估计，在六项任务推理基准上平均76.5分，超越GPT-6 Astra与Claude Opus 5.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务需要基于观测执行状态的可靠推理，因此需将空间感知与时间理解结合，让任务级判断与实际执行进度和结果相连接。

Method: 构建370万问答对，监督信号来自第一人称人类视频、机器人轨迹与仿真，覆盖执行的不同阶段；在Qwen3.5-9B上结合任务、空间定位与进度估计数据微调。

Result: 六项公开任务推理基准平均76.5分（GPT-6 Astra 72.7、Claude Opus 5.5 73.5）；七项空间定位基准平均75.90，RefCOCOg上Acc@0.5达91.25；受控实验显示任务专用模型更有效利用视觉历史并更准确区分执行状态。

Conclusion: ACE-Brain-0.7有效结合空间感知与时间理解，实现强执行 grounded 推理，性能优于闭源模型。

Abstract: Reliable reasoning for long-horizon embodied tasks requires predictions grounded in the observed execution state. We present ACE-Brain-0.7, a 9B vision--language model that combines spatial grounding, task understanding, and progress estimation. To train the model, we curate 3.7 million question--answer pairs, drawing execution supervision from egocentric human videos, robot episodes, and simulation. These questions span different stages of an execution and connect task-level judgments with observed progress and outcomes. We then fine-tune Qwen3.5-9B on this collection together with additional task, spatial-grounding, and progress-estimation data. Across six public task-reasoning benchmarks, ACE-Brain-0.7 achieves an average score of 76.5, ahead of GPT-6 Astra at 72.7 and Claude Opus 5.5 at 73.5. Our model also leads the evaluated models in spatial grounding, averaging 75.90 across seven benchmarks and reaching 91.25 Acc@0.5 on RefCOCOg. Controlled evaluations further indicate that our task-only model makes more effective use of visual history and more accurately distinguishes execution states. Together, these results show that ACE-Brain-0.7 effectively combines spatial perception with temporal understanding, delivering strong execution-grounded reasoning and comparing favorably with closed-source models.

</details>


### [6] [An Open-Source ROS 2 Framework for Shared Control in Surgical Teleoperation: Integrating the Haply Inverse3 with the da Vinci Research Kit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26485)
*Balint Varga,Sebastian Fritz,Gergely Kiss,Kristof Takacs,Peter Galambos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个开源、原生支持ROS 2的框架，将三自由度Haply Inverse3触觉设备与da Vinci研究套件集成，并评估了两种虚拟夹具引导模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术远程操作缺乏触觉反馈，现有商用触觉力反馈平台闭源，阻碍了触觉共享控制的开放研究。

Method: 作者开发了一个集成3-DOF Haply Inverse3与dVRK的开源ROS 2框架，采用将3-DOF平移输入与单独跟踪的VerseGrip姿态组合为6-DOF患者侧机械臂命令的映射策略，并以两种虚拟夹具引导模式作为参考实现，在仿真热丝基准任务中开展被试内用户研究，并在物理dVRK硬件上演示。

Result: 与无辅助手动控制相比，线性引导显著减少了导线接触次数和任务完成时间；渐进刚度引导呈一致但统计不显著的同向趋势；主观负荷、能动性和满意度对引导模式呈有利的描述性趋势。

Conclusion: 结果证明了紧凑设备与dVRK耦合实现触觉共享控制的可行性，并提供了开放可复现的研究平台；同时表明更简单的线性反馈对缺乏经验的操可能比复杂反馈更稳健。

Abstract: Surgical teleoperation deprives surgeons of touch, forcing visual inference of tissue forces; the commercial platforms now restoring force feedback remain closed, preventing open research on haptic shared control. This paper provides a system contribution: it presents an open-source, ROS 2-native framework that integrates the compact, three-degree-of-freedom (3-DOF) Haply Inverse3 haptic device with the da~Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). The framework includes a novel mapping strategy that combines 3-DOF translational device input with separately tracked VerseGrip orientation into 6-DOF patient-side manipulator commands. Two virtual-fixture-inspired guidance modes serve as reference implementations to evaluate the framework in a within-subjects user study on a teleoperated hot-wire benchmark task in simulation, and the architecture is further demonstrated on physical dVRK hardware. Relative to unassisted manual control, linear guidance significantly reduced both the number of wire contacts and the task completion time, whereas the progressive-stiffness guidance produced consistent but statistically non-significant trends in the same direction. Subjective ratings of workload, agency, and satisfaction showed favorable descriptive trends for the guidance modes. The results demonstrate the feasibility of haptic shared control with a compact device coupled to the dVRK and provide an openly available, reproducible platform for future research. They further suggest that simpler linear feedback may be more robust for inexperienced operators than more complex profiles.

</details>


### [7] [Mind2Act World: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Embodied Mind, Physical Action, and Mind-Action Composition in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26444)
*Yuzhi Huang,Jie Wu,Fanding Huang,Junchao Lin,Weichen Zhang,Ziyi Xiong,Yang Li,Zhengwei Xie,Zhimin Wang,Zhen Liu,Jiazhen Huang,Yuzheng Zhang,Yuche Liu,Hongyi Yang,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang,Hakan Aktas,Adam Wu,Duo Wu,Kangye Ji,Shuzhao Xie,Huanan Liu,Weijue Bu,Chenxin Li,Xinyuan Wang,Tianyi Bai,Yinan Mao,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Heng Yu,Ehsan Adeli,Minchen Li,Yong-Lu Li,Cengiz Öztireli,Huazhe Xu,Chao Yu,Jing Zhang,Shanghang Zhang,Weitao Zhou,Wenwu Zhu,Cheng Chi,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Mind2Act World诊断基准，包含30个模拟操作任务、180种配置，分Mind（认知推理）、Act（物理执行）、Mind2Act（闭环组合）三个视图，用于区分系统是缺乏能力还是无法组合能力；并配有诊断工具将失败归因于推理、执行或组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么在同一任务中同时评估推理与执行，要么将二者分开评估而不考察其组合，因此难以判断失败究竟来自推理、执行还是二者的组合，无法有效诊断具身智能系统的核心短板。

Method: 构建包含5个真实应用领域、30个模拟操作任务的基准，每任务3个难度等级×2种场景设置（Clean与Random），共180种配置；设计Mind（基于历史观测做决策）、Act（精确及时执行）、Mind2Act（动作结果反馈指导后续决策的闭环任务）三个视图；并引入诊断框架，将三个代表性系统的失败片段归因于Mind、Act或其组合，生成系统画像卡。

Result: 评估多模态智能体、视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）和世界-动作模型（WAM）发现：单一视图的能力无法迁移到Mind2Act任务；最强智能体在Mind和Act上分别成功52.7%和39.0%，但在Mind2Act上仅12.0%；最强智能体能保持任务状态却不擅长持续接触，而端到端策略能执行熟练动作但几乎不保留任务状态。

Conclusion: 通过环境反馈组合推理与执行（即Mind2Act闭环能力）是当前具身智能的主要开放问题，单一能力上的优势不足以支撑复杂物理任务的可靠完成。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence aims to let robots perform complex physical tasks with human-like adaptability and reliability, which requires three complementary capabilities: history-based cognitive reasoning, precise and timely physical execution, and the composition of reasoning and execution through environmental feedback. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate reasoning and execution within the same tasks or assess them separately without their composition, so they reveal little about whether a failure comes from reasoning, execution, or their composition. We introduce Mind2Act World, a diagnostic benchmark of 30 simulated manipulation tasks from five real-world application domains. Each task has three difficulty levels and two scene settings (Clean and Random), yielding 180 configurations. The tasks form three views: Mind tests decisions based on past observations, Act tests precise and timely execution, and Mind2Act combines both in closed-loop tasks where action outcomes guide later decisions. Comparing the three views shows whether a system lacks a capability or fails to compose capabilities it already has. A diagnosis harness further attributes the failed episodes of three representative systems to Mind, Act, or their composition and summarizes each system in a profile card. Our evaluation of multimodal agents, vision-language-action models (VLAs), and world-action models (WAMs) shows that competence in a single view does not carry over to Mind2Act tasks. Even the strongest agent succeeds in 52.7% of Mind and 39.0% of Act episodes but in only 12.0% of Mind2Act episodes. The strongest agent retains task state but struggles with sustained contact, whereas end-to-end policies execute practiced motions but retain little task state. These findings point to composing reasoning and execution through environmental feedback as the main open problem.

</details>


### [8] [Awaken-HI: Scaling Humanoid Intelligence with Whole-Body Human Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26420)
*EngineAI*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Awaken-Human提供约5万小时同步全身运动的自我中心立体视频数据集，覆盖100多种技能；基于此提出的Awaken-HI模型通过共享动作专家联合预测人体运动与重定向机器人动作，实现人形机器人移动操作技能的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需完成日常家务与工作，但现有自我中心视频数据集缺乏大规模同步全身运动数据，难以支撑移动操作技能的学习。

Method: 构建Awaken-Human数据集（约5万小时自我中心立体视频+同步全身人体运动，覆盖100+技能）；提出视觉-语言-动作模型Awaken-HI，通过共享动作专家联合预测原始人体运动与重定向机器人动作，并用人体轨迹直接监督该共享专家。

Result: 人体预训练数据增加使留出机器人动作预测误差按经验缩放律下降，并提升下游真机任务平均成功率；全语料预训练加T800适配后，八项任务平均成功率达93.75%，单项与长程评测均优于GR00T N1.7和π0.5，且策略可泛化至未见物体及熟悉物体的新组合目的地。

Conclusion: 人体轨迹直接监督共享动作专家可更有效地将移动操作技能从人迁移至人形机器人，扩大人体预训练数据规模能持续降低动作预测误差并提升真机成功率。

Abstract: A central goal of humanoid robotics is for robots to perform the everyday work people do in homes and workplaces. Existing egocentric video datasets cover many of these activities but provide little synchronized whole-body motion data for learning loco-manipulation at scale. We build Awaken-Human, which pairs approximately 50,000 hours of egocentric stereo video with synchronized whole-body human motion and covers more than 100 skills. Building on this, we also introduce Awaken-HI (Humanoid Intelligence), a vision-language-action model for humanoid loco-manipulation that jointly predicts original human motion and retargeted robot actions through a shared action expert. We show that loco-manipulation skills transfer more effectively from people to humanoids when human trajectories directly supervise the shared action expert. Increasing human pretraining data reduces held-out robot action prediction error according to an empirical scaling law and raises the mean success rate on downstream real-robot tasks. After full-corpus pretraining and T800 adaptation, Awaken-HI achieves a mean success rate of 93.75% across eight individual tasks and outperforms GR00T N1.7 and $\pi_{0.5}$ in both individual and long-horizon evaluations. The same policy generalizes to objects unseen during robot post-training and to new pairings of familiar objects and destinations.

</details>


### [9] [TacInno: Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models the Contact Dynamics Beyond Vision and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26405)
*Weile Li,Yunfan Lou,Muqing Shi,Zihao Zhang,Zhenbiao Huang,Boxian Yao,Xiyu Zhu,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacInno方法，仅将视觉与动作无法解释的触觉创新作为离散编码训练目标，部署时无需额外模块，在仿真和真实任务上显著超越最强视触觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉VLA模型对触觉输入利用不足，因为许多触觉变化（如夹爪闭合时的凝胶形变）可由视觉观察和执行动作预测，导致触觉增益不稳定。

Method: TacInno以视觉观察和执行动作为条件，用分词器将未来触觉变化压缩为离散编码（触觉创新），策略TacInno-VLA学习预测该编码，部署时不增加编码模块。

Result: 在六个仿真任务上平均成功率达87.5%，四个真实任务上达70.5%，分别比最强视触觉基线高10.7和9.0个百分点；消融实验验证了视觉与动作条件、多帧编码及辅助编码预测的贡献。

Conclusion: 触觉创新可作为接触丰富操作的有效监督信号，提升视触觉VLA模型的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently been equipped with tactile sensing to perceive visually subtle contact events in contact-rich manipulation. However, adding tactile input yields inconsistent gains, and some visuo-tactile VLA models rely little on tactile input, because much of the tactile change, such as the gel deformation during gripper closure, is predictable from visual observations and executed actions. To address this problem, we introduce TacInno, which isolates the contact dynamics that vision and executed actions cannot explain, termed tactile innovation, and uses tactile innovation only as a training target. Specifically, the TacInno tokenizer, conditioned on visual observations and executed actions, compresses the future tactile change into a discrete code, and the policy, TacInno-VLA, learns to predict the code, adding no module for the code at deployment. TacInno-VLA achieves average success rates of 87.5% across six simulation tasks and 70.5% across four real-world tasks, outperforming the strongest visuo-tactile baseline by 10.7 and 9.0 percentage points, respectively. Ablation studies validate the contributions of visual and action conditioning, multi-frame encoding, and auxiliary code prediction. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile innovation as a supervision signal for contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond the Sink Interpretation: Radial Attention in Flow-Based VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26385)
*Yuxi Zheng,Yueqian Lin,Yudong Liu,Zijia Tang,Junyu Pan,Yiheng Wang,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在π0.5 VLA策略中，动作序列首尾token虽获得超额注意力（5.4%对均匀4%）且由RoPE驱动，但不符语言模型中的sink特征：其值范数正常，掩码或置零值对成功率影响小于4个百分点，替换值方向仅降3.2-7.2个百分点（远小于非端点token的28.4个百分点）；真正符合sink特征的是前缀BOS，置零其值反而提升成功率（+13.2和+6.0个百分点）；基于相对偏移的路由可保持75%动作token离散度并在三个留出任务上维持42.7%成功率（基线38.7%），而均匀或打乱路由降至3-4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将流式VLA策略中首尾动作token的高注意力称为radial action sink，并据此设定执行时长；但语言模型中sink的定义是高入注意力、小值且对输出影响小，本文检验该特征是否成立，而非检验时长规则。

Method: 在一个π0.5检查点和五个RoboCasa任务上，测量首尾token的注意力质量占比、值范数、掩码/置零值/替换值方向对成功率的影响，并分析RoPE的作用；同时检验前缀BOS是否符合sink特征，并测试相对偏移路由在留出任务上的效果。

Result: 首尾token获得5.4%动作键质量（超均匀份额1.4个百分点），RoPE是成因；但其值范数接近均值，掩码或置零值改变成功率小于4个百分点，替换值方向降3.2-7.2个百分点，而非端点token替换降28.4个百分点。前缀BOS符合sink特征，置零其值在两次配对运行中分别提升+13.2（95% CI [7.0,19.4]）和+6.0（[0.2,11.8]）个百分点。相对偏移路由保留75%动作token离散度，三个留出任务成功率42.7%对基线38.7%，均匀或打乱路由降至3-4%。

Conclusion: 高注意力质量本身不能说明token的功能；首尾动作token并非语言模型意义上的sink，真正符合sink特征的是前缀BOS，且基于相对偏移的路由对保持策略性能至关重要。

Abstract: Prior work calls the high attention on the first and last action tokens of a flow-based VLA policy a radial action sink, and uses these tokens to set the execution horizon. In language models, a sink is a token with high incoming attention, a small value, and little effect on the output. We test whether that profile holds, not the horizon rule, in one $\pi_{0.5}$ checkpoint on five RoboCasa tasks. The two endpoints receive 5.4% of action-key mass, 1.4 percentage points above their uniform share, and rotary positional encoding produces this excess: directly in shallow layers and through upstream action-token RoPE deeper in the network. The endpoints do not fit the sink profile. Their value norms stay near the action-token mean in all 18 layers. On 250 paired episodes, masking them or zeroing their values changes success by less than 4 points, and replacing their value directions with those of another observation lowers it by 3.2 to 7.2 points across three donors, whereas the same replacement on two non-endpoint tokens in the executed prefix lowers it by 28.4 points. The token that does fit the sink profile on mass and value norm is the prefix BOS; on this checkpoint, zeroing its value raises success in two paired runs, by +13.2 points (95% CI [+7.0, +19.4]) and +6.0 points ([+0.2, +11.8]). Inside the action chunk, a relative-offset routing profile estimated on other tasks keeps 75% of action-token spread and keeps closed-loop success on three held-out tasks (42.7% against a 38.7% baseline), while uniform or shuffled routing drops success to 3-4%. High attention mass alone does not tell what a token does.

</details>


### [11] [Alignment Drives Reuse: Learning Generalizable Robot-Use Agents from Heterogeneous Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26376)
*Ziyue Wang,Yaopeng Yang,Liyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniCraft通过对齐异构机器人交互，在无需演示或更新模型权重的情况下学习共享操作技能，并实现跨新机器人和智能体模型的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型泛化性强，但机器人操作因实体、任务形式、几何约定和动作接口的异构性难以泛化，交互经验碎片化。

Method: UniCraft用统一坐标约定表示场景几何，对齐不同机器人的传感与控制接口，以规范格式组织多基准任务；交错3个基准、6种实体的任务，每个智能体模型采集2700次学习轨迹，联合优化共享技能与编排策略，不依赖演示或模型权重更新。

Result: 训练中，LIBERO-PRO未见场景和任务成功率提升9-28个百分点，RoboTwin 2.0提升10.8-13.3，RoboCasa365提升3.1-14.4（第1-3轮）；训练后冻结技能，在robosuite和RoboDojo上对4种新机器人和5个额外智能体模型实现零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 跨基准和跨实体的异构交互可通过对齐联合提升单一共享操作技能集，并支持零样本泛化到新实体和智能体模型。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) demonstrate broad generalization across tasks and domains. However, comparable generalization in robot manipulation remains challenging. Heterogeneity in robot embodiments, task formulations, geometric conventions, and action interfaces fragments interaction experience across benchmarks and hardware. We rethink robot skill learning through alignment: Can heterogeneous interactions across benchmarks and embodiments jointly improve one shared skillset for robot manipulation? We propose UniCraft, a framework for learning robot manipulation skills by aligning heterogeneous settings. Specifically, UniCraft represents observed scene geometry using consistent coordinate conventions and aligns sensing and control interfaces across robots. It organizes tasks from different benchmarks in a canonical format, allowing interaction feedback to jointly refine shared skills and the orchestration policy that guides their selection and composition, without any demonstrations or model weight updates. We interleave tasks from 3 benchmarks and 6 embodiments, collecting 2,700 learning rollouts per agent model. During training, UniCraft improves pooled task success on unseen scenarios and tasks by 9-28 percentage points on LIBERO-PRO, 10.8-13.3 on RoboTwin 2.0, and 3.1-14.4 on RoboCasa365 (Epochs 1-3). After training, with the learned skills frozen, UniCraft demonstrates zero-shot transfer across 4 new robot embodiments and 5 additional agent models on robosuite and RoboDojo.

</details>


### [12] [PUPPET: Physical-Unified Platform for Presence, Embodiment, and Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26350)
*Mahika Vats,Milan Groshev,Carlos Gomez Cubero,Eduardo Castelló Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PUPPET 是一个将物理机器人实验室通过持续同步的 VR 副本呈现出来的信息物理框架，并集成了试验验证流程，实验表明 VR 远程操作在任务效率和数据可用性上优于 2D 远程操作，接近现场执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程访问机器人硬件并不能保证获得可用的实验数据；遥操作试验可能完成任务，但仍产生不完整或不一致的记录。

Method: 提出 PUPPET 信息物理框架：通过机器人、搁置环境和任务对象的持续同步 VR 副本开放物理机器人实验室，并在实验工作流中集成试验验证流水线；采用被试内研究，30 名参与者在现场、2D 遥操作和 PUPPET 三种条件下完成同一桌面到货架的对象转移任务。

Result: VR 使学习后完成时间比 2D 减少 27.7%（68.7 s 对 95.0 s），与现场执行（72.1 s）在统计上无显著差异；同时产生更多可验证试验（85.6% 对 66.0%）和无干预试验（89.7% 对 67.2%）。

Conclusion: PUPPET 表明交互模态会影响远程任务表现以及所得实验记录的可复用性。

Abstract: Remote access to robotics hardware does not by itself guarantee usable experimental data. A teleoperated trial can complete the task and still produce an incomplete or inconsistent record. PUPPET aims to solve this problem by presenting a cyber-physical framework that opens a physical robotics laboratory through a continuously synchronized VR replica of the robot, stranding environment, and task objects. In addition, PUPPET integrates a trial validation pipeline built into the experimental workflow. We evaluated PUPPET in a within-subject study with 30 participants, who performed the same table-to-shelf object-transfer task in person, through 2D teleoperation, and through PUPPET. VR reduced post-learning completion time by 27.7% over 2D (68.7,s vs. 95.0,s), statistically indistinguishable from in-person execution (72.1,s). It also produced more validation-ready (85.6% vs. 66.0%) and intervention-free (89.7% vs. 67.2%) trials. With PUPPET we show that modality shapes the remote task performance and the reusability of the resulting experimental record.

</details>


### [13] [Reasoning on Call: Efficient Agentic Robot Control for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26341)
*Zilang Cen,Chao Tang,Fangcheng Liu,Weishi Mi,Wei Hao,JiaYu Xu,Dehao Huang,Yehui Tang,Xiaohui Cui,Tingguang Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoC框架通过在线任务状态监控器协调低层策略与高层推理代理，按需触发代理介入，在长程机器人操作中提升成功率并大幅降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型可支持长程机器人操作，但频繁调用带来高延迟与高token成本；核心难点在于判断何时需要代理协助或介入，以及错误应修正机器人执行、子任务规划还是两者兼有。

Method: 提出RoC（Reasoning on Call）框架，用在线任务状态监控器结合时序视觉观测与有序交互记忆，联合估计当前子任务进度并预测后继子任务；据此触发事件驱动的代理审查，协调执行修复与计划修订；经核实的结果用于更新记忆和子任务转换，使常规执行交由低层策略、推理仅聚焦需额外评估的决策。

Result: 在RMBench上，相比最强代理基线任务成功率提升24.0个百分点、token消耗降低65.3%；相比最强策略基线任务成功率提升15.1个百分点；真实世界三个长程操作任务实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: RoC支持高效的代理介入，并能在长程机器人操作中实现执行与规划的协同恢复。

Abstract: Large multimodal models can support long-horizon robot manipulation, but frequent model calls incur substantial latency and token costs. The central challenge is determining when agent assistance or intervention is needed and whether errors require correcting robot execution, subtask planning, or both. To address this problem, we propose RoC (Reasoning on Call), an efficient framework that coordinates a low-level policy and a high-level reasoning agent through an online task-state monitor. The monitor combines temporal visual observations with ordered interaction memory to jointly estimate progress within the active subtask and predict its successor. These predictions guide event-triggered agent review for coordinated execution repair and plan revision. Verified outcomes govern memory updates and subtask transitions, allowing routine execution to remain with the policy while reasoning focuses on decisions requiring additional assessment. On RMBench, RoC improves task success rate by 24.0 percentage points and reduces token consumption by 65.3% compared with the strongest agent-based baseline. It also outperforms the strongest policy-based baseline by 15.1 percentage points in task success rate. Real-world experiments further validate its effectiveness across three long-horizon manipulation tasks. Overall, RoC supports efficient agent intervention and coordinated recovery across execution and planning for long-horizon robot manipulation.

</details>


### [14] [Exposure-Aware Informative Path Planning Using Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26302)
*Muzaffar Qureshi,Yihui Mao,James W. Cross,Kyle Volle,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究移动传感代理团队在有限预算下的暴露感知信息路径规划（EA-IPP）问题，将GP信息增益与测量暴露指数权衡建模为标量化双目标信念状态MDP，并用共享参数PPO离线训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息路径规划方法在运动或资源约束下优化信息量，但未考虑场暴露约束；本文旨在让代理在映射未知标量场的同时限制对高场强区域的暴露。

Method: 将EA-IPP形式化为标量化双目标信念状态马尔可夫决策过程，权衡归一化GP信息增益与归一化测量驱动的团队暴露指数；采用参数共享PPO框架，去中心化actor与集中式团队critic，离线训练后在未见场上评估。

Result: 在雷达、羽流和野火场族上，EA-IPP代理取得最低GP重建RMSE，同时D-最优信息保持在非学习信息基线的10%以内；最小场暴露分别比非暴露感知基线低约34.6%、20.1%和27%。

Conclusion: 所提EA-IPP方法在保持接近最优信息获取的同时显著降低场暴露，验证了学习式暴露感知路径规划在多类标量场中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the finite-budget exposure-aware informative path planning (IPP) problem for a fleet of mobile sensing agents. The agents map an unknown scalar field while limiting exposure to high field intensity. Existing IPP methods optimize informativeness under motion or resource constraints, but do not address field exposure constraints. We formulate exposure-aware IPP (EA-IPP) as a scalarized bi-objective belief-state Markov decision process that trades the normalized Gaussian-process (GP) information gain against a normalized measurement-derived fleet exposure index. A parameter-shared proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework with decentralized actors and a centralized fleet critic is trained offline and evaluated on unseen fields. Across the radar, plume, and wildfire field families, the EA-IPP agent achieves the lowest GP reconstruction RMSE among the evaluated baselines while retaining D-optimal information within $10\%$ of the non-learning information baseline. The minimum field exposure is approximately $34.6\%$, $20.1\%$, and $27\%$ below the non-exposure-aware baseline for radar, plume, and wildfire, respectively.

</details>


### [15] [LocoMuJoCo: A Comprehensive Imitation Learning Benchmark for Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26293)
*Firas Al-Hafez,Davide Tateo,Oleg Kaidanov,Aditya Bhatt,Artur Zelik,Zhengyi Luo,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LocoMuJoCo 是面向全身控制的开源模仿学习与强化学习基准，统一 MuJoCo（CPU）与基于 GPU 的 MJX、MjWarp 后端，提供多种人形、肌骨、四足机器人及动作捕捉数据，支持模块化插件和 JIT 训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制研究缺乏统一、可复现的基准，仿真后端（CPU/GPU）、机器人形态、数据与算法实现割裂，难以公平比较和端到端训练。

Method: 在单一 API 下耦合 MuJoCo 与 MJX/MjWarp；提供 9 种人形、3 种（肌）骨骼、4 种四足实体及每种人形配套的动捕数据集，并支持 AMASS、LAFAN1 重定向；采用模块化插件架构使观测、奖励、控制模式、地形与域随机化可互换；以紧凑 JAX 实现多种 RL/IL 算法实现 JIT 编译端到端训练；配套 loco-mujoco-real 仓库支持 sim-to-real 部署；并介绍 SMPL-to-robot 与 robot-to-robot 重定向流程。

Result: 论文描述了库的设计、重定向流水线与使用示例；LocoMuJoCo 以 MIT 许可开源发布。

Conclusion: LocoMuJoCo 为全身控制中的 IL/RL 研究提供统一、模块化、可端到端训练并支持真机部署的开源基准平台。

Abstract: LocoMuJoCo is an open-source benchmark for imitation learning (IL) and reinforcement learning (RL) on whole-body control. The library couples MuJoCo (CPU) with MJX and the MjWarp backend (GPU) under a single API, exposes 9 humanoid, 3 (musculo-)skeletal, and 4 quadruped embodiments, and ships a curated native motion-capture dataset for every humanoid, to which it can also retarget AMASS and LAFAN1. A modular plug-in architecture makes observations, rewards, control modes, terrains, and domain randomization fully interchangeable, while compact JAX implementations of many RL and IL algorithms provide JIT-compiled end-to-end training, and the companion loco-mujoco-real repository provides sim-to-real deployment. We describe the design, the SMPL-to-robot and robot-to-robot retargeting pipeline, and a usage example. LocoMuJoCo is released under MIT at this https URL .

</details>


### [16] [I See You: Initial Multi-Robot LiDAR Registration with Mutual-Observation Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26276)
*Jonathan Lichtenfeld,Oskar von Stryk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集中式、可部署的多机器人预任务注册框架，通过成对粗到精对齐和确定性合理性检查主动拒绝不可能的位姿假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人搜救需在无基础设施条件下完成初始对齐，现有单次扫描LiDAR全局配准在混叠或退化环境中易静默失败，且超过两个智能体的方法缺乏真实场景评估。

Method: 集中式框架：对机器人两两进行粗到精点云配准，并用确定性合理性检查拒绝不可能的位姿假设；在四机器人同步真实数据集（含真值）上验证。

Result: 检查拒绝了超过三分之一错误成对配准，误拒正确配准低于1%，并将团队注册中机器人位姿错误的实例减少38%。

Conclusion: 该框架无需外部基础设施即可实现预任务多机器人注册，显著降低错误位姿传播，数据集与代码公开。

Abstract: Compared to single-robot deployments, multi-robot teams can significantly improve search-and-rescue (SAR) mission success by covering large areas in parallel. To collaborate effectively, these robots must be registered in a common reference frame. Standard registration methods typically rely on external infrastructure or significant map overlap, but real deployments require infrastructure-free, stationary initial alignment for immediate operation. While global LiDAR point cloud registration addresses this, existing single-scan methods often fail silently in aliased or degenerate environments. Approaches scaling beyond two agents also remain rarely evaluated in real-world scenarios. We propose a centralized, deployment-ready framework for pre-mission multi-robot registration. Our system performs pairwise coarse-to-fine alignment and actively rejects impossible pose hypotheses via deterministic plausibility checks. We validate the framework on a real-world custom dataset of four synchronized robots in various environments with ground truth. The checks reject more than a third of the incorrect pairwise registrations while falsely rejecting less than 1% of the correct ones, and reduce the number of team registrations with a wrong robot pose by 38%. Dataset and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [17] [FastRoverVLA: Fast Vision-Language-Action Execution for Off-Road Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26242)
*Changtao Zhao,Yingxue Guan,Li Wang,Shijie Zhao,Yuhang Zhang,Shengkai Zhang,Qing Wang,Ziying Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastRoverVLA通过将视觉规划生成与运动条件执行解耦，在不重新训练的情况下复用解码后的规划，并用速度-曲率动作缓冲在多次更新间保持运动校正，从而以极低的请求率显著降低轨迹误差和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型会频繁更新轨迹，导致昂贵多模态推理被反复调用；直接复用预测虽能降低需求，但会使指令暴露于车辆运动变化之下，产生规划与执行不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出免训练的FastRoverVLA接口：在两次视觉预测之间保持已解码规划；将速度-曲率动作缓冲与每次更新时的实测运动对齐，积分成局部轨迹，并通过消耗当前动作推进，再把校正后的剩余部分写回缓冲以跨更新保留运动校正；采用有界刷新规则归一化新解码规划中的速度与曲率变化，并依据规划粗糙度分数分配短或长复用预算；该接口无需骨干特征，也无需额外学习驾驶策略。

Result: 在3秒时域上，独立真实请求评估在ORAD-3D上取得ADE/FDE为0.328/0.596 m，在RELLIS-3D上为0.266/0.526 m；在请求率6.0%和4.8%下，FDE分别降低18.0%和36.7%；在四种骨干和两个数据集上均保持更低误差，串行重放时Qwen3-VL请求处理成本分别降低16.6倍和20.8倍；机制与纯运动控制实验表明持续状态校正是精度收益的主要来源。

Conclusion: FastRoverVLA证明，解耦视觉规划生成与运动条件执行、并配合持续状态校正的缓冲复用机制，能够在不增加学习策略的前提下大幅减少多模态请求并提升轨迹精度，为越野VLA模型的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Off-road vision-language-action (VLA) models generate scene-conditioned plans, but frequent trajectory updates can repeatedly invoke expensive multimodal inference. Reusing a prediction reduces this demand while leaving its commands exposed to changes in vehicle motion. FastRoverVLA addresses this mismatch by decoupling visual plan generation from motion-conditioned execution. Its training-free interface maintains decoded plans between visual predictions. A speed-curvature action buffer is aligned with measured motion at each update, integrated into a local trajectory, and advanced by consuming the current action. The corrected remainder is written back to the buffer, preserving motion corrections across successive updates. A bounded refresh rule normalizes speed and curvature variations within a newly decoded plan and assigns a short or long reuse budget according to the resulting plan-roughness score. The interface requires neither backbone features nor an additional learned driving policy. Over a 3 s horizon, independent real-request evaluation achieves average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) of 0.328/0.596 m on ORAD-3D and 0.266/0.526 m on RELLIS-3D, reducing FDE by 18.0% and 36.7% at request rates of 6.0% and 4.8%, respectively. These runs retain lower errors across four backbones and both datasets and reduce Qwen3-VL request-processing cost by factors of 16.6 and 20.8 under serial replay, while mechanism and motion-only controls identify persistent state correction as the main source of the observed accuracy benefit. The source code and evaluation materials are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models to Stop Safely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26239)
*Zheyuan Liu,Chuhan Wang,Jinduo Guo,Yue Huang,Xiangchi Yuan,Ningshan Ma,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAFI方法，通过在禁止任务演示中构建回退目标并联合适配策略，使机器人在任务执行中途被接管时能安全停止并保持抓取，在LIBERO上接管后目标成功率降至1.3%且无物理事故。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的任务抑制与拒绝方法仅在初始状态有效，执行中途接管时策略仍可能完成任务；即使未完成，机器人也可能掉落物体，使'未完成'成为不充分的安全指标。

Method: SAFI分两阶段：(1)回退目标构建——在禁止任务演示全程赋予静止手臂指令与一致夹爪目标；(2)联合策略适配——将回退目标与保留任务的原始动作一起学习，从而在中间执行状态学到显式替代响应并保持其他技能。

Result: 在LIBERO与BadLIBERO任务上使用OpenVLA-OFT进行实验，评估禁止任务完成率、物理事故与保留任务性能；接管后聚合目标成功率降至1.3%，无物理事故与动作饱和，同时保留任务成功率保持较高水平。

Conclusion: SAFI能在执行中途接管时提供安全替代响应，有效降低禁止任务完成率并避免物理事故，同时不损害原有任务能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to perform diverse tasks from visual observations and language instructions. While prior work has explored task suppression and refusal, stopping prohibited behavior once execution is underway and controlling the resulting physical response remain challenging. Our evaluation reveals that policies which suppress a task from its initial state can still complete it when taking over partway through execution. Even when the task remains unfinished, the robot may drop a held object, making non-completion an incomplete measure of safety. To address these challenges, we introduce State-Aware Fallback Imitation (SAFI) for tasks where holding provides an appropriate alternative response. SAFI comprises two stages, (1) fallback target construction, which assigns stationary arm commands and consistent gripper targets throughout prohibited-task demonstrations, and (2) joint policy adaptation, which learns these targets alongside the original actions of retained tasks. This supervision teaches an explicit alternative response at intermediate execution states while maintaining the robot's other skills. Extensive experiments with OpenVLA-OFT across LIBERO and BadLIBERO tasks evaluate prohibited completion, physical incidents, and retained-task performance. On the evaluated LIBERO tasks, SAFI reduces aggregate target success after takeover to 1.3\%, with no recorded physical incidents or action saturation, while preserving high retained-task success.

</details>


### [19] [EmbodyAgent: Post-Training Embodied VLM Agents via Distillation, SFT, and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26196)
*Wei Dai,Jinkun Liu,Jiayi Chen,Han Liu,Wenbo Ding,Shaolun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodyAgent 提出一个端到端后训练框架，通过黑盒数据合成、SFT 蒸馏和在线 RL 三阶段，将开源 9B 视觉语言模型训练为高性能具身智能体，在 LIBERO Spatial 任务上将留出测试成功率从 0/100 提升至 84/100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源 VLM 直接用作机器人智能体时无法满足交互式工具契约，前沿教师模型仅以黑盒形式可用且权重与 token 概率不可得，单纯监督模仿在 rollout 分布偏移下性能饱和，因此需要完整的后训练栈。

Method: 框架包含三条紧耦合流水线：黑盒数据合成（在强制先思考后写代码的契约下查询教师模型并按任务成功过滤轨迹）、SFT 蒸馏（将解释与程序目标蒸馏进视觉与语言编码器均可训练的 9B VLM）、在线 RL（用组相对优势与运行时错误惩罚的结果奖励优化学生生成的 episode）；三阶段共享同一个无原语 Python 执行工具，仅暴露原始视觉观测与七维末端执行器控制，无动作解码器、无接口切换。

Result: 在十个 LIBERO Spatial 任务的严格分离评估协议下（状态划分不相交、贪心测试解码、仅训练期防护），100 个测试状态的留出成功率从基座 9B VLM 的 0/100 提升至 SFT 后的 14/100 和 RL 后的 84/100，训练 rollout 成功率从 8.4% 升至 87.7%，每 episode 中位模型调用次数从 36 降至 11。

Conclusion: 完整后训练栈可显著将开源 VLM 转化为高性能具身智能体；同时识别出基于结果奖励的可执行代码 RL 会诱发自增强的运行时错误模式，并建立失败类别监控与采样侧数据整编协议以稳定训练。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can act as closed-loop robot-use agents (RUAs) by writing and executing control code through tool interfaces. However, turning an open-weight VLM into a high-performing embodied agent requires a complete post-training stack: out-of-the-box open VLMs fail completely at interactive tool contracts, strong frontier teachers are accessible only as black-box endpoints without exposed weights or token probabilities, and supervised imitation alone plateaus under rollout distribution shift. We present EmbodyAgent, an end-to-end post-training framework with three tightly coupled pipelines: (1) a black-box data synthesis pipeline that queries a strong teacher under a forced think-then-code contract and filters trajectories by task success; (2) an SFT pipeline that distills the explanation-and-program targets into an open-weight 9B VLM with both vision and language encoders trainable; and (3) an online RL pipeline that optimizes student-generated episodes via group-relative advantage with a runtime-error-penalized outcome reward. All three pipelines share one primitive-free Python execution tool exposing only raw visual observation and seven-dimensional end-effector control, with no action decoder and no interface shift across stages. Under a strictly separated evaluation protocol on ten LIBERO Spatial tasks (disjoint state splits, greedy test-time decoding, training-only safeguards), held-out success on 100 test states rises from 0/100 (base 9B VLM) to 14/100 (after SFT) and 84/100 (after RL), while training rollout success climbs from 8.4% to 87.7% and median model calls per episode drop from 36 to 11. We further identify a self-reinforcing runtime-error mode induced by outcome-based RL on executable code, and establish a failure-category monitoring and sampling-side curation protocol that stabilizes training.

</details>


### [20] [Planning to Stop: Factored Certification and Terminal Repair for Noncircular Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26167)
*Runtian Zheng,Congpeng Zhang,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoDiff是一种运动规划器，通过原始目标停止集包含、见证引导终端修复和因子化证明编译，在有限预算内提高紧空间导航的认证覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧空间导航要求机器人清除障碍并在目标内停止，同时需应对不确定性；现有方法在有限时间内认证覆盖不足。

Method: 提出GeoDiff，耦合原始目标停止集包含、见证引导终端修复和因子化证明编译；利用精确可行平移特征指导修复，通过请求局部复用减少重复几何计算；建立编译器等价与到达停止健全性的条件。

Result: 在11,440个平面图衍生任务中，GeoDiff在15秒内以5厘米容差认证61/139个任务，基线为44/139；最强外部证书适配器从119/139条原生路径认证13/139个任务；在BARN地图任务上，Jackal形状机体认证202/270，门户基线为3/270。

Conclusion: 终端推理与证明复用可在保持原始物理任务和目标的同时，提高有限预算下的认证覆盖率。

Abstract: Tight-space navigation requires a robot to clear obstacles and stop inside its goal despite uncertainty. We present GeoDiff, a motion-program planner coupling original-goal stopped-set inclusion, witness-guided terminal repair, and factored proof compilation. An exact feasible-translation characterization guides repair; request-local reuse reduces repeated geometric work. We establish conditions for compiler equivalence and reach-and-stop soundness. Across 11,440 floorplan-derived jobs, GeoDiff certifies 61/139 tasks at 5-cm tolerance within 15 s, versus 44/139 for the stop-set baseline. The strongest observed external certificate adapter certifies 13/139 tasks from 119/139 native routes, separating route availability from certification. On tasks derived from public BARN maps, GeoDiff certifies 202/270 at 5-cm tolerance for a Jackal-shaped body, versus 3/270 for the portal baseline. Development analyses distinguish emitter savings from solver coverage, and 1,420 route-tracking episodes characterize the execution interface. These results show how terminal reasoning and proof reuse increase finite-budget certification coverage while preserving the original physical task and goal.

</details>


### [21] [One View, One Action: Spatially Generalizable VLAs through Observation-Aligned Experts and Cross Graph Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26161)
*Jinxi Liu,Ruifeng Zhai,Hao Liu,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVOA-VLA通过在各视角相机坐标系下预测动作，使同一物理运动提供多样的观测对齐监督，并结合基座与视角专家间的双向有向注意力图及跨图一致性，提升多视角VLA模型的空间泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角视觉-语言-动作模型存在空间泛化挑战，尤其在相机视角和机器人初始状态变化时。现有模型仅在机器人基座坐标系下接收动作监督，需隐式学习各视角观测与基座动作的空间对应关系，导致泛化不足。

Method: 提出OVOA-VLA，在每个视角相机坐标系下预测动作；联合训练基座坐标系专家和视角特定动作专家，其机器人状态输入和动作目标均在各自相机坐标系下表达；构建两个互补的有向注意力图建模基座与视角专家间的双向交互；引入跨图一致性鼓励两个图对同一样本的基座预测一致；支持完整多视角和仅基座两种推理。

Result: 在多个仿真基准上提升操作性能及对相机视角和机器人初始化扰动的鲁棒性；LIBERO-Plus上完整推理和仅基座推理平均成功率分别为88.1%和85.7%；真实世界三个任务在空间分布偏移下，仅基座推理将平均成功率从π0.5的28.3%提升至58.3%。

Conclusion: 在视角相机坐标系下进行动作监督并联合训练基座与视角专家，能有效利用视角特定监督改善基座预测，显著增强多视角VLA模型的空间泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-view vision-language-action (VLA) models still face challenges in spatial generalization, particularly under changes in camera viewpoints and robot initial states. These models typically receive action supervision only in the robot base frame, requiring them to implicitly learn the spatial correspondence between observations from each view and base-frame actions. We propose One View, One Action (OVOA-VLA), which learns to predict actions in each view's camera frame, allowing the same physical motion to provide diverse, observation-aligned supervision across views. OVOA-VLA jointly trains a base-frame expert and view-specific action experts, whose robot-state inputs and action targets are expressed in their respective camera frames. Two complementary directed attention graphs model both directions of interaction between the base and view-specific experts, jointly leveraging view-specific action supervision to improve base-frame prediction. Cross Graph Consistency further encourages agreement between the two graphs' base-frame predictions for the same sample. With the same multi-view inputs, the model supports both full and base-only inference. Experiments across multiple simulation benchmarks demonstrate improved manipulation performance and robustness to camera-viewpoint and robot-initialization perturbations. Full and base-only inference achieve average success rates of 88.1% and 85.7% on LIBERO-Plus, respectively. In real-world evaluations, base-only inference raises the average success rates from $\pi_{0.5}$'s 28.3% to 58.3% across three tasks under spatial distribution shifts.

</details>


### [22] [A Practical Proprioceptive Data Collection Utility for the Unitree GO1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26146)
*Jack Anders Smitterberg,Tan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个开源图形化工具，用于在无需ROS专业知识的情况下从Unitree Go1机器人可靠地采集本体感知数据，并对其采集质量进行了表征，同时用简单的分类实验证明数据可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unitree Go1是腿足机器人研究常用平台，但其SDK文档稀疏且原生中间件ROS1已完全弃用，导致本体感知数据采集困难，论文旨在降低采集门槛。

Method: 开发了一个开源GUI工具，支持实时开始、停止和标注记录，可采集关节状态、IMU读数、足力传感器数据和陀螺仪测量；对51条记录进行了采集质量表征（采样率和重复样本），并训练时序卷积网络进行地形分类作为数据可用性的初步检验。

Result: 地形分类结果较弱：时序卷积网络在十类地形上的留出记录准确率约38%（随机水平10%），而忽略记录边界的窗口级划分将同一指标虚高至约99%；同时报告了采集到的采样率和重复样本情况。

Conclusion: 该工具能降低采集Go1本体感知数据的门槛，且记录信号中确实包含地形信息，但分类器并非竞争性方案，窗口级划分会严重高估性能。

Abstract: The Unitree Go1 is a widely used platform for legged robotics research, yet collecting proprioceptive data from it is unnecessarily difficult: the Unitree SDK is sparsely documented, and ROS1 (Robot Operating System), the Go1's native middleware, is fully deprecated. We present an open-source graphical utility that enables researchers to reliably record proprioceptive data from the Go1, including joint states, IMU (Inertial Measurement Unit) readings, foot force sensor data, and gyroscope measurements, without requiring ROS expertise. The tool provides a simple GUI (Graphical User Interface) for starting, stopping, and labeling recordings in real time. The contribution of this paper is the collection utility. We characterize the acquisition quality of 51 recordings collected with it, including the achieved sampling rate and repeated samples. As a secondary, deliberately modest check that the data is useful, we train a terrain classifier on it. The results are weak: a Temporal Convolutional Network reaches about 38% accuracy on held-out recordings across ten terrain classes (10% would be chance), and a window-level split that ignores recording boundaries inflates the same measurement to about 99%. We report this as a demonstration that the logged signals carry terrain information, not as a competitive classifier. The utility is designed to lower the barrier to collecting Go1 proprioceptive data without ROS.

</details>


### [23] [Spatially Conditioned Trajectory Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26127)
*Yefim Nudelman,Dor Atzmon,Tom Tirer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCTD提出一种地图条件轨迹扩散生成器，通过保留位置感知占据令牌和交叉注意力，无需采样时障碍引导即可生成避障轨迹，并与CBS结合实现多机器人协调规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散规划器通常依赖采样时引导避障，或将地图几何聚合为全局表示；需探索一种在采样过程中直接利用空间信息、且无需额外障碍代价引导的轨迹生成方法。

Method: SCTD在时间U-Net中通过交叉注意力访问多个位置感知占据令牌，并用硬端点修复强制起止状态；单机器人演示训练一个模型，生成避障轨迹；高层CBS通过约束重规划协调轨迹，采样时保留智能体间引导，并跨布局和团队规模复用模型。

Result: 在15,000个留出任务上，SCTD成功率高于评估的引导扩散基线，硬绕行任务提升最大，轨迹更平滑；空间令牌条件优于全局地图嵌入，地图替换测试表明依赖所给几何；多机器人系统团队成功率高于扩散基线，实验覆盖最多15个机器人。

Conclusion: SCTD通过空间条件扩散与CBS协调，实现了无需采样时障碍代价引导的避障轨迹生成，并在单机器人与多机器人规划中优于对比基线。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Motion Planning (MRMP) requires trajectories that avoid environmental obstacles and collisions between robots in a shared workspace. Diffusion-based planners often address obstacle avoidance through sampling-time guidance, while some conditioned approaches aggregate map geometry into a global representation. We present Spatially Conditioned Trajectory Diffusion (SCTD), a map-conditioned trajectory generator for multi-robot planning. SCTD retains multiple position-aware occupancy tokens accessed through cross-attention throughout a temporal U-Net, with hard endpoint inpainting enforcing start and goal states. One model trained on single-robot demonstrations across procedural environments generates obstacle-aware trajectories without sampling-time obstacle-cost guidance. High-level Conflict-Based Search (CBS) coordinates these trajectories through constrained replanning, retaining inter-agent guidance during sampling and reusing the model across workspace layouts and team sizes. Experiments assess single-robot success and trajectory quality, generalization to unfamiliar layouts with added obstacles, and coordinated multi-robot planning. On 15,000 held-out tasks, SCTD achieves higher success than the evaluated guided diffusion baseline, with the largest gains on hard-detour tasks, while trajectory-quality comparisons show smoother trajectories. Conditioning ablations show higher success with spatial tokens than with a global map embedding, and map-replacement tests demonstrate dependence on the supplied geometry. The resulting multi-robot system achieves higher team success than the compared diffusion baselines, with experiments covering teams of up to 15 robots.

</details>


### [24] [PhysVAM: Physics-oriented Future Value Action Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26105)
*Yuxiang Yang,Jingbo Zhang,Jiaru Zhong,Junliang Chen,Yirui Zhou,Guangming Wang,Haitao Lin,Huaiyuan Xu,Yi Wang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVAM 提出一种面向物理的未来价值动作模型，通过预测未来任务价值而非重建未来视觉状态，来指导动态机器人操作中的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态操作中物体在感知和执行期间持续运动，仅预测场景或物体状态无法揭示交互将如何推进或阻碍任务；因此需要一种面向任务的前瞻性，而非未来视觉状态重建。

Method: PhysVAM 以当前观测和历史物体/机器人运动为条件，联合学习动作生成与多时域未来价值预测；价值分支从成功和失败轨迹中预测未来任务价值以学习各时域特定的潜在表示，并通过门控交叉注意力将这些表示融入动作策略。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验显示任务成功率更优，在仿真和真实任务中分别比最强基线绝对提升 27.0 和 27.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以未来任务价值为形式的交互前瞻可有效引导动作生成，避免重建未来视觉状态，并在动态机器人操作中显著提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Predicting future scene or object states does not necessarily reveal how robot-object interactions will advance or hinder a task. This challenge is particularly pronounced in dynamic robotic manipulation, where objects continue moving during perception and execution. Effective action generation therefore requires not only modeling object dynamics but also anticipating how future interactions will shape task progress. To this end, we propose \textbf{PhysVAM}, a Physics-oriented Future Value Action Model that represents anticipated interactions through their effects on future task progress. This value-based formulation provides task-directed foresight without reconstructing future visual states. Conditioned on current observations and historical object and robot motion, PhysVAM jointly learns action generation and multi-horizon future-value prediction. The value branch learns horizon-specific latent representations through future task-value prediction from successful and failed trajectories. These future-value representations are integrated into the action policy through gated cross-attention, guiding action generation toward interactions that facilitate task success. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate superior task success, with absolute success-rate improvements of 27.0 and 27.5 percentage points over the strongest baselines in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [UniVEP: Unifying Velocity and Endpoint Prediction for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26103)
*Zhengtu Liang,Qiwei Liang,Tao Lin,Shihang Weng,Boyang Cai,Xiangyu Chen,Yikeng Chen,Jiaming Liang,Bohan Tan,Yu Yan,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了机器人策略中速度预测、端点预测及速度加权端点预测三种目标，发现互补优势后提出UniVEP，联合训练并在噪声下降时从端点恢复切换到速度精修，实现单步高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配机器人策略在整个动作生成过程使用单一预测目标，但去噪恢复动作与精修近干净动作面临不同挑战，单一目标难以兼顾。

Method: 在StarVLA和LiLa-WAM等策略、多基准与推理预算下系统比较V、X、X--V三种预测目标，分析不同噪声水平下的协方差谱与预测稳定性，并提出UniVEP：联合学习两种目标并加一致性监督，随噪声降低从端点恢复切换到速度精修，每步仅需一次网络运行。

Result: 在RoboTwin上UniVEP单次StarVLA运行达61.36%成功率，超过最佳单目标基线十六次运行的59.96%；LiLa-WAM两次运行即优于最佳十六步单目标基线，推理延迟降低77.1%；仿真与真实操作均显示更高成功率与效率。

Conclusion: 速度与端点预测在不同噪声水平下具有互补优势，联合训练与噪声自适应切换可显著提升少步生成的成功率和推理效率。

Abstract: Flow-matching robot policies usually use one prediction target throughout action generation, even though recovering actions from noise and polishing nearly clean actions bring different challenges. We systematically compare velocity prediction (V), endpoint prediction (X), and velocity-weighted endpoint prediction (X--V) across multiple robot policies, benchmarks, and inference budgets. On StarVLA and LiLa-WAM, X--V works best for few-step generation, while V gains more from iterative refinement. We further uncover their complementary strengths across noise levels: clean actions have concentrated covariance spectra, endpoint predictions stay more stable under high noise, and velocity predictions offer more accurate corrections near clean actions. Inspired by these findings, we introduce UniVEP, which learns both prediction targets together with consistency supervision and switches from endpoint recovery to velocity refinement as noise drops. Each step only needs one network run, and joint training also boosts single-step endpoint generation. Tests on simulated and real-world manipulation show better success rates and efficiency. On RoboTwin, UniVEP hits 61.36% success with one StarVLA run, beating the best single-target baseline at sixteen runs (59.96%). With LiLa-WAM, two runs outperform the best sixteen-step single-target baseline and cut inference latency by 77.1%.

</details>


### [26] [Dynamic Human-Robot Role Allocation through Entropy-Based Monitoring and LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26090)
*Elena Merlo,Marta Lagomarsino,Hamidreza Raei,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以人类自由参与为前提、由成本驱动的行为树动态分配机器人活动的框架，结合大语言模型解析偏好与香农熵监测人类行为，可识别异常并自主纠正，实验显示能实现直观流畅协作且不增加感知工作负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机协作角色分配策略通常同时给人机分配任务，限制用户主动性，且分配决策可能与用户意图冲突、依据不透明，降低接受度与协作流畅性。

Method: 框架让人类自由参与任务，机器人依据用户表达的偏好与行为，通过成本驱动的行为树架构动态分配活动；用大语言模型从自然语言指令中提取显式偏好，并用基于香农熵的手-物交互分析在线监测人类活动；同时能识别人工执行异常并自主触发机器人纠正动作。

Result: 通过试点研究和14名被试的多被试实验验证，结果表明该分配策略能够实现直观且投入的协作，同时不增加感知工作负荷。

Conclusion: 成本驱动行为树结合偏好解析与熵监测的动态分配策略，可在不增加用户负担的情况下提升人机协作的直观性与流畅性。

Abstract: Role allocation strategies in human-robot collaboration typically assign tasks to both the human and the robot, limiting the user's ability to take initiative. Furthermore, allocation decisions may conflict with the user's intentions, and the rationale behind such allocations may remain unclear, potentially reducing both acceptance and collaboration fluency. The novelty of the proposed framework is that the human is left free to contribute to the task, while the robot is dynamically allocated activities through a cost-driven Behavior Tree architecture according to user-expressed preferences and behavior. Explicit preferences are extracted from natural language instructions using a large language model, whereas human activity is monitored online through a Shannon entropy-based analysis of hand-object interactions. Furthermore, the framework can recognize anomalies in human execution and autonomously trigger corrective robot actions. The approach was validated through a pilot study and a multi-subject experiment involving 14 participants. Results demonstrate the potential of such an allocation strategy for enabling intuitive and engaging collaboration without increasing the perceived workload.

</details>


### [27] [Reference-Twist-Based Velocity-Domain Spatiotemporal Calibration for mmWave Radar, Event Camera, and IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26083)
*Zhenghao Zou,Yang Lyu,Xiaohu Guo,Chunhui Zhao,Quan Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向毫米波雷达、事件相机与IMU的刚体运动一致性，提出以可信参考体扭转为条件的速度域时空标定方法，估计相对旋转、杆臂、时间偏移及传感器偏置与尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器导航需要统一时空基准，但毫米波雷达、事件相机和IMU之间难以通过目标、特征、点云或位姿关联建立几何标定；这些传感器共享刚体运动，可利用速度域信息进行标定。

Method: 在给定可信参考体扭转条件下，将雷达多普勒、事件法向流和惯性测量回归到同一运动，实现联合与参考条件化估计；推导条件可辨识性及CRLB级协方差与偏置界，并给出条件信息有效的操作条件。

Result: 通过室内和森林中轴分离运动的录制数据，评估初始化、时间对齐、运动退化、参考输入敏感性和残差一致性；估计结果依赖于参考精度、同步、运动激励和投影多样性，参考不增加物理可观测性。

Conclusion: 参考条件化速度域标定可在无需人工目标、运动恢复结构或跨模态点云配准的情况下标定多传感器时空参数，但所得估计为条件估计，其有效性受参考质量与运动条件约束。

Abstract: Multi-sensor navigation requires a common spatial and temporal basis, but geometric calibration relies on targets, features, point clouds, or pose associations that are difficult to establish across mmWave radars, event cameras, and inertial measurement units (IMUs). These sensors nevertheless share rigid-body motion. This paper formulates reference-conditioned velocity-domain spatiotemporal calibration for a rigid suite of two mmWave radars, one event camera, and two IMUs. Given a trusted reference body twist, radar Doppler, event normal flow, and inertial measurements are regressed against the same motion to estimate relative rotations, lever arms, time offsets, and sensor-specific bias and scale without artificial targets, structure from motion, or cross-modal point-cloud registration. The reference is treated as a conditioning input rather than a sensor to be calibrated. We compare joint and reference- conditioned estimation, derive conditional identifiability and CRLB-level covariance and bias bounds, and state the operating conditions under which conditioning is informative. Indoor and forest recordings with axis-separated motion evaluate initial- ization, temporal alignment, motion degeneracy, reference-input sensitivity, and residual consistency. Because the reference does not add physical observability, the estimates remain conditional on reference accuracy, synchronization, motion excitation, and projection diversity.

</details>


### [28] [AGE-Capture: Autonomous, General, and Efficient UAV Capture for High-Fidelity Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26048)
*Xiaofeng Jin,Ningbo Bu,Matteo Frosi,Yiran Guo,Weilun Ren,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AGE-Capture 是一个面向真实无人机的自主、传感器自适应且高效的主动采集框架，通过渲染驱动的轻量在线状态评估候选视角效用与任务进度，实现高保真场景重建的自主终止与飞行引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机高保真场景重建不仅需要识别有价值视角，还需在不同传感配置和有限飞行资源下自主获取信息观测并判断任务完成，现有方法难以兼顾自主性、传感器适应性与效率。

Method: 基于可渲染性构建轻量在线状态评估候选视角效用、任务进度与飞行执行；将几何探索与重建观测解耦并建立统一相机射线接口，使不同测距传感器与RGB相机共享重建驱动采集管线；采用重建前沿视角刻画残余采集价值以进行区域筛选和自主终止，结合动态感知分层规划与异步事件驱动捕获减少区域间运动、等待规划时间和重复观测。

Result: 在十个室内外仿真场景中，AGE-Capture 平均PSNR比在线基线高3.30–3.53 dB，平均仅使用通用任务时间预算的58.2%，平均峰值GPU显存占用比基线低91.0%–93.3%；不同传感器配置实验验证了共享采集管线的适应性，九个真实场景飞行进一步展示了实际计算与运动约束下的机载闭环运行。

Conclusion: AGE-Capture 能够以更高重建质量、更低计算与时间开销在真实无人机上实现自主、传感器自适应的高效主动采集与闭环重建。

Abstract: High-fidelity scene reconstruction with an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) requires more than identifying valuable viewpoints: the system must autonomously acquire informative observations and determine task completion under different sensing configurations and limited flight resources. We present AGE-Capture, an autonomous, sensor-adaptive, and efficient active-capture framework for real UAVs. Building on renderability, the framework uses a lightweight online state to evaluate the utility of candidate views, assess task progress, and guide flight execution. By decoupling geometric exploration from reconstruction observations and establishing a unified camera-ray interface, the framework allows different range sensors and RGB camera models to share a reconstruction-driven acquisition pipeline. Reconstruction-frontier viewpoints characterize residual acquisition value for region screening and autonomous termination; dynamics-aware hierarchical planning combined with asynchronous event-driven capture reduces unnecessary inter-region motion, time spent waiting for plans, and repeated observations. Across ten indoor and outdoor simulation scenes, AGE-Capture achieves a mean peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) $3.30$--$3.53\,\mathrm{dB}$ higher than that of the online baselines while using $58.2\%$ of the common mission-time budget on average. Its mean peak GPU memory usage is $91.0\%$--$93.3\%$ lower than that of the same baselines. Experiments with different sensor configurations validate the adaptability of the shared acquisition pipeline, while flights in nine real-world scenes further demonstrate onboard closed-loop operation under practical computational and motion constraints.

</details>


### [29] [Agentic Scaling of Embodied Tasks in Simulation for Synthetic Data Collection and Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26046)
*Kang Wang,Xiaolei Zhou,Xiaoyu Wu,Zhendong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticTask是一个基于智能体的框架，可在人类意图下扩展现有任务集合，在RoboCasa上通过关联交互需求与绑定、场景和机器人配置等，半自动生成并执行任务实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展具身交互数据需要大量可用任务，但逐个指定和验证任务实例成本高昂，因此需要一种能低成本扩展现有任务集合的方法。

Method: AgenticTask用统一表示将交互需求与物体/部件绑定、场景与机器人配置、初始条件和完成标准关联；任务生成智能体复用交互结构构造受支持的变体，并利用验证反馈修正不兼容配置；任务执行智能体用相同绑定和几何记录选择机器人支持的技能，并按原始完成标准检查任务完成情况。

Result: 在RoboCasa上，从20,323次构建尝试中保存16,450个任务实例，吞吐量为每小时166.97个保存实例，62.26%的构建尝试通过配置的物理检查；执行智能体完成约38%的评估任务，成功案例涵盖物体放置、铰接操作和顺序排列。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架能实现可度量的任务集合扩展，并提供具体执行证据，为后续具身AI交互数据采集奠定基础；诊断分析指出构建可靠性、动作选择、任务-机器人兼容性和运行时集成是改进重点。

Abstract: Scaling embodied interaction data requires a broad collection of usable tasks, yet specifying and validating each task instance remains costly. We present AgenticTask, an agent-based framework that expands existing task collections under human intent. Its central representation links interaction requirements to object and part bindings, scene and robot configurations, initial conditions, and completion criteria. A task-generation agent reuses interaction structure to construct supported variations and revises incompatible configurations using validation feedback. A task-execution agent uses the same bindings and geometric records to select robot-supported skills and check completion against the original task criteria. On RoboCasa, the framework builds the AgenticTask collection, saving 16,450 task instances from 20,323 construction attempts at a throughput of 166.97 saved instances per elapsed hour, with 62.26% of construction attempts passing the configured physical checks. The execution agent completes approximately 38% of evaluated tasks, with successful cases spanning object placement, articulated manipulation, and sequential arrangement. Diagnostic analysis identifies improvement priorities in construction reliability, action selection, task-robot compatibility, and runtime integration. Together, these results demonstrate task-collection expansion with measured construction productivity and concrete execution evidence, providing a foundation for subsequent interaction-data collection for embodied AI.

</details>


### [30] [Close&Safe: Maximal Task Preservation for Redundant Manipulators via C-Space Boundary Detour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26031)
*Abhigyan Singh,Manthan Goyal,Fares Abu-Dakka,Ravi Prakash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Close&Safe规划器，将标称任务轨迹的跟踪与障碍物规避解耦，仅在不可行区间进行最小偏离的局部修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 擦拭、抛光等接触丰富操作要求连续末端轨迹而非目标位姿；当规划模型未包含的障碍物与其相交时，传统规划器返回全程不同的避碰路径，丢弃了原本未被遮挡的轨迹。

Method: 将跟踪与规避解耦：可行区间精确跟随标称轨迹；不可行区间在标称轨迹周围走廊内采样构型空间障碍边界，连接成路线图，搜索偏离最小且最早重接标称轨迹的绕行路径。

Result: 在多种仿真障碍配置、Franka真机实验及关键算法组件消融中，实现无碰撞轨迹修复与局部任务修改，仿真和硬件上分别恢复52.4-78%的标称轨迹。

Conclusion: Close&Safe能在障碍物允许范围内最小化对任务的干扰，实现局部化的无碰撞轨迹修复。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation such as wiping, polishing and surface inspection specifies the task as a continuous end-effector trajectory rather than a goal pose. When an obstacle absent from the planning model intersects it, conventional planners return a collision-free path that may differ from the nominal throughout, discarding trajectory that was never obstructed. We present Close&Safe (Close to the intended task, Safe from obstacles), which decouples tracking from avoidance: the nominal is followed exactly wherever it remains feasible, and avoidance acts only on the interval where it is not, so tracking is hindered as little as the obstacle allows. On that interval we sample the configuration-space obstacle boundary within a corridor around the nominal, connect the samples into a roadmap, and search it for the detour that deviates least before rejoining the nominal at the earliest feasible configuration. We evaluate across diverse simulated obstacle configurations, physical Franka experiments, and ablations of key algorithmic components. The results demonstrate collision-free trajectory repair with localized task modification, while recovering 52.4-78% in simulation and on hardware.

</details>


### [31] [Learning What Actions Change: Action Effect Matching for Dynamics-Faithful World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25980)
*John Won,Yonghoon Dong,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AFFECT提出一种辅助训练目标，通过向动作注入时间相关噪声来学习动作扰动引起的视觉变化分布，从而提升世界模型对细微动作差异的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型虽然能生成逼真视频，但难以捕捉小动作变化对后续观测的影响，限制了其在机器人策略评估和基于模型的规划中的应用，因为几乎相同的动作可能导致截然不同的结果。

Method: 在基于任务无关人类遥操作训练的策略动作中注入时间相关噪声，训练世界模型使其生成视频对该噪声的响应与真实录制视频一致，无需从同一初始状态重复执行。

Result: 在OGBench仿真以及真实DROID和ALOHA-2设置中，AFFECT使世界模型rollout更贴近真实结果；DROID上策略成功率与世界模型预测的Pearson相关从0.47升至0.79，FVD降低最多23%；用于规划时任务成功率提升最多8.3%。

Conclusion: AFFECT通过让世界模型学习动作扰动引起的视觉变化分布，显著提升了世界模型的保真度和下游规划性能。

Abstract: World models aim to predict environment dynamics under actions, enabling the prediction of action consequences without executing them in the real world. Yet despite producing increasingly realistic videos, current world models often fail to capture how small action changes affect future observations. This limits their use in tasks such as robotic policy evaluation and model-based planning, where nearly identical actions can lead to qualitatively different outcomes. To address this, we propose AFFECT, an auxiliary training objective for learning the distribution of visual changes induced by action perturbations. Specifically, we inject temporally correlated noise into the actions of a policy trained on task-agnostic human teleoperation. We then train the world model so that its generated videos respond to this noise as the recorded videos do, without requiring repeated executions from the same initial state. Across OGBench simulation and real-world DROID and ALOHA-2 setups, AFFECT makes world-model rollouts more faithful to real outcomes. On DROID, the Pearson correlation between policy success rates in the world model and on the real robot rises from 0.47 to 0.79, alongside up to 23% lower FVD. These gains carry over to planning, where selecting actions with AFFECT-trained world models improves task success by up to 8.3%.

</details>


### [32] [Learning optimal gaits and styles for terrain-adaptive quadruped locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25958)
*Guillaume Bellegarda,Auke Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究结合深度强化学习与抽象振荡器网络，学习11种四足步态及其运动风格，并证明步态最优性由地形、性能指标和速度共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学与机器人学长期关注“哪种步态最好”，但现有答案多基于平地能量效率，忽略了地形和优化准则的影响。

Method: 使用深度强化学习和模拟脊髓中枢模式发生器的抽象振荡器网络，在多种复杂地形上学习11种四足步态及对应运动风格，再用第二个策略根据用户指定准则（如运输成本、基座稳定性、关节平滑度）选择步态。

Result: 每种步态至少在一种地形-指标-速度组合下最优；框架能零样本适应训练中未见过的步态耦合与风格、远超训练分布的地形，以及五倍训练质量的更大四足动物；仅增加一个俯仰跟踪奖励项即可支持双足模式运动。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种通用且系统的方式来生成富有表现力的运动，并选择最适合给定地形和准则的步态。

Abstract: Animals use many different gaits to locomote at different speeds across varying terrains. A longstanding question in biology and robotics is: which gait is best? This question is most often addressed from an energy-efficiency perspective on flat terrain, leaving open how the answer depends on terrain and on the criterion optimized. Here we show that gait optimality is determined jointly by terrain, performance metric, and velocity. We use deep reinforcement learning and networks of abstract oscillators, modeling spinal central pattern generators, to learn 11 quadrupedal gaits across diverse challenging terrains, together with the locomotion style (base height, foot ground clearance, foot offsets) that each requires. A second policy then selects among them to optimize a user-specified criterion such as cost of transport, base stability, or joint smoothness. Across these performance metrics and within this repertoire, every gait is optimal under at least one terrain-metric-velocity combination. The framework adapts zero-shot to gait couplings and styles never seen during training, to terrains far beyond the training distribution, and to larger quadrupeds five times the training mass. With a single additional pitch-tracking reward term, the same gait couplings also support bipedal-mode locomotion. Our method provides a general and systematic way to produce expressive locomotion and to select the gait best suited to a given terrain and criterion. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [33] [Fast-JEPA: Advancing the Success-Latency Frontier for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25927)
*Yao Zhang,Bin Yu,Shijie Lian,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Yu Wu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-JEPA 是一种面向潜空间世界模型的高效规划框架，通过重构预测计算加速动作序列搜索，在多个任务上兼顾高成功率与低规划延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间世界模型虽然能预测动作序列的结果，但测试时对候选方案进行递归 rollout 比较代价高昂，规划延迟大，难以满足执行时限和动态条件下的实际控制需求。

Method: Fast-JEPA 重构预测计算：先构建目标导向的计划来引导搜索，并直接预测完整候选动作序列的结果以高效比较，从而在细化阶段避免递归状态 rollout；同时提出 DynaGoal 基准，用于评估动态分布外条件、执行截止时间和受控响应延迟下的世界模型规划。

Result: 在 PushT、Reacher、Cube 和 TwoRoom 上平均成功率达 96.50%，规划速度比 INTACT 快 2.41 倍；搜索预算扫描表明其在不同候选数量和细化深度下均保持竞争力与低延迟；在 DynaGoal-Shift 和 DynaGoal-Deadline 上平均成功率分别为 73.83% 和 87.67%。

Conclusion: Fast-JEPA 推进了预测搜索的成功率-延迟 Pareto 前沿，证明了低延迟预测规划在变化控制条件和执行时限约束下具有实际价值。

Abstract: Latent world models predict the outcomes of action sequences, but comparing candidate plans through recursive rollout remains expensive at test time. We introduce Fast-JEPA, a latent world model planning framework that advances the success-latency Pareto frontier of predictive search. Fast-JEPA reorganizes predictive computation to support effective action-sequence search at low planning cost. It constructs a goal-directed plan to guide search and directly predicts the outcomes of complete candidates for efficient comparison, avoiding recursive state rollout during refinement. Across PushT, Reacher, Cube, and TwoRoom, Fast-JEPA achieves 96.50% average success rate with 2.41x faster planning than INTACT. Search-budget sweeps show that its competitive success and low latency extend across candidate counts and refinement depths. We further introduce DynaGoal, a benchmark for evaluating world-model planning under dynamic out-of-distribution conditions, execution deadlines, and controlled response delays. Fast-JEPA achieves average success rates of 73.83% on DynaGoal-Shift and 87.67% on DynaGoal-Deadline, demonstrating the practical value of low-latency predictive planning under changing control conditions and execution-time constraints. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [34] [Multiple-Camera Tracking of Rigid Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25904)
*Frédérick Martin,Radu Horaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用单目或多目相机跟踪刚性物体的方法，通过最小化预测模型点与图像轮廓的误差来对齐三维模型与图像，并深入分析了雅可比矩阵的奇异性及多相机配置的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性物体跟踪在计算机视觉中具有重要应用，但基于点对点与点对轮廓匹配的跟踪方法在奇异性和鲁棒性方面缺乏系统分析，尤其是多相机配置如何克服单相机奇异性问题尚需研究。

Method: 将跟踪过程建模为视觉伺服回路，在每帧图像采集时更新相机内外参数；通过最小化预测模型点与图像轮廓之间的图像误差来对齐三维模型；详细分析点对点和点对轮廓匹配下的雅可比矩阵，确定所需最小匹配数及导致秩亏的奇异配置，并建立两种匹配情形之间的数学联系；进一步研究多相机配置以优化鲁棒性。

Result: 建立了点对点与点对轮廓匹配之间的数学联系，证明后者具有与前者相同类型的奇异性；确定了最小匹配数和奇异配置；实验使用复杂船舶零件和最多三个相机验证了方法，表明跟踪器可有效用作相机标定程序。

Conclusion: 该方法能够鲁棒地跟踪刚性物体，多相机配置可克服单相机奇异性及数据缺失或噪声问题，且跟踪器本身可作为一种相机标定手段。

Abstract: In this paper we describe a method for tracking rigid objects using one or several cameras. The tracking process consists of aligning a 3-D model representation of an object with image contours by measuring and minimizing the image error between predicted model points and image contours. The tracker behaves like a visual servo loop where the internal and external camera parameters are updated at each new image acquisition. We study in detail the Jacobian matrix associated with this minimization process in the presence of both point-to-point and point-to-contour matches. We establish the minimal number of matches that are needed as well as the singular configurations leading to a rank-defficient Jacobian matrix. We find a mathematical link between the point-to-point and point-to-contour cases. Based on this link we show that the latter has the same kind of singularities than the former. Moreover, we study multiple camera configurations which optimize the robustness of the method in the presence of single-camera singularities, bad, noisy, or missing data. Extensive experiments done with a complex ship part and with up to three cameras validate the method. In particular we show that the tracker may well be used as a camera calibration procedure.

</details>


### [35] [From Sequence to Space: Explicit Spatial Belief with Vision-Language Distillation for Active Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25866)
*Ziyang Xu,Peng Han,Hai Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EAGLE，一种显式空间信念智能体，用蒸馏后的Swin学生网络从部分观测预测空中特征与不确定性并构建空间信念张量，再冻结学生网络用紧凑CNN actor-critic进行PPO训练，实现无需VLM或缓存视觉token的主动地理定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动地理定位方法虽能较好对齐目标模态，但将导航状态保留在大型序列Transformer中，并使用依赖隐藏目标位置的密集距离奖励，推理成本高且不够轻量。

Method: 离线联合训练教师条件Swin分支与无上下文Swin学生分支，二者均从随机掩码的部分拼接图输入；教师分支关注由Qwen2.5-VL-7B提取的冻结全场景上下文，两分支均接受掩码单元空中特征重建与不确定性监督，学生额外蒸馏教师的停止梯度特征预测。蒸馏后丢弃Qwen、缓存上下文与教师分支，保留学生从部分观测预测单元级空中特征嵌入与预测不确定性，并与模态对齐目标嵌入及轨迹导出的访问掩码组合构建空间信念张量；随后冻结学生，用紧凑CNN actor-critic以PPO优化。

Result: 在MASA、xBD零样本迁移和MM-GAG零样本空中目标划分上分别取得78.1%、76.4%和76.8%的平均成功率，达到当前最优，且推理时不使用VLM或缓存视觉token。

Conclusion: EAGLE通过显式空间信念建模与教师-学生蒸馏，将重型视觉语言模型知识迁移到轻量学生网络，在保持最先进性能的同时显著降低推理依赖，代码将公开。

Abstract: Active geo-localization (AGL) asks an agent to navigate a small aerial search grid and reach a target specified by an aerial image, a ground-level photograph, or text. Existing methods align the goal modalities well, but they keep the navigation state inside a large sequential Transformer and train with a dense distance reward that uses the hidden goal location. This paper proposes EAGLE, an Explicit spatial-belief Agent for Geo-Localization and Exploration. Offline training jointly optimizes a teacher-conditioned Swin branch and a context-free Swin student from the same randomly masked partial mosaics. The teacher branch attends to frozen full-scene context extracted by Qwen2.5-VL-7B, both branches receive masked-cell aerial-feature reconstruction and uncertainty supervision, while the student additionally distills stop-gradient feature predictions from the teacher. After distillation, Qwen, its cached context, and the teacher-attention branch are discarded. The retained student predicts cell-level aerial-feature embeddings and predictive uncertainty from partial observations alone. These predictions are combined with the modality-aligned goal embedding and a trajectory-derived visitation mask to construct the spatial belief tensor. The student is then frozen while a compact CNN actor-critic is optimized with PPO. EAGLE achieves state-of-the-art average success rates of 78.1%, 76.4%, and 76.8% on MASA, xBD zero-shot transfer, and the MM-GAG zero-shot aerial-goal split, respectively, without using a VLM or cached visual tokens at inference. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [36] [Learning-Based System Identification for DVL-Constrained AUV Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25854)
*Mingxi Zhou,Emir Cem Gezer,Lin Zhao,William McConnell,Chengzhi Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 全连接前馈神经网络可从IMU等可测量输入预测AUV三轴速度，实现无需DVL的水下定位，平均误差优于DVL辅助航位推算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数AUV依赖DVL与IMU进行航位推算，但DVL在软底质、超出量程等场景下无法底锁，仅靠IMU会显著漂移，且DVL成本高不利于多机器人集群作业。

Method: 基于广义六自由度动力学方程与运动学推导速度近似函数，证明其适合以可测量量为输入的前馈神经网络；用AUV实测数据训练全连接前馈网络，通过消融实验考察超参数影响并用Akaike信息准则选模型，在两天采集的三组新任务数据上测试。

Result: 神经网络预测速度的定位相比DVL辅助航位推算具有更低的平均均方根距离误差和标准差，但改进程度取决于海流条件。

Conclusion: 轻量级全连接前馈神经网络为DVL不可用或缺少其测量时的惯性水下定位提供了有吸引力的方案。

Abstract: Nowadays, most AUVs utilize Doppler velocity logs (DVLs) and inertial measurement units (IMUs) to achieve satisfactory dead-reckoning results. However, the DVL bottom lock may not be available in some scenarios, such as moving over a soft bottom or traveling further away from the seafloor beyond the DVL's range. As a result, the localization system solely reliant on the IMU data will experience significant drift. The relatively high cost of DVLs also inhibits multirobot or swarm operations.
