In this article, we present the results of using fully connected feedforward neural networks to predict vehicle velocity (surge, sway, and heave) for AUV localization. Approximation functions for the vehicle velocities are derived based on the generalized six degree-of-freedom dynamic equation and kinematics, proving that they are suitable for feedforward neural networks with measurable inputs. The neural networks are trained using field data collected using an AUV. An ablation study was conducted to investigate the impacts of hyperparameters, and the most effective models were selected using the Akaike information criterion. The selected neural network models are tested on three new sets of mission data collected on two different days. The quantitative results show that localization using vehicle velocities predicted by neural networks yields lower averaged root-mean-square distance errors and standard deviation compared to the DVL-aided dead-reckoning method. However, the extent of this improvement was found to depend on ocean current conditions. The promising results show that lightweight fully connected feedforward neural networks offer an attractive approach for inertial-based underwater localization when DVLs or their measurements are not available.

</details>

*Yasuhiro Fukuoka,Haruya Shirado,Yuto Nakamura,Haruto Okabe,Ahmad Najmuddin Ibrahim,Takahiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种附加伸缩底盘执行器的四轮轮椅楼梯攀爬机构，保证前后轮至少一侧静止，力与功主要由伸缩底盘承担且对楼梯倾角不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升四轮轮椅上下楼梯的安全性与稳定性，降低轮子受力与打滑风险，并兼顾平地机动性。

Method: 在四轮轮椅上增加辅助伸缩底盘执行器，构成简单的楼梯攀爬机构，通过伸缩底盘提供主要驱动力与机械功。

Result: 楼梯上行时前后轮至少一侧保持静止以增强安全；所需力与机械功主要依赖伸缩底盘而非轮子，且对楼梯倾角不敏感；陡梯上轮子打滑风险降低；平地可通过最短轴距实现紧凑转向、最长轴距增强稳定性。

Conclusion: 该伸缩底盘机构能安全高效地辅助四轮轮椅攀爬楼梯，并改善平地机动性与稳定性。

Abstract: We propose a simple stair traversal mechanism that augments a four-wheel wheelchair with an auxiliary telescopic chassis actuator. The proposed mechanism provides the following key contributions during stair ascent: (A) enhanced safety by ensuring that either the front or rear wheels remain stationary at all times; (B) primary reliance of the required force and mechanical work on the telescopic chassis rather than the wheels; (C) insensitivity of the required force and mechanical work to stair inclination; and (D) reduced risk of wheel slip on steeper stairs. In addition, the telescopic chassis is expected to improve level-ground mobility by enabling tight turning with the shortest wheelbase and enhancing stability with the longest wheelbase.

</details>


### [38] [Collision-Free Is Not Enough: Passage-Conditioned Navigation for Wheeled-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25788)
*Wai Ka Sun,Waiyiu Pong,Bowen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通道条件导航方法，利用单次27分钟教学记录的记忆事件，在无训练、无机器人模型条件下通过入口门控确保机器人重复穿越地形过渡时的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有腿机器人绕行后接近楼梯或边缘时可能偏差数厘米或数度，固定运动技能易停滞或跌倒；现有方法依赖机器人模型或大量试错，在未知技能的新场地无法使用。

Method: 记录每次教学穿越为事件，在每次局部重规划后强制执行：入口门控在边缘前检查，若偏离教学穿越超过10厘米或10°，则令机器人转向或后退重试；无需训练或机器人模型。

Result: 定位噪声下，受扰边缘穿越保持在限差内的比例从24.1%升至88.6%；接触仿真中180次爬梯无跌倒（忽略方向时9次），被推离1.2米后重新汇入路线，终点平均距路线0.14米（不汇入时1.10米），完成率与未受扰机器人相同；真实轮腿机器人比赛271米多障碍地形、7.0米高差，六次全完成，获2026全球开源AI挑战赛第三名（2999份提交），为任务中唯一完全自主完赛的决赛者。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于重复路线穿越地形过渡的固定技能机器人，如巡检和巡逻，单次教学即可避免错误入口导致的任务失败。

Abstract: A collision-free detour can still bring a legged robot to the next stair or ledge a few centimetres or degrees off, and a fixed locomotion skill, factory or learned, can stall or fall. Existing methods prevent this with a robot model or extensive trial and error, but on a new site with unknown skills neither is available, so they cannot be used. We find that a single 27-minute teaching session already supplies what these methods have to derive or learn: for every stair and ledge, one crossing the skill has completed. Our method, passage-conditioned navigation, makes the route remember how each transition was entered, not only where the path went. It stores each taught crossing as an event and, without training or a robot model, enforces it after every local replan: before each ledge, an entry gate makes the robot turn or back up and retry if it is more than 10 cm or 10° off the taught crossing. Under localization noise, with path, gait schedule, and sensing unchanged, this raises the share of disturbed ledge crossings that stay within these limits from 24.1% to 88.6%. In contact simulation with our learned policies, a robot pushed off course before stairs never falls in 180 climbs, against 9 falls when the stored direction is ignored; pushed 1.2 m away, it rejoins the route, ending on average 0.14 m from it (1.10 m without rejoining), and finishes as often as an undisturbed robot. This suits any robot with fixed skills that repeats a route over terrain transitions, as in inspection and patrol, where one wrong entry can end the task. We deployed it in competition on a real wheeled-legged robot: over 271 m of multi-obstacle terrain spanning 7.0 m of elevation, it completed all six runs, won third place at the 2026 Global Open-source AI Challenge, which drew 2,999 submissions, and was the only finalist in its task to finish fully autonomously.

</details>


### [39] [ECHO: In-Context Behavior foundation model for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25778)
*Xingyi Wang,Bin Li,Jiyuan Shi,Chenyun Zhang,Weiji Xie,Xiangyu Miao,Junsong Wu,Wei Song,Shiqiang Zhu,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ECHO是一种上下文行为基础模型，将通用行为先验与动力学特定的残差自适应分离，在多种运动和物理条件下超越所有基线，并在Unitree G1上实现全身遥操作与负载自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型虽具备广泛行为技能，但缺乏对底层动力学的区分，导致策略倾向于保守折衷而非环境特定精度。

Method: 提出ECHO：先用混合与退火采样在单次预训练中建立行为先验；再通过上下文表示与残差自适应从交互历史学习结构化动力学表示，指导对冻结先验的残差修正，无需测试时参数更新。

Result: 大量实验表明ECHO在多样运动和物理条件下优于所有基线；在Unitree G1上实现涵盖极端杂技和地形交互的全身遥操作，以及操作与全身控制中的负载自适应。

Conclusion: 分离通用行为能力和动力学特定执行，可将交互经验转化为环境特定控制，实现精确执行而不牺牲广泛行为覆盖。

Abstract: Humanoid behavior foundation models (BFMs) acquire diverse skills through large-scale motion imitation and massively parallel reinforcement learning, yet broad behavioral capability does not ensure precise execution across physical conditions. Without distinguishing the underlying dynamics, policies may favor conservative compromises over environment-specific precision. We introduce ECHO, an in-context BFM that turns interaction experience into environment-specific control, separating general behavioral capability from dynamics-specific execution. Mixed and Annealed Sampling first establishes a behavior prior in a single pretraining stage by balancing targeted practice with broad motion coverage. Building on this prior, Context Representation and Residual Adaptation learns structured dynamics representations from interaction histories to guide residual corrections to the frozen prior, enabling adaptation without test-time parameter updates. Extensive experiments show that ECHO outperforms all baseline methods across diverse motions and physical conditions. Moreover, deployment on the Unitree G1 demonstrates whole-body teleoperation spanning extreme acrobatics and terrain interaction, alongside payload adaptation in manipulation and whole-body control. Page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [40] [Hindsight Self-Distillation: Teaching Robots to Act from the Present](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25777)
*Haisen He,Yechi Liu,Tianle Shi,Haoyun Liu,Chuanzhang Meng,Tiancai Jiao,Yifan Bai,Xinyuan Chang,SongLin Dong,Zhiheng Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HSD 通过利用演示中后帧的未来信息，以自蒸馏方式训练仅依赖当前帧的学生策略，在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人必须在看到动作结果之前选择动作；演示中的后续帧包含动作后果信息，但部署时不可得，需要把这些未来信息蒸馏到仅用当前帧的策略中。

Method: 训练时教师分支同时看当前帧与后续帧，学生分支仅看当前帧，二者共享视觉编码器与动作预测器；各分支用少量特征向量汇总图像，动作预测器只使用这些视觉摘要；教师从演示动作学习，学生在每个去噪步骤从演示或教师的去噪预测学习，并对齐教师的视觉摘要；部署时学生只编码一次当前图像并在每个去噪步骤复用。

Result: 平均成功率在 LIBERO 上为 98.50%，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上为 92.31%。

Conclusion: 后见自蒸馏能让仅用当前帧的策略有效获取未来信息，实现高成功率且推理高效。

Abstract: A robot must choose an action before it can see the outcome. In a recorded demonstration, later camera frames show what happened after the robot moved. Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD) uses these later frames to teach a policy what to extract from current images. During training, a teacher sees both the current and later frames, while a student sees the current frames. They share a visual encoder and an action predictor. Each branch summarizes its images with a small set of feature vectors. These visual summaries supply all visual input to the action predictor. The teacher learns from demonstrated actions. For each noisy action sequence, the student learns from either the demonstration or the teacher's denoising prediction. It also learns to match the teacher's visual summaries. We analyze which aspects of the teacher's predictions the student can recover from its inputs. At deployment, the student encodes the current images once and reuses its summaries at every denoising step. HSD achieves average success rates of 98.50\% on LIBERO and 92.31\% on RoboTwin 2.0.

</details>


### [41] [TipDex-VTLA: Learning When to Refine Actions with Touch for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25683)
*Xudi Ge,Yepeng Liu,Haifeng Liu,Enze Shi,Liangqi Zhang,Yongchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TipDex-VTLA视觉-触觉-语言-动作策略，通过视觉动作可靠性估计与触觉预测验证来调节执行时的动作修正，提升光照变化下的灵巧操作鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光照变化会在物理状态不变时导致灵巧操作的动作漂移；现有VTLA策略虽引入触觉反馈，但在视觉条件变化时如何以及何时利用触觉精修动作仍具挑战。

Method: 构建VTLA策略TipDex-VTLA，包含两部分：视觉动作敏感度估计（VASE）从冻结参考策略对光照扰动的动作敏感度中学习视觉可靠性；执行期触觉预测与修正（TACE）在匹配时刻比较预测与实测触觉，将差异与视觉可靠性结合，对未执行动作进行有界特征修正的门控。

Result: 在四个真实世界灵巧操作任务和UniVTAC仿真基准上评估；仅用正常视觉条件数据训练，真实任务中正常光照下平均全任务成功率87.5%，挑战性未见光照下73.8%，显著优于部分最先进方法。

Conclusion: 结合视觉可靠性估计与触觉预测验证可有效在挑战性未见视觉条件下精修动作，提升灵巧操作策略的鲁棒性，并将开源数据与代码。

Abstract: Changes in illumination can induce action drift in dexterous manipulation even when the physical state remains unchanged. Although existing vision--tactile--language--action (VTLA) policies incorporate tactile feedback, when and how touch refines actions under changing visual conditions remains challenging. We introduce \textbf{TipDex-VTLA}, a VTLA policy that combines estimated visual action reliability and tactile forecast verification to regulate execution-time refinement. Specifically, Visual Action Sensitivity Estimation (VASE) learns visual reliability from a frozen reference policy's action sensitivity to illumination perturbations. Tactile Anticipation and Correction during Execution (TACE) compares predicted and measured touch at matched times and combines the resulting discrepancy with visual reliability to gate bounded feature corrections for unexecuted actions. This effectively helps to refine actions under challenging unseen visual conditions. We evaluate TipDex-VTLA on four real-world dexterous manipulation tasks and the UniVTAC simulation benchmark. TipDex-VTLA trained on data collected under normal visual conditions, achieves average full-task success rates of 87.5% (resp. 73.8%) under normal (resp. challenging unseen) lighting in real-world tasks, significantly outperforming some state-of-the-art methods. We will release the collected data and source code.

</details>


### [42] [LeCompass: Task-Level Execution through State-Indexed Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25679)
*Kai Shi,Shiqi Zhao,Zimo Li,Hairong Qu,Hongyu Ma,Yongsong Yang,Jiahao Ai,Ziran Yin,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeCompass是一个基于冻结LeWorldModel的状态索引执行框架，通过提议器生成中间潜在目标、CompassFlow在轨迹先验源空间中搜索动作候选，并结合空间反馈与记忆实现任务级执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件JEPA虽能在表示空间建模动作后果，但潜在空间局部可达性评估本身不构成任务级执行接口：需生成中间目标、局部推理预算有限、执行需要空间反馈和持久交互状态。

Method: 构建以冻结LeWM为核心的状态索引执行框架：提议器提供中间潜在目标；CompassFlow在学习的轨迹先验源空间搜索，在有限预测评估预算下获得有效动作候选；patch-token读出提供空间反馈；基于观测的记忆支持阶段转换与有界恢复；状态索引目标支持跨回合上下文-目标组合。

Result: 在1000次官方TEST完整回合Cube试验中，完整LeCompass执行系统达到86.9%的原生4厘米到达率；匹配分析显示预测排序将TEST PushT成功率从4.7%提升至44.9%，将TEST TwoRoom从33.8%提升至77.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明可实现任务级执行，且潜在目标提议、预测搜索与观测 grounded 控制的作用因任务而异。

Abstract: Action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a foundation for predictive decision-making by modeling the consequences of actions in representation space. Yet evaluating local reachability in latent space does not by itself provide a task-level execution interface: intermediate targets must be generated, local inference has finite budgets, and execution requires spatial feedback and persistent interaction state. We introduce LeCompass, a state-indexed execution framework built around a frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM). A proposer supplies intermediate latent targets, while CompassFlow searches the source space of a learned trajectory prior to obtain effective action candidates under limited predictive-evaluation budgets. Patch-token readouts provide spatial feedback, while observation-based memory supports phase transitions and bounded recovery. State-indexed targets support cross-episode context-goal composition. On 1,000 official TEST full-episode Cube trials, the complete LeCompass execution system achieves 86.9% native 4-cm reach. Separate matched analyses show that predictive ranking raises success from 4.7% to 44.9% on TEST PushT and from 33.8% to 77.7% on TEST TwoRoom. These results demonstrate task-level execution with task-dependent roles for latent target proposal, predictive search, and observation-grounded control.

</details>


### [43] [CauSyncVLA: Causal Latent World Modeling for Asynchronous Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25667)
*Xu Pan,Chubin Zhang,Xingrui Yu,Runmin Lan,Zihao Wang,Wenkai Guo,Bofang Jia,Bo An,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CauSyncVLA是一个异步双系统控制器，通过因果潜在世界模型预测感知记忆并用预测流热启动校正动作轨迹，在视觉推理与执行重叠时保持动作连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略需在视觉推理仍在运行时做出反应，异步重叠计算与执行虽减少等待，但会导致感知记忆过时和连续动作块之间不连贯。

Method: 提出CauSyncVLA异步双系统控制器：用因果潜在世界模型从延迟视觉锚点和已执行状态-动作历史预测当前感知记忆，并通过动作敏感蒸馏保留冻结专家的控制响应；基于更新后的记忆，用预测流热启动（PFWS）对齐未执行动作horizon进行单步流校正，无需额外训练。

Result: 在LIBERO-10上达到93.8%成功率，推理吞吐量提升5.04倍，并改善闭环鲁棒性和机器人运动平滑性。

Conclusion: CauSyncVLA通过因果感知记忆预测与动作轨迹校正，有效解决了异步VLA执行中的感知过时和动作不连贯问题，在保持高成功率的同时显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies must react while visual inference is still running. Overlapping computation and execution reduces waiting, yet leaves perceptual memory stale and successive action chunks disconnected. We present CauSyncVLA, an asynchronous dual-system controller that aligns perceptual-memory prediction and trajectory correction with actual robot execution. Rather than forecasting pixels or physical states, a Causal Latent World Model predicts current perceptual memory from delayed visual anchors and executed state-action history, supervised through action-sensitive distillation to retain the frozen expert's control response. Conditioned on this updated memory, Predictive Flow Warm-Starting (PFWS) aligns the unexecuted action horizon for single-step flow correction without extra training. On LIBERO-10, CauSyncVLA reaches 93.8% success at 5.04$\times$ inference throughput, improving closed-loop robustness and robot motion smoothness.

</details>


### [44] [GNetSS: Towards Learning GNSS Corrections without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25665)
*JiaXu Chee,Simon Denman,Fahira Afzal Maken,Qingqing Cheng,Amir Khodabandeh,Milad Ramezani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自监督GNSS定位校正框架GNetSS，结合Transformer网络与可微定位求解器，无需真值位置训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督式GNSS校正方法依赖真值位置，难以规模化部署；单点定位（SPP）在复杂环境下鲁棒性不足。

Method: GNetSS由Transformer网络和确定性可微定位求解器组成：网络建模卫星间几何与时序上下文，预测逐卫星伪距校正；求解器在GNSS几何及可选运动约束下将校正后的测量转换为接收机位置校正；训练信号直接来自GNSS测量一致性，无需真值。

Result: 在开阔天空、城市和森林环境中均提升鲁棒性，尤其能抑制大误差离群值，最大三维定位误差较SPP降低50%以上。

Conclusion: 自监督伪距校正为实现可扩展、鲁棒的GNSS定位提供了潜力，适用于真实自动驾驶应用。

Abstract: We present GNetSS, a self-supervised GNSS position correction framework that improves the robustness of single point positioning (SPP) without requiring ground-truth locations for training. GNetSS combines a transformer-based network with a deterministic differentiable positioning solver. The network predicts satellite-wise pseudorange corrections by modelling inter-satellite geometry and temporal context, while the solver converts these corrected measurements into receiver position corrections under GNSS geometry and optional motion constraints. Unlike supervised GNSS correction methods that rely on ground-truth, GNetSS derives its training signal directly from GNSS measurement consistency. Experimental results show that GNetSS improves robustness across open-sky, urban, and forested environments. The method is particularly effective at suppressing large-error outliers, reducing the maximum 3D positioning error by over 50% compared with SPP. These results demonstrate the potential of self-supervised pseudorange correction for scalable and robust GNSS positioning in real-world autonomy applications. Code and the in-house dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [45] [A$^2$-VLA: Adapting Vision-Language-Action Policies to New Tools through Agentic Affordance Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25656)
*Chenyuan Zhou,Chaoyi Huang,Zibo Wang,Zhiyuan Li,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出A^2-VLA框架，结合具身智能体与VLA策略，将工具可供性视为可构建、测试和修正的假设，实现跨工具操作技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同形状工具可具有相似功能，但跨工具迁移操作技能需理解工具几何与任务的关联并调整策略，核心挑战是发现跨工具的共用可供性并验证其能否支持新工具完成类似任务。

Method: 给定少量源工具的遥操作演示，智能体在仿真中重建场景并自主确定任务相关的可供性特征（功能区域及其关键几何属性）；通过重建场景中的自主实验提炼特征与可供性提取技能，验证通过的技能存入可复用技能库；训练和推理时以编码的功能区域特征作为π_{0.5}骨干的条件输入。

Result: 在基于可供性的仿真跨工具轨迹重定向中达到86.1%成功率；在三个真实机器人工具使用任务上，较π_{0.5}任务成功率提升30.7个百分点。

Conclusion: A^2-VLA通过可供性假设的自主发现与验证，有效提升了跨工具操作技能迁移与真实机器人任务成功率。

Abstract: Tools with different shapes can have similar functions, but transferring manipulation skills across tools requires understanding how tool geometry relates to the specific task and adjusting the manipulation strategy accordingly. The key challenge is to discover the common affordances that contribute to task completion across different tools and verify whether they support new tools in performing similar work. We present A$^2$-VLA, a tool-adaptation framework that combines an embodied agent with a VLA policy. It treats tool affordances as hypotheses the agent can construct, test, and revise. Given teleoperated demonstrations with a few source tools, the agent reconstructs the scenes in simulation and autonomously determines task-relevant affordance features: functional regions and their key geometric properties. It develops affordance-extraction skills and refines both the features and skills through autonomous experiments in the reconstructed scenes. Validated extraction skills accumulate in a reusable skill library for unseen tools. Finally, A$^2$-VLA uses encoded functional-region features as conditioning inputs to a $\pi_{0.5}$ backbone during training and inference. Evaluation examines affordance reliability in reconstructed scenes and real-robot task success on unseen tools. Our method achieves 86.1% success in simulated cross-tool trajectory retargeting based on affordances. On three real-robot tool-use tasks, A$^2$-VLA improves task success by 30.7 percentage points over $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond Easy Clients: Difficulty- and Improvement-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25637)
*Ye Lin Tun,Chu Myaet Thwal,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FedDIA联邦强化学习聚合方法，按客户端对全局模型的改进量与环境难度评分，解决客户端掌握环境后更新无信息却回报高的平台期问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦聚合对客户端均匀加权，忽略机器人环境难度差异；按回合回报加权更糟，因为简单环境天然回报高。两者都受平台期问题困扰：客户端掌握环境后更新信息少但回报仍高。

Method: FedDIA根据本地训练对接收到的全局模型的改进程度给客户端打分，并用环境难度放大该分数；难度由模型自身表现推断，无需环境描述；两个信号均以全局模型为基准，故跨客户端可比。

Result: 在八个异构客户端的无地图导航任务上，FedDIA训练与留出环境平均成功率达75.5%，最强联邦基线为68.7%；迁移到手写仓库和居住空间布局或一个客户端故障时优势仍保持。

Conclusion: 以全局模型为参照、结合改进量与推断难度的客户端评分，可有效缓解平台期问题，提升异构联邦强化学习的聚合效果与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning enables a fleet of robots to jointly learn a shared navigation policy without exchanging raw experience. In practice, however, the robots rarely operate in the same conditions: an open aisle is far easier to navigate than a cluttered room, and takes less learning to master. Standard federated aggregation rules ignore this. Uniform averaging weights every client equally, whether or not it is still learning, and weighting by episode return is worse, since easy environments permit high returns by construction. Both failures stem from what we call the \emph{plateau problem}: once a client masters its environment, its updates carry little new information even though its reported return remains high. We propose \emph{FedDIA}, which scores each client by how much its local training improves the global model it received, amplified by the difficulty of its environment. Difficulty is inferred from the model's own performance, without requiring any description of the environment. Both signals are measured against the global model, so they are comparable across clients. On a mapless navigation task with eight heterogeneous clients, FedDIA reaches a mean success rate of $75.5\%$ across training and held-out environments, against $68.7\%$ for the strongest federated baseline. Its advantage holds when the models are transferred to hand-authored warehouse and living-space layouts, or when one of the clients is defective.

</details>


### [47] [PAD: Policy-Aware Data Valuation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25604)
*Chengkai Xu,Yicheng Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Yiming Cui,Shiyu Fang,Peng Hang,Hao Zhang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出策略感知数据估值框架PAD，为每个候选驾驶演示分配针对目标策略的效用值，实现高效数据筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶依赖数据规模扩展，但数据增长不保证策略性能同比提升；同一演示对不同规划器可能冗余甚至有害，现有筛选方法无法衡量下游策略效用。

Method: PAD结合场景级校准反馈、投影策略梯度与局部训练几何的正则化近似，生成可扩展的基于影响函数、面向特定策略的数据价值。

Result: 组移除实验验证价值方向保真性；跨规划器分析显示同一候选池在不同规划器家族中排名差异显著；在DiffusionDrive上NavTest提升3.5分、NavHard提升5.0分EPDMS，并在扩散、回归、流匹配规划器上优于仅真实数据和全池训练，同时显著减少场景级性能回退。

Conclusion: 策略感知数据估值可作为替代无差别数据积累的基础，实现定向策略改进，使自动驾驶系统扩展更高效可靠。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving increasingly depends on scaling training demonstrations, yet data growth does not guarantee proportional gains. However, additional data do not guarantee better policies: a demonstration that benefits one planner may be redundant or harmful to another. Existing curation methods typically infer data value from intrinsic sample properties or training-dependent proxies that do not measure downstream policy utility. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Policy-Aware Data Valuation (PAD)}, a framework that assigns each candidate demonstration a utility specific to the target driving policy. PAD combines scene-level calibration feedback with projected policy gradients and a regularized approximation of local training geometry, producing scalable influence-based values for the policy. A controlled group-removal study establishes the directional fidelity of these values, while cross-planner analysis shows that the same candidate pool induces markedly different rankings across planner families. On DiffusionDrive, PAD improves EPDMS over real-only training by $3.5$ points on NavTest and $5.0$ points on NavHard. Policy-specific curation consistently outperforms real-only and full-pool training across diffusion-, regression-, and flow-matching-based planners while substantially reducing scene-level regressions. These results establish policy-aware data valuation as a basis for replacing indiscriminate data accumulation with targeted policy improvement, enabling more efficient and reliable scaling of autonomous-driving systems.

</details>


### [48] [GraF-VTA: Neuro-Symbolic Trajectory Generation from Vision and Temporal Logic via Graph-Based Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25589)
*Zezhi Liu,Zhiwei Zheng,Hanqian Luo,Deyun Qin,Shizhen Wu,Yongchun Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraF-VTA以图结构融合STL语法与视觉特征、因果观测历史及闭环重规划，生成满足时序逻辑的任务轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测和信号时序逻辑规划机器人任务，需要将任务要求锚定到场景证据，并根据执行反馈调整计划。

Method: 提出基于图的GraF-VTA框架：双向交叉注意力融合STL语法token与视觉特征，可选冻结自动机嵌入提供无时间结构；时间金字塔保留当前细节并压缩旧帧形成执行上下文；以这些特征和机器人状态为条件，用流匹配模型生成计划，执行动作前缀后基于刷新观测重规划。

Result: 在五个仿真环境中，单图配置在四个环境于K≥16时开环Success@K高于TeLoGraF和Vision-TL-Action；Panda匹配更新消融中，Success@256从无观测历史的53.91%提升至带金字塔记忆的62.50%；闭环Dubins评估中Success@1024达64.06%，对比adapted S-MSP的12.50%和adapted Logic-VLA的27.34%。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉条件、观测历史与重规划相结合，可提升满足STL轨迹的覆盖能力。

Abstract: Robotic planning from visual observations and signal temporal logic (STL) requires grounding task requirements in scene evidence while adapting plans to execution feedback. We present GraF-VTA, a graph-based framework that extends Vision-TL-Action with structured task representations, causal observation history, and closed-loop trajectory generation. Bidirectional cross-attention fuses STL syntax tokens with visual features, while an optional frozen automaton embedding supplies complementary untimed task structure. A temporal pyramid preserves detailed current observations and compresses older frames to provide recent execution context. Conditioned on these features and robot state, a flow-matching model generates plans whose action prefixes are executed before replanning from refreshed observations. Across five simulation environments, the single-graph configuration achieves higher open-loop Success@$K$ than TeLoGraF and Vision-TL-Action on four environments at the reported budgets $K\geq16$. On Panda, matched-update ablations increase Success@256 from 53.91\% without observation history to 62.50\% with pyramid memory. In closed-loop Dubins evaluation, it reaches 64.06\% Success@1024, compared with 12.50\% for adapted S-MSP and 27.34\% for adapted Logic-VLA. These results support combining structured visual conditioning with observation history and replanning to improve coverage of STL-satisfying trajectories.

</details>


### [49] [Cognitive-Horizon Action Chunking: Memory-Guided Adaptive Reasoning for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25587)
*Weile Gong,Zijian Lu,Jiahao Zheng,Bo Xu,Yiping Zuo,Jiajia Guo,Xin He,Weijun Wang,Weibei Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架CHAC，通过记忆引导自适应推理减少多模态基础模型在具身导航中的推理调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型在具身导航中频繁推理带来巨大计算开销，需要在不重新训练策略的前提下降低模型调用频率。

Method: CHAC利用保留的视觉上下文、完成线索与控制器反馈确定认知视界，即执行缓存路点数量后再刷新预测；在策略请求停止时，用局部访问历史引导有界修正，最多增加一次移动且不额外调用模型。

Result: 在HM3D-OVON和R2R-CE上分别减少33.4%和64.7%的视觉语言模型调用，成功率分别下降0.63和10.82个百分点；两个基准上每次模型调用的成功率和SPL均提升；四项物理导航任务各40次运行中，CHAC成功18次对原生14次，模型调用减少54.9%，GPU推理时间减少49.7%。

Conclusion: CHAC以免训练方式显著降低导航推理计算成本，并在物理任务中提升成功次数与效率。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models are advancing embodied navigation, but frequent inference incurs substantial computational cost. We present Cognitive-Horizon Action Chunking (CHAC), a training-free framework for memory-guided adaptive reasoning with a frozen navigation policy. Retained visual context, completion cues, and controller feedback determine the cognitive horizon, or how many cached waypoints the robot executes before refreshing its prediction. At the policy's stop request, local visitation history guides a bounded correction with at most one extra movement and no additional model calls. CHAC reduces vision-language model calls by 33.4% on HM3D-OVON and 64.7% on R2R-CE, with success-rate decreases of 0.63 and 10.82 percentage points, respectively. Success rate and success weighted by path length improve per model call on both benchmarks. Across four physical navigation tasks with 40 runs per method, CHAC achieves 18 successes versus 14 for native execution, reducing model calls by 54.9% and GPU inference time by 49.7%.

</details>


### [50] [Trajectory-Calibrated Merging of Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25575)
*Wenshuo Chen,Honglei Jin,Le Yang,Jianfei Song,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Junze Zhu,Kailong Wang,Zhengxi Jin,Haozhe Jia,Honglin Chu,Songning Lai,Xiucheng Wang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory-Calibrated Regression (TCR)，一种执行感知、免梯度的机器人专家策略合并框架，将多个专家策略整合为单一策略并在LIBERO上显著优于现有合并方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型被适配为任务专家后能力分散在多个策略中，现有模型合并方法在闭环执行中难以可靠保持行为：参数空间对齐只是行为的间接代理，回归方法在标定输入未覆盖的状态上可能无法保持行为。

Method: TCR在两个互补时间尺度上收集标定输入：专家rollout中不同任务执行阶段的输入，以及迭代动作生成过程中的中间状态；为应对合并带来的表示偏移，通过已合并块重新计算每个块的输入，并用各专家在该块内的计算构造模块输入，拟合合并权重以匹配专家的线性输出；采用两遍标定流程，利用独立rollout缓存并保留冻结专家作为教师进一步精化合并策略。

Result: 在LIBERO上合并四个π0.5专家，1200个评估回合成功率77.83%，分别比FeatCal、RegMean和直接权重平均高出9.50、24.17和33.00个百分点。

Conclusion: TCR生成的模型保留原始推理图，无需专家路由或额外梯度训练，能有效将机器人专家策略整合为单一策略并保持闭环执行行为。

Abstract: Robot foundation models are increasingly adapted into task specialists to expand their embodied capabilities. Yet these capabilities remain fragmented across separate policies, and existing model-merging methods do not reliably preserve them during closed-loop execution: parameter-space alignment provides only an indirect proxy for behavior, while regression may fail to preserve behavior at states not covered by its calibration inputs. We present Trajectory-Calibrated Regression (TCR), an execution-aware, gradient-free framework that consolidates robot specialists into a single policy. Across two complementary timescales, TCR collects calibration inputs from expert rollouts spanning different stages of task execution and from intermediate states within iterative action generation. To account for representation shifts introduced by merging, TCR recomputes each block's input through the already merged blocks. It then uses each expert's computation within the block to form module inputs and fits merged weights to match the experts' linear outputs. A two-pass calibration procedure further refines the merged policy using separate rollout caches while retaining the frozen specialists as teachers. The resulting model preserves the original inference graph and requires neither expert routing nor additional gradient training. On LIBERO, TCR merges four $\pi_{0.5}$ specialists and achieves 77.83% success across 1,200 evaluation episodes, outperforming FeatCal, RegMean, and direct weight averaging by 9.50, 24.17, and 33.00 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [51] [Communication-Aware UAV Spatio-Temporal Relay Cooperation and Planning in Complex Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25558)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yingjie He,Zehao Zhuo,Xiqun Chen,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ST-RCP框架，利用配送无人机作为机会中继，通过时空预测与联合规划解决城市低空无人机通信中断问题，显著提升通信覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市低空空间通信环境异质，无人机移动导致时变通信中断，影响任务执行与飞行安全，且不希望依赖额外通信基础设施。

Method: 构建ST-RCP框架：在有限规划时域内预测通信盲区事件，建立helper-event兼容图，联合优化中继选择、障碍感知轨迹重规划与通信感知调度，并评估多种规划器在覆盖、计算成本与能耗间的权衡。

Result: 基于真实交通场景实验，峰值场景下ST-RCP使下降阶段通信覆盖率较基站基线提升29.93%，较1、2、3架专用中继无人机配置分别提升27.38%、22.02%、15.20%；过程灵活性分析表明预测性任务感知中继扩大了可行中继资源池并提升通信连续性。

Conclusion: 轨迹赋能的无人机协同可提升城市空中作业的安全性与韧性，ST-RCP在保持配送任务可行性的同时有效改善通信覆盖。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed in low-altitude urban airspace for delivery, emergency response, and other applications. However, UAV mobility across spatially heterogeneous urban communication environments can lead to time-varying communication outages, disrupting mission execution and compromising flight safety. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a Spatio-Temporal Relay Cooperation and Planning (ST-RCP) framework that leverages delivery UAVs as opportunistic communication relays for disconnected UAVs without requiring additional communication infrastructure. The framework predicts communication blind-spot events over a finite planning horizon, constructs a helper-event compatibility graph, and jointly optimizes relay selection, obstacle-aware trajectory replanning, and communication-aware scheduling. Multiple planners are evaluated to investigate the trade-offs among communication coverage, computational cost, and energy consumption. Experiments based on real-world traffic scenarios compare the performance of ST-RCP against baseline methods. In the peak scenario, ST-RCP improves descent-phase communication coverage by 29.93% compared with the base-station baseline and by 27.38%, 22.02%, and 15.20% compared with configurations using one, two, and three dedicated relay UAVs, respectively. Process flexibility analysis demonstrates that predictive task-aware relaying expands the pool of feasible relay resources and improves communication continuity while maintaining the feasibility of delivery missions. These findings demonstrate the potential of trajectory-enabled UAV coordination to enhance the safety and resilience of urban aerial operations.

</details>


### [52] [CoachSI: Self-Improvement of Robot Policies with a Language Coach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25529)
*Jiachen Tao,Gengyu Zhang,Haoxuan Wang,Bing Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoachSI方法，利用冻结的视觉语言模型以语言形式为机器人策略提供下一步原子指令作为教练信号，通过成功轨迹重标注微调策略，显著提升长时程任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略能可靠执行原子技能，但在组合成长时程任务时失败；仅靠策略自身经验自改进无法突破其分布限制，缺少的是“下一步该做什么”而非技能本身。

Method: 使用冻结的现成VLM观察机器人并以策略原子指令格式命名下一步（不输出动作）；将教练成功完成的轨迹用原始任务指令进行事后重标注，用于微调策略；微调后的策略独立部署，动作主干保持冻结。

Result: 在World Action Model和VLA模型停滞的状态上，命名下一步使子目标完成率提升4.6倍；微调后未见场景长时程任务无辅助成功率从47.4%升至59.3%，比人类演示微调高9.8个百分点，比等数据预算的自采集成功微调高7.3个百分点；仅用相机图像的教练达到56.8%，高于两个基线。

Conclusion: 语言教练教会策略下一步应发生什么，已有技能完成其余部分，从而在动作主干冻结下实现长时程任务的自改进。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies execute atomic skills reliably but fail when the same skills are composed into long-horizon tasks. Self-improvement from the policy's own experience does not close this gap: experience collected by the policy alone stays within its own distribution and reproduces what it can already do. We show that what the policy lacks is not a skill but the next step. We present CoachSI, a self-improvement method in which a frozen, off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM) coaches the policy in language: it watches the robot and names the next step in the format of the policy's atomic instructions, and never outputs an action. Successful coached rollouts are relabeled in hindsight with the original task instruction and used to fine-tune the policy. The fine-tuned policy is then deployed on its own. On states where a World Action Model and a Vision-Language-Action model have stalled, naming the next step raises subgoal completion by 4.6x over the unchanged task instruction, and the frozen VLM supplies it from the same camera images the policy sees. After fine-tuning, unassisted success on long-horizon tasks in unseen scenes rises from 47.4% to 59.3%, 9.8 points above fine-tuning on human demonstrations and 7.3 points above fine-tuning on self-collected successes at an equal data budget. With a coach that sees only camera images, it reaches 56.8%, above both baselines. The gain is obtained with the action backbone frozen: language coaching teaches the policy what should happen next, and the skills it already has do the rest.

</details>


### [53] [Learning Ankle Exoskeleton Control for Reactive Standing Balance Through Neuromusculoskeletal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25480)
*Maria T. Tagliaferri,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仿真到真实的框架，结合运动模仿与强化学习训练踝关节外骨骼平衡辅助控制器，并在健康受试者中验证其可提升抗扰动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人及神经肌肉损伤者反应性平衡缺陷增加跌倒风险，需要改善平衡恢复的干预手段；可穿戴机器人外骨骼有潜力，但控制器需适时适度辅助，而评估每种策略需反复施加失稳扰动，开发困难。

Method: 用单个受试者的小规模运动捕捉数据训练肌肉驱动的神经肌肉骨骼模型，使其复现对后向平移扰动的运动学与动力学响应，作为虚拟人；以此训练强化学习外骨骼控制器，控制器仅用惯性传感器测量生成双侧踝辅助，无需显式扰动检测；随后部署于双侧踝外骨骼，在5名未参与训练的健康受试者中评估。

Result: 辅助使受试者可承受的不迈步扰动幅度提高11.8%，峰值质心位移和速度分别降低13.5%和28.2%；个体收益随基线恢复策略和体重而变化。

Conclusion: 单个虚拟人可产生可迁移的平衡辅助效果，更多样化的虚拟人有望进一步提升控制器有效性。

Abstract: Deficits in reactive balance can increase fall risk among older adults and individuals with neuromuscular impairments, motivating the need for interventions that improve balance recovery. Wearable robotic exoskeletons offer a promising approach to addressing this need by augmenting balance control. However, developing controllers that deliver appropriately timed and scaled assistance remains challenging, as evaluating each candidate strategy requires repeated exposure to destabilizing perturbations. We propose a simulation-to-real framework that combines motion imitation and reinforcement learning to develop balance-assistive ankle exoskeleton control. A muscle-driven neuromusculoskeletal model was trained using a small set of motion-capture data from a single subject to reproduce kinematic and kinetic responses to backward-translation perturbations. This model served as a virtual human for training a reinforcement-learning exoskeleton controller. The resulting controller generated bilateral ankle assistance using only inertial sensor measurements, without explicit perturbation detection. We implemented the controller on-board a bilateral ankle exoskeleton and evaluated it in five healthy subjects whose data were not used for training. Assistance increased the perturbation magnitude that subjects could withstand without stepping by 11.8% and reduced peak center-of-mass displacement and velocity by 13.5% and 28.2%, respectively. Individual benefits varied with baseline recovery strategy and body mass. These results indicate that a single virtual human can yield transferable balance assistance, and that more diverse virtual humans could further improve its effectiveness.

</details>


### [54] [Demonstration-Free Skill Composition for Navigation and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25462)
*Madeleine Nouri,Gelareh Mohammadi,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估了无需演示的分层强化学习流程在MiniGrid导航和机器人操作任务中的表现，并与扁平强化学习基线进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习可将序列任务表示为单独训练技能的组合，但需要评估无需演示的分层流程在训练需求和任务性能上与扁平基线的差异。

Method: 在导航中，两个参数化技能在手动高层计划下复用于未见过的FourRooms布局；在操作中，分别训练到达、抓取对齐和提升技能，下游技能从前驱rollout收集的状态初始化。

Result: 在未见导航布局上，分层流程显著优于扁平DQN基线且训练交互更少；操作中分层链比扁平SAC训练步数更少且种子间变异更低，冻结的Pick和Lift技能可通过额外训练Place技能复用于堆叠。

Conclusion: 无需演示的分层强化学习流程在样本效率和稳定性上优于扁平基线，并支持技能复用。

Abstract: Hierarchical reinforcement learning enables sequential tasks to be represented as compositions of separately trained skills. This paper evaluates demonstration-free hierarchical pipelines for MiniGrid navigation and robotic manipulation, comparing their training requirements and task performance with flat reinforcement learning baselines. The evaluation concerns complete pipelines with task-specific observation, reward and control structures. In navigation, two parameterised skills are reused across unseen FourRooms layouts under manually specified high-level plans. In manipulation, reaching, grasp alignment and lifting are trained separately, with downstream skills initialised from states collected during predecessor rollouts. Across unseen navigation layouts, the hierarchical pipeline substantially outperforms the evaluated flat DQN baselines while requiring fewer training interactions. In manipulation, the hierarchical chain requires fewer training steps than flat SAC and exhibits lower observed variation across seeds. The frozen Pick and Lift skills are further reused for stacking by training an additional Place skill.

</details>


### [55] [Stereo Visual Odometry with In-the-Loop Geometric Feed-Forward Models Towards Dense Metric Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25445)
*Fahira Afzal Maken,Sagun Man Singh Shrestha,Edward Vanderfeen,Lucas Carvalho de Lima,Rongxin Li,Changming Sun,Milad Ramezani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合几何前馈模型稠密深度预测与因子图视觉里程计的立体视觉里程计框架，实现精确定位与稠密度量建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接以可能含噪的立体深度条件化几何前馈模型效果不佳，需在保留模型式立体视觉里程计鲁棒性与效率的同时，利用稀疏但可靠的度量几何校准稠密深度预测。

Method: 利用立体与时间多视图观测估计的优化3D地标，在每个局部子图内对所有关键帧联合估计尺度-平移变换以校准稠密预测；采用分层因子图架构：局部层滑动窗口优化高频相机位姿与多视图精化3D地标，仅对选定关键帧生成稠密预测并用SE(3)广义ICP配准，连续子图间用共享几何进行Sim(3)对齐以校正残余尺度不一致，最终将子图内与子图间约束纳入全局因子图。

Result: 在公开与自采数据集上，里程计精度与几何及学习型基线具有竞争力，同时提供准确的稠密度量重建，并提升了对学习深度预测失败的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过用稀疏可靠度量几何校准稠密几何前馈模型预测并结合分层因子图优化，可在保持模型式立体视觉里程计鲁棒性与效率的同时实现一致的轨迹与稠密度量重建。

Abstract: We present a stereo visual odometry (SVO) framework that integrates geometric feed-forward models (GFMs) for dense depth prediction with factor-graph-based visual odometry to achieve accurate localisation and dense metric mapping. Our key idea is to exploit sparse but reliable metric geometry estimated from stereo and temporal multi-view observations to calibrate dense GFM predictions, while retaining the robustness and efficiency of model-based SVO. Rather than directly conditioning the GFM on potentially noisy stereo depth, optimised 3D landmarks are used to jointly estimate a scale-shift transformation over all keyframes within each local submap, yielding metrically consistent dense depth predictions. To balance accuracy and computational efficiency, we introduce a hierarchical factor-graph architecture. At the local level, sliding-window optimisation estimates high-frequency camera poses and multi-view-refined 3D landmarks. Dense predictions are generated only for selected keyframes and registered within each submap using SE(3) Generalised ICP. Consecutive submaps are further aligned in Sim(3) using their shared geometry, allowing residual scale inconsistencies to be corrected. These intra- and inter-submap constraints are incorporated into a global factor graph to produce a consistent trajectory and dense metric reconstruction. Extensive experiments on public and in-house datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive odometry accuracy against geometric and learning-based baselines while providing accurate dense metric reconstruction and improved robustness to failures in learned depth prediction.

</details>


### [56] [Stuck in Place: Stall Diagnosis and Remedies for JEPA Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25415)
*Junhyeok Lee,Dongheon Lee,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA世界模型的失败大多源于规划循环中的停滞固定点，而非分布偏移下的模型误差；作者提出Unstick方法打破该固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时适应将JEPA世界模型在推物任务上的失败归因于分布偏移下的模型误差并更新权重，但未检验该归因是否与规划循环本身有关。

Method: 跟踪实际执行的规划循环，发现绝大多数失败为停滞（模型预测有进展但物体未动），热启动GD规划器返回近乎不变的固定点计划；提出Unstick，结合部署前在策略微调与固定权重的循环打破器（误差残差记忆或每次重规划随机重初始化）。

Result: 在推物任务中，多数设置（无论有无分布偏移）至少96%的失败为停滞；使用记忆时GD可恢复大部分适应增益，重初始化在形状偏移上恢复大部分增益但在视觉偏移上较少。

Conclusion: 在GD下限制这些规划器的主要因素是规划循环的固定点，该固定点在有/无分布偏移时均存在，且固定权重规划器即可打破。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models plan from pixels with model predictive control, yet often fail on tasks their model should handle. Test-time adaptation attributes such failures to model error under distribution shift and updates the weights on the current episode, without checking this attribution against the planning loop itself. We follow the executed planning loop on pushing tasks. In most settings, with and without distribution shift, nearly all failures (at least 96%) are stalls, where the object stays in place while the model predicts progress. The warm-started gradient-descent (GD) planner then returns nearly the same plan, a fixed point where the agent mostly idles. We propose Unstick, which pairs on-policy fine-tuning before deployment with a fixed-weight loop breaker: a residual memory of the episode's prediction errors or random re-initialization at every replan. With GD, Unstick recovers most of the adaptation gain on pushing tasks with the memory, and with re-initialization most of it on shape shifts but much less on visual shifts. A large part of what limits these planners under GD is thus a fixed point of the planning loop, present with and without distribution shift, which a planner with fixed weights can break. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [WAMotor: Accelerating World Action Models via Speculative Inference under Dual Nonuniform Denoising Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25413)
*Zexun Lin,Yuan Feng,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAMotor提出首个面向世界动作模型（WAMs）双重非均匀性的投机推理方法，通过延迟投机选择可靠锚点速度并结合积分步长感知验证，实现2.86×–4.28×端到端加速且保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs整合流匹配速度以统一世界建模与动作生成，但多步去噪延迟高；现有投机推理方法要么需训练任务专用草稿模型，要么在复用历史速度作草稿时忽视WAM去噪动力学，导致精度损失。

Method: 重新审视WAM去噪动力学，识别出速度平滑性非均匀（首尾步速度残差差异大）与积分时间步非均匀的双重非均匀性，并理论推导轨迹误差容忍界，刻画速度残差与积分步长对最终动作分布的影响；据此提出WAMotor：通过延迟投机选取可靠锚点速度，并采用按积分步长缩放接受阈值的时间步感知验证，可即插即用。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Plus上广泛评测，WAMotor实现2.86×–4.28×端到端加速，同时保持任务成功率。

Conclusion: WAMotor是首个针对WAMs双重非均匀性定制的投机推理方法，可无缝集成现有WAMs，在保证成功率的前提下显著降低去噪延迟。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) unify world modeling and action generation for robotic control by integrating flow-matching velocities, yet suffer from high multi-step denoising latency. Recent studies have explored speculative inference to accelerate WAMs by drafting and verifying denoising steps in parallel. However, existing methods either require training task-specific draft models or overlook critical WAM denoising dynamics when reusing past velocities as drafts. We revisit the denoising dynamics of WAMs and identify a dual nonuniformity that breaks standard velocity-reuse assumptions: velocity smoothness is nonuniform as adjacent velocity residuals differ sharply at early and final steps, so reuse there yields large residuals, which nonuniform integration time steps further translate into local state errors that accumulate into the final action distribution. Accordingly, we formalize this by theoretically deriving a trajectory error tolerance bound that characterizes how velocity residuals and integration time steps affect the final action distribution. Based on this, we propose WAMotor, the first speculative inference method tailored to the dual nonuniformity in WAMs. It selects reliable anchor velocities by deferring speculation and employs time-step-aware verification that scales acceptance thresholds by integration time step size. WAMotor offers plug-and-play benefits, enabling seamless integration with prior WAMs. Extensive evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and LIBERO-Plus show that WAMotor achieves 2.86$\times$--4.28$\times$ end-to-end speedups while maintaining task success rates. Our code is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [58] [Dune: A Zeroth-Order Optimizer with Time-Conditioned Historical Error Regression for VLA Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25400)
*Chengyang Dong,Ze Chen,Kai Ye,Chutian Wang,Heng Xu,Wei Mo,Maomao Li,Jiayan Xu,Jiayuan Wu,Yihao Xiao,Linhua Ye,Zhonghua Peng,Ruize Han,Jialong Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出零阶优化器Dune，通过融合历史误差预测与当前扰动观测，稳定长时程测试时自适应，低内存下提升VLA模型成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于反向传播的TTA在资源受限机器人上内存开销大，而零阶方法在有限扰动预算下估计方差高，长时程更新易停滞或退化。

Method: Dune从过去优化动态中学习轻量级时间条件误差预测器，将预测与瞬时零阶估计融合，实现无需反向传播和额外前向的方差缩减；并从理论上刻画高维小种群下估计方差对收敛的限制。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实机器人实验中，Dune在低内存占用下取得更高成功率，消融实验证实闭环稳定性和扰动后恢复能力提升。

Conclusion: 历史统计信息可有效缓解零阶估计方差，使长时程测试时自适应在资源受限场景下稳定可行。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) enables Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models to handle distribution shifts during deployment without source-domain retraining. However, backpropagation-based adaptation incurs heavy memory overhead on resource-constrained robots, whereas zeroth-order (ZO) methods suffer from high estimation variance under limited perturbation budgets. We observe that while ZO estimation enables low-memory adaptation with rapid initial gains, it often stalls or degrades over long-horizon updates due to noisy gradient estimates. To address these limitations, we propose Dune, a zeroth-order optimizer that stabilizes long-horizon adaptation by coupling online historical error prediction with current perturbation observations. Specifically, Dune learns a lightweight, time-conditioned error predictor from past optimization dynamics and fuses its predictions with instantaneous ZO estimates, achieving effective variance reduction without backpropagation or extra forward passes. Additionally, we theoretically characterize how estimation variance restricts convergence in high-dimensional, small-population regimes, motivating the use of historical statistics. Extensive experiments on LIBERO and real-world robots demonstrate that Dune achieves superior success rates under a low memory footprint, with ablations confirming improved closed-loop stability and post-disturbance recovery.

</details>


### [59] [RefreshGrad: An Online Adaptive Data-sampling Method for Diffusion-based Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25398)
*Dijia Zhan,Jinyi Li,Xuemiao Xu,Yan Zhou,Yu Liu,Shaoshan Liu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RefreshGrad在线自适应数据采样框架，周期性利用五种策略原生信号评估时间窗口价值并动态调整采样权重，在LIBERO-Plus任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示数据训练VLA策略时通常均匀采样轨迹片段或依赖静态离线数据筛选，但演示数据在不同时间窗口的监督价值差异很大，且每个窗口对策略的价值在训练过程中会动态变化，而非固定或仅由原始损失反映。

Method: RefreshGrad在不改变策略架构、训练目标和推理成本的前提下，周期性使用五种策略原生信号从不断演化的策略中为时间窗口打分，并在训练过程中动态调整其采样权重，使优化聚焦于信息丰富且可学习的窗口。

Result: 在十个LIBERO-Plus操作任务上，视觉扰动下平均成功率从60.7%（Uniform）和68.2%（FAKTUAL）提升至76.1%；干净场景下从57.4%和73.3%提升至81.3%。

Conclusion: 在线自适应地评估并调整时间窗口采样权重能有效提升VLA策略训练效果，且无需修改模型架构或增加推理开销。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) policies from demonstrations typically samples trajectory segments uniformly or relies on static offline data curation before training. However, we find that demonstration data vary sharply in supervision value across temporal windows, and that each window's value to the policy evolves substantially during training rather than being fixed or captured by raw loss alone. Motivated by this finding, we propose RefreshGrad, an online adaptive data-sampling framework that periodically scores temporal windows from the evolving policy using five policy-native signals and dynamically adjusts their sampling weights during training. RefreshGrad focuses optimization on informative, learnable windows without altering the policy architecture, training objective, or inference cost. Across ten LIBERO-Plus manipulation tasks, RefreshGrad boosts average success from 60.7% (Uniform) and 68.2% (FAKTUAL) to 76.1% under visual perturbations, and from 57.4% and 73.3% to 81.3% in clean scenes.

</details>


### [60] [Differentiable Hydrodynamics for Whole-Body MPC of Underwater Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25373)
*Hefan Wang,Yvan Petillot,Carlos Mastalli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可微解析水动力模型，结合球谐力/力矩场与CFD辨识的附加惯性矩阵，用于水下车辆-机械臂系统的全身MPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下车辆-机械臂系统需协调推进器与关节电机以扩展工作空间，但全身MPC依赖在线可计算且可微的水动力模型，而CFD在线评估过于昂贵。

Method: 构建可微解析水动力模型：用光滑球谐函数表示力与力矩场，结合从CFD辨识的耦合附加惯性矩阵；模型紧凑表达速度与加速度相关载荷并提供闭式导数，导数通过自由漂浮车辆-机械臂动力学传播，用于全身轨迹优化与滚动时域控制，联合优化推进器力与关节力矩。

Result: 留出CFD测试验证了主要平移力、旋转力矩场及附加惯性响应；解析雅可比计算更快且避免中心有限差分的步长敏感性；在独立海洋仿真器Stonefish中闭环实验展示了全身MPC。

Conclusion: 将几何相关水动力直接提供给基于梯度的控制，使水动力及其灵敏度参与车辆与机械臂之间的运动分配，而非仅视为扰动。

Abstract: Underwater vehicle-manipulator systems distribute actuation across thrusters and joint motors. Whole-body model predictive control (MPC), rather than separate vehicle and arm controllers, can coordinate both to extend the workspace and exploit the system's full kinematic capability. Doing so, however, requires a hydrodynamic model and derivatives throughout the prediction horizon. Computational fluid dynamics (CFD) captures geometry-dependent forces and moments for such a model, but is too expensive to evaluate online. We present a differentiable analytical hydrodynamic model combining smooth spherical-harmonic force and moment fields with a coupled added-inertia matrix identified from CFD. It compactly represents velocity and acceleration-dependent loads and provides their derivatives in closed form. These derivatives are propagated through free-floating vehicle--manipulator dynamics for whole-body trajectory optimization and receding-horizon control, jointly optimizing thruster forces and joint torques for reaching and continuous tracking. Held-out CFD tests validate the principal translational-force and rotational-moment fields and the added-inertia response. The analytical Jacobians evaluate faster and avoid the step-size sensitivity of central finite differences. Closed-loop Stonefish experiments demonstrate whole-body MPC in an independent marine simulator. By making geometry-dependent hydrodynamics directly available to gradient-based control, the model incorporates water forces and their sensitivities into motion allocation between the vehicle and arm, rather than treating them only as disturbances.

</details>


### [61] [Learning to Move from and for Manipulation Policies: Spatializing Latent Action Representations for Goal-Pose-Free Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25366)
*Siyuan Zheng,Zhong Cao,Henry X. Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M4M框架，利用预训练操作策略的隐表示学习操作感知的移动能力，统一导航与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作成功依赖机器人与目标物体的空间关系及任务，需调整位姿；联合训练导航与操作计算和数据成本高，预设目标位姿缺乏灵活性。

Method: 以预训练操作策略动作生成网络的隐表示作为密集监督，结合少量操作执行结果，学习空间条件嵌入以估计候选位姿的操作成功率；从立体观测估计未访问位姿的成功率，并集成到导航策略训练中，用学习到的交接策略决定何时从导航切换到操作。

Result: 在未见过的RoboCasa厨房的八个铰接物体任务中，M4M平均任务成功率为47.0%，最强基线为26.5%，直接操作为19.2%；在关闭冰箱实验中，四个冻结操作策略均有一致提升。

Conclusion: M4M使机器人能够以统一、任务感知的方式移动和操作，显著提升任务成功率。

Abstract: The success of manipulation policies is closely tied to the spatial relationship between the robot and the target object, as well as the task being performed, making the ability to adjust the robot's pose critical for successful execution. When the robot starts away from the task, such adjustments require coordinating navigation with local manipulation. Jointly training these capabilities incurs substantial computational and data costs, while navigating toward a predefined goal pose limits the flexibility needed to adapt to task-specific manipulation requirements. To address these challenges, we propose Learning to Move from and for Manipulation Policies (M4M), a framework that learns manipulation-aware mobility directly from pretrained manipulation policies. By leveraging the hidden representations of the policy's action-generating network as dense supervision, together with a limited number of manipulation execution outcomes, we learn a spatially conditioned embedding that captures task-dependent manipulation success across candidate robot poses. From the robot's current stereo observations, this representation estimates manipulation success at unvisited poses without rolling out the manipulation policy at each location. It is further integrated into navigation policy training, while a learned handoff policy decides when to transition from navigation to manipulation, enabling the robot to move and manipulate in a unified, task-aware manner. Across eight articulated-object tasks in unseen RoboCasa kitchens, M4M achieves 47.0% mean task success, compared with 26.5% for the strongest baseline and 19.2% for direct manipulation execution, and refrigerator-closing experiments show consistent improvements across four frozen manipulation policies.

</details>


### [62] [MoRE: Mixture-of-Experts for Multi-Physics Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25336)
*Ziyu Zhu,Shaowei Liu,Albert J. Zhai,Henry Che,Wei-Chiu Ma,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MoRE，一种基于混合运动专家的3D世界模型，将机器人交互分解为刚体、可变形和抓取三种物理机制，结合MPC提升真实世界操控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D世界模型难以统一处理多样物体交互带来的异质动力学。

Method: 将机器人交互分解为不同物理机制，分别分配给刚体、可变形、抓取三类专用运动专家；训练混合仿真与真实数据；与模型预测控制（MPC）集成。

Result: MoRE在各物理机制下预测精度提升，并带来更强的真实世界MPC操控性能。

Conclusion: 围绕物理机制组织的混合运动专家可在单一模型中兼顾多样物理行为并保留机制特定先验，提升预测与下游控制效果。

Abstract: A robotic world model should predict how objects evolve under robot actions and physical dynamics. While recent 3D world models have shown strong predictive capabilities, they still struggle with the heterogeneous dynamics arising from diverse object interactions. To address this in a unified framework, we decompose robotic interactions into distinct physical regimes and assign each to a specialized motion expert. We introduce MoRE, a 3D world model with a mixture of motion experts organized around rigid, deformable, and prehensile dynamics. This design enables a single model to capture diverse physical behaviors while preserving regime-specific priors. Trained on mixed simulation and real-world data, MoRE integrates with Model Predictive Control (MPC) to support diverse manipulation tasks. Experiments show that MoRE improves prediction accuracy across physics regimes, leading to stronger real-world MPC performance. Code, data, and project website are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [Plan your Self-Adaptation! Ontology-Mediated Task and Architecture Co-adaptation Planning for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25254)
*Gustavo Rezende Silva,Tobias John,Francisco Martín,EinarR Broch Johnsen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PLANTA提出一种本体介导的任务与架构协同自适应方法，通过整合OWL本体和PDDL规划模型实现联合规划，并在执行中重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人需要在运行条件变化时同时调整任务计划和架构配置，二者相互依赖：架构决定可执行动作，任务计划决定所需能力，因此需要在任务规划中考虑这些依赖。

Method: 使用OWL本体表示架构备选方案及其功能与质量需求，用PDDL定义任务动作和任务目标，自动翻译并集成这些模型，采用满足性规划进行任务与重构动作的联合规划，并在执行中变化使当前计划失效时重规划。

Result: 在水下检测和地面导航两个机器人场景中评估，支持运行时协同自适应的可行性；在地面导航场景中，与最优策略生成相比规划时间和内存消耗更低，且生成的计划质量相近。

Conclusion: PLANTA能够实现任务与架构的运行时协同自适应，并在评估场景中以更低计算开销获得与最优策略生成质量相近的计划。

Abstract: Autonomous robots may need to adapt both their task plans and architectural configurations as operating conditions change. These decisions are interdependent: architectural configurations determine which actions a robot can perform, while task plans determine which capabilities are required. This paper presents PLANTA, an ontology-mediated approach to task and architecture co-adaptation that considers these dependencies during mission planning. PLANTA uses an ontology expressed in the Web Ontology Language to represent architectural alternatives and their associated functional and quality requirements, while the Planning Domain Definition Language defines task actions and mission goals. By automatically translating and integrating these models, PLANTA enables joint planning of task and reconfiguration actions. The approach uses satisficing planning and replans when changes during execution invalidate the current plan. PLANTA is evaluated in two robotic scenarios involving underwater inspection and ground navigation. The results support the feasibility of runtime co-adaptation and show lower planning time and memory consumption compared with optimal-policy generation in the evaluated navigation scenario, while producing plans of similar quality.

</details>


### [64] [WEVO: World Model-Enabled VLA Optimization for Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25251)
*Qianzhong Chen,Muyuan Lin,Yuyin Sun,Mac Schwager,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WEVO 提出了一种无需遥操作的人形全身 VLA 后训练框架，在动作条件视频世界模型中做闭环强化学习，真实机器人任务平均成功率从 23.3% 提升到 75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能提升 VLA 策略，但人形机器人的真实探索不安全，仿真强化学习又会利用仿真到现实的残差差距。

Method: 先在仿真操作与移动操控数据上改进接触动力学与视角变化预测，用合成演示训练的 VLA 采集每任务 20 次自主真实机器人 rollout 以对齐世界模型；随后将 VLA、全身控制器与视频世界模型组成带稠密进度奖励的闭环强化学习环境。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务上，play 数据提升了所有评估的世界模型指标与下游策略成功率；视频世界模型强化学习将平均成功率从 23.3% 提升至 75.0%，而真实机器人强化学习为 48.3%。

Conclusion: 仿真提供策略初始化与广泛覆盖，视频世界模型提供与部署对齐的策略改进环境，二者互补。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can improve vision-language-action (VLA) policies beyond imitation, but humanoid exploration is unsafe and simulator RL can exploit residual sim-to-real gaps. We present WEVO, a teleoperation-free framework for post-training a whole-body humanoid VLA in an action-conditioned video world model (WM). Simulated manipulation and locomotion play data improve contact-dynamics and viewpoint-change prediction. A VLA trained on synthetic demonstrations collects 20 autonomous real robot rollouts per task to align the WM with deployment observations. The VLA, whole-body controller (WBC), and video WM then form a closed-loop RL environment with dense progress rewards. Across three real robot tasks, play data improves every evaluated WM metric and downstream policy success; video WM RL raises mean success from 23.3% to 75.0%, compared with 48.3% for real robot RL. We therefore leverage the complementary strengths of both environments: simulation provides policy initialization and broad coverage, while the video WM offers a deployment-aligned environment for policy improvement. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [65] [A Synthetic Multi-Modal Testbed for Security Stress Testing of Agentic Counter-UAS Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25249)
*Ivan A. Fernandez,Jingdao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的合成C-UAS测试平台，用于可重复地压力测试感知与跟踪算法，并以EKF为例评估LiDAR退化下的位置估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主信息物理系统的感知与状态估计易受传感器退化、不一致或被操纵的影响，进而引发安全相关故障，因此需要一个可控、可重复的测试环境来研究传感退化对下游自主估计与韧性的影响。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中构建合成C-UAS测试平台：简化的四旋翼按脚本轨迹飞行，共置虚拟传感器生成同步的RGB、RTX LiDAR和示意性类红外观测；平台可改变目标距离、运动、杂波、遮挡和传感器可用性，并规划传感器丢失、时间错位、跨模态不一致和虚假观测等压力用例。初步示例采用非线性协调转弯扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF），在20秒机动轨迹上基于LiDAR目标质心测量进行评估。

Result: 以EKF为初始示例，在20秒机动轨迹上对比了LiDAR观测逐步退化和丢失时的位置误差与不确定性变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究传感退化如何影响下游自主估计及C-UAS系统韧性提供了可重复的实验环境。

Abstract: Agentic cyber-physical systems rely on perception and state estimation to support autonomous decisions. Degraded, inconsistent, or manipulated sensor observations can therefore propagate through an autonomous pipeline and create security-relevant failures. This work presents a synthetic Counter-Unmanned Aircraft System (C-UAS) testbed developed in NVIDIA Isaac Sim for repeatable stress testing of perception and tracking algorithms supporting agentic decision-making. A simplified quadrotor follows scripted trajectories while co-located virtual sensors generate synchronized RGB, RTX LiDAR, and illustrative IR-like observations. The testbed varies target range, motion, clutter, occlusion, and sensor availability, with future stress cases including sensor dropout, temporal misalignment, cross-modal disagreement, and false observations. As an initial exemplar, a nonlinear coordinated-turn Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) is evaluated over a 20-s maneuvering trajectory using LiDAR target-centroid measurements. Position error and uncertainty are compared as LiDAR observations degrade and are lost. The framework provides a reproducible environment for studying how sensing degradation affects downstream autonomous estimation and resilience in agentic C-UAS systems.

</details>


### [66] [Verify2Act: Critic-Guided Latent World Models for Verifying Language-Conditioned Manipulation Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25243)
*Chrisantus Eze,Christopher Crick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Verify2Act框架将语义规划与潜在物理验证分离，在DINOv2特征空间用世界模型预测并双头评价器验证，实现低成本机械臂高成功率操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能提出多步操作计划，但缺乏对场景变化的接地模型；像素级世界模型验证慢且长时漂移。

Method: VLM提出候选计划，潜在世界模型V2A-WM在DINOv2特征空间想象执行，双头评价器检查时间一致性与目标满足，全部在数字孪生数据上训练。

Result: 六项语言条件任务成功率83.3%，优于消融世界模型61.7%、纯VLM 53.3%、像素扩散世界模型46.7%；所有执行计划均经验证且无规划失败，失败均源于低级抓取放置失误；CALVIN五任务链成功率最高，反思VLM调用比像素扩散少30倍；杂乱螺母装配成功率最高。

Conclusion: 潜在物理验证可有效提升VLM操作规划的成功率与效率，并避免规划级失败。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can propose multi-step manipulation plans, but they have no grounded model of how a scene will change, and the pixel-level world models that could check those plans are slow and drift over long horizons. We present Verify2Act, a modular verification framework that separates semantic action proposal from latent physical verification. A VLM proposes candidate plans, a latent world model (V2A-WM) imagines each of them in the DINOv2 feature space, and a dual-head critic checks the imagined rollouts for temporal consistency and goal satisfaction before anything is executed. V2A-WM extends recent flow-matching world models with cross-attention action grounding and a causal temporal history window. We deploy the full system on a low-cost robot arm, training the world model and critic only on data generated in a digital twin. Across six language-conditioned tasks, Verify2Act reaches 83.3\% task success, compared with 61.7\% for a latent world model without our architectural changes, 53.3\% for the VLM alone, and 46.7\% for a pixel-diffusion world model. It verified every plan it executed and incurred no planning-induced failures: all of its failed episodes were caused by low-level grasp or placement slips. In simulation, Verify2Act obtains the highest five-task chain success on CALVIN among the compared baselines with 30$\times$ fewer reflective VLM calls than pixel-diffusion verification, and the highest success on cluttered nut assembly.

</details>


### [67] [Ferrofluid Shape Control with Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25232)
*Anthony Czubarow,Antonio Terpin,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用深度基于模型的强化学习，通过物理交互控制单个固定电磁铁，使铁磁流体在非平衡瞬态下达到多边形、星形和五格骨牌等多种指定形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁流体可作为软体机器人，但其非线性连续动力学使可靠控制困难；现有工作仅能建立和维持平衡构型，而利用有限驱动下的流体动力学达到瞬态非平衡形状是互补目标。

Method: 采用深度基于模型的强化学习，策略直接通过物理交互学习，基于视觉观察为单个固定电磁铁选择电流指令，引导移动的铁磁流体趋向指定几何形状。

Result: 学习到的策略反复成功实现多种形状，包括多边形、星形和五格骨牌。

Conclusion: 直接从物理交互学习可实现可靠的流体形状控制，为铁磁流体软体机器人的控制开辟新可能。

Abstract: Ferrofluids are liquids containing magnetic nanoparticles, enabling shape control through external magnetic fields. This deformability supports their use as soft robotic bodies that can navigate confined spaces and manipulate objects. Their nonlinear continuum dynamics, however, make reliable control challenging. Existing work shows how spatial magnetic fields from electromagnet arrays can establish and maintain desired ferrofluid configurations. A complementary objective is to exploit the fluid's dynamics under limited actuation to reach prescribed shapes that may not be sustainable at equilibrium. Here, we use deep model-based reinforcement learning to control these transient shapes. Learning directly through physical interaction, our policies select current commands for a single fixed electromagnet based on visual observations, guiding the moving ferrofluid toward prescribed geometries. The learned policies repeatedly attain a variety of shapes spanning polygons, asterisks and pentominoes. These results demonstrate that learning directly from physical interaction enables reliable fluid shaping, opening new possibilities for controlling ferrofluid-based soft robots.

</details>


### [68] [Bridging vision-language reasoning and learned control for legged navigation in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25215)
*Takahiro Miki,Robin Schmid,Manthan Patel,Turcan Tuna,Julia Richter,Julian Nubert,Joonho Lee,Matthias Müller,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROAM 将视觉语言模型（VLM）的世界知识与强化学习（RL）学到的运动与局部规划策略结合，使腿足机器人能在高山和城市环境完成公里级自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界环境多样复杂，自主导航需要既能应对多样地形的鲁棒底层运动控制，又需要解释环境线索的高层推理能力，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: ROAM 由在互联网规模数据上预训练的 VLM 提供常识推理与语义解释（评估风险、解读标识、生成带导航代价的语义标签），开放词汇分割模块把标签转为稠密实时风险图，用于调节两个预训练 RL 策略（敏捷运动与局部导航），从而耦合高层 VLM 推理与底层物理控制。

Result: 真实世界实验表明系统能自主穿越多种复杂环境：以极少干预完成公里级徒步，解读标识以避开危险，并评估环境以识别安全落脚区域。

Conclusion: 该工作向非结构化环境中的可靠部署迈进一步，但也凸显 VLM 在可靠性与空间推理方面的挑战，基础模型虽是关键组件但尚非野外导航的完整解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in diverse real-world environments remains one of the greatest challenges in robotics. It demands both robust low-level motion control to handle diverse terrain and high-level reasoning to interpret environmental cues. We present ROAM (Robust Open-world Autonomous Mobility), a system that enables legged robots to perform kilometer-scale navigation in complex alpine and urban settings. ROAM combines the world knowledge of a Vision-Language Model (VLM), pre-trained on internet-scale data, with robust locomotion and local planning policies learned in simulation via reinforcement learning (RL). The VLM provides common-sense reasoning and semantic interpretation of the scene, assessing risks, interpreting signs, and producing semantic labels with associated navigation costs. An open-vocabulary segmentation module converts these labels into a dense, real-time risk map, which conditions two pre-trained RL policies responsible for agile locomotion and local navigation, effectively coupling high-level VLM reasoning with low-level physical control. Real-world experiments showed the system's ability to autonomously navigate a wide range of complex real-world environments. The robot completes kilometer-long hikes with minimal intervention, interprets signs to avoid danger, and evaluates its environment to identify safe areas on which to step. This work represents a step toward the reliable deployment of systems in unstructured environments. However, it also underscores the challenges that remain in VLM reliability and spatial reasoning, indicating that foundational models are a crucial component but not yet a complete solution for navigation in the wild.

</details>


### [69] [VG-Steer: Visual-Geometric Policy Steering from In-Context Aligned Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25199)
*Arthur Bucker,Pramod Anantharam,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VG-Steer，利用大视觉模型和视觉语言模型推理，将任务相关实体对及其物体关系距离投影结合为视觉-几何引导信号，在推理时校正冻结策略以应对分布外场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据稀缺且多模态导致端到端策略过拟合，微小分布偏移即失效；现有测试时适应方法依赖从机器人数据隐式学习的信号，同样易受偏移影响，而利用显式空间信息的方法校正过于粗糙，难以捕捉操作任务所需的精细运动。

Method: VG-Steer通过迁移成功分布内轨迹中沿有意义几何轴（物体关系距离对）的精细运动来校正现有策略，借助大视觉模型（LVM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义理解推理，将任务相关实体对及其物体关系距离投影融合为定制的视觉-几何引导信号。

Result: 在仿真基准中高效校正流匹配策略以应对位姿和任务目标偏移；真实机器人实验中扩大冻结策略覆盖的工作空间区域，在训练位置外一个网格步长的线缆放置任务上成功率提升一倍以上，并引导单任务策略操作从未训练过的物体，同时几乎消除物体混淆。

Conclusion: VG-Steer通过显式视觉-几何引导有效实现测试时策略校正，提升分布外泛化能力，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Adapting robot policies to out-of-distribution (OOD) settings with limited real-world data remains a key bottleneck to generalist robots. The multimodality and scarcity of robotics data lead end-to-end approaches to overfit and break under small distribution shifts. Test-time adaptation can alleviate this by correcting frozen policies at inference, but most methods ground their correction in a signal learned implicitly from robotic datasets, making it susceptible to the same shifts as the policy it corrects. Recent methods that instead exploit explicit spatial information are typically limited to coarse corrections, missing the nuanced, task-relevant motion most manipulation tasks require. We propose VG-Steer, which corrects existing policies by transferring the nuanced motion of successful in-distribution rollouts, captured along meaningful geometric axes, known here as object-relational distance pairs. VG-Steer leverages the semantic understanding of Large Vision Models (LVMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) reasoning, which combines the task-relevant entity pairs and their object-relational distance projections into a single tailored visual-geometric guidance signal. In a simulation benchmark, it efficiently corrects flow-matching policies under pose and task-goal shifts; on a real robot experiment, it widens the region of the workspace the frozen policy covers, more than doubling its success rate on cable placements one grid step outside the training positions, and steers a single-task policy toward objects it was never trained on while nearly eliminating object confusion. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [70] [Old-School Planners Drive (Sometimes) Better Than Learned Ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25138)
*Andreas Look*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文实现十六种教科书式规划器，并在nuScenes、nuPlan和HUGSIM三个基准上评估，发现最佳手工规划器在开环和闭环指标上可与端到端模型UniAD的规划器竞争甚至更优，且在HUGSIM上将驾驶评分从32.0提升至41.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶模型从数据中学习规划，手工规划器被普遍视为过时；作者检验这一判断是否合理，即手工规划器是否仍具竞争力。

Method: 实现十六种教科书式规划器，每种由路径来源（参考线、A*、混合A*、RRT*）与轨迹方法（Frenet栅格、非线性优化、s–t搜索、动态窗口）组合而成；在nuScenes开环、nuPlan闭环（真值感知）和HUGSIM照片级闭环上评估，并在HUGSIM中仅替换UniAD的规划器、读取其冻结感知，不使用高精地图或其他特权信息。

Result: 在nuScenes和nuPlan上最佳教科书规划器具竞争力（L2误差0.735 m对UniAD的0.947 m；nuPlan Test14-hard含反应式智能体得分79.3对PDM-Closed的76.1）；在HUGSIM上十六种中有九种超过UniAD学习规划器，最佳组合（混合A*路径加非线性轨迹优化）将HD-Score从32.0提升至41.8；增益在静止和对向车辆场景最大，对转向撞向自车的车辆无增益；在UniAD规划器撞车的182次运行中，有31次最佳教科书规划器在相同感知下完成路线。

Conclusion: 手工教科书式规划器并未过时，在相同感知下可超越端到端学习规划器；部分碰撞源于规划器而非感知，表明规划模块仍是端到端系统的瓶颈。

Abstract: End-to-end driving models learn to plan from data, and hand-written planners are widely treated as a thing of the past. We test whether this is justified: we implement sixteen textbook planners, each combining a path source (reference line, A*, hybrid A*, RRT*) with a trajectory method (Frenet lattice, nonlinear optimization, $s$--$t$ search, dynamic window), and evaluate them on three benchmarks: open loop on nuScenes, closed loop on nuPlan with ground-truth perception, and photorealistic closed loop on HUGSIM. On HUGSIM, they replace only the planner of the end-to-end model UniAD and read its frozen perception, without an HD map or other privileged information. On nuScenes and nuPlan, the best textbook planners are competitive (L2 error of 0.735\,m against 0.947\,m for UniAD's planner; a nuPlan score of 79.3 against 76.1 for the rule-based PDM-Closed on Test14-hard with reactive agents). On HUGSIM, nine of the sixteen score above UniAD's learned planner on the same perception,. The best, a hybrid A* path followed by nonlinear trajectory optimization, raises HUGSIM's driving score (HD-Score) from 32.0 to 41.8. The gain is largest with standing and oncoming cars; against cars that steer at the ego, there is none. In 31 of the 182 runs in which UniAD's planner hits a car, the best textbook planner completes the route on the same perception: these crashes are due to the planner, not the perception.

</details>


### [71] [GEMS: Generation and Experience Memory for Subgoal-Guided Long-Horizon World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25134)
*Yangchen Zhao,Ali Payani,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GEMS通过生成时间结构化的子目标并结合潜在经验记忆选择状态，利用距离门控决定预测结果与子目标还是记忆状态比较，从而提升长时程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型在短时程目标条件规划中有效，但长时程任务面临预测误差累积和潜在空间相似度对远距离目标指导有限的问题。现有方法引入子目标，但生成子目标可能不准确，记忆方法受经验覆盖限制。

Method: 提出GEMS，生成时间结构化的子目标以指导动作搜索，并从潜在经验记忆（LEM）中选择状态；距离门控决定预测结果是评估于生成子目标还是选定的记忆状态。

Result: 在六个任务及任务套件（操作、导航、到达）上验证有效性。在Cube、Push-T、Meta-World上成功率分别为92.8%、68.4%、54.1%，比最强基线分别提升24.0、7.3、22.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 生成子目标与经验记忆可共同增强基于冻结世界模型的长时程规划。

Abstract: Visual world models enable effective short-horizon goal-conditioned planning, such as reaching nearby targets and pushing objects over short distances, by predicting candidate action outcomes and comparing their representations with a goal representation. However, extending this approach to long-horizon tasks is challenging: prediction errors accumulate over extended rollouts, while latent-space similarity to distant goals provides limited guidance for ranking candidate outcomes. Existing methods address these limitations by introducing intermediate subgoals, either generated by learned models or selected from experience memory. Generated subgoals can contain hallucinations or be inaccurate in aspects such as object positions and timing. Memory-based approaches are constrained by experience coverage and may lack precise guidance for selecting appropriate intermediate states from stored observations. We introduce GEMS, which generates temporally structured subgoals to guide action search and state selection from Latent Experience Memory (LEM). A distance gate determines whether predicted outcomes are evaluated against the generated subgoal or the selected memory state. Experiments across six tasks and task suites spanning manipulation, navigation, and reaching demonstrate the effectiveness of GEMS for long-horizon planning. GEMS outperforms state-of-the-art world-model planning baselines across different manipulation settings, achieving success rates of 92.8% on Cube, 68.4% on Push-T, and 54.1% on Meta-World. It improves over the strongest evaluated baseline on these tasks by 24.0, 7.3, and 22.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate that generated subgoals and experience memory can jointly strengthen long-horizon planning with a frozen world model.

</details>


### [72] [MAWAM: A Multi-Agent World-Action Model for Coordinated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25105)
*Xianzhe Fan,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Jing Wang,Yecheng Wu,Yu-Cheng Chou,Johan Bjorck,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Jan Kautz,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Zhiding Yu,Hengshuang Zhao,Zhiqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAWAM是一个多智能体世界-动作模型，能在单次采样中联合生成每个机器人未来的视觉观测和可执行动作块，在真实机器人协作任务和仿真中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队中每个动作都会改变队友所处的环境，因此协调操作需要同时预测团队动作及其共同创造的物理未来。

Method: 基于预训练的单机器人世界-动作骨干，MAWAM通过跨智能体注意力联合去噪所有机器人的动作与未来视图token，同时为每个机器人保留独立的控制接口。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务（收集洗衣袋、折叠浴巾、递伞）上平均成功率达67.8%，比同数据同相机训练的MA-VLA高18.9个百分点；仿真中在最多四臂团队的八个任务上平均成功率达90.3%。消融显示跨智能体注意力和团队阶段未来视频监督均提升协调控制：去掉注意力后四臂接力从95.0%降至39.0%，去掉该监督后Place Food从78.0%降至47.0%。

Conclusion: 联合建模团队动作与未来视觉观测，并引入跨智能体注意力和团队阶段未来视频监督，能有效提升多机器人协调操作的成功率。

Abstract: Every move in a robot team changes the world in which its teammates must act. Coordinated manipulation therefore calls for predicting the team's actions together with the physical future they jointly create. We introduce MAWAM, a multi-agent world-action model that jointly generates each robot's future views and executable action chunks in a single sample. Built from a pretrained single-robot world-action backbone, MAWAM jointly denoises all robots' action and future-view tokens through cross-agent attention, while preserving a separate control interface for each robot. MAWAM achieves the highest success on all three real-robot tasks (laundry-bag collection, bath-towel folding, and umbrella handover), with 67.8% mean success, 18.9 percentage points above MA-VLA trained on the same demonstrations and cameras. In simulation, MAWAM reaches 90.3% mean success on eight tasks with teams of up to four arms. Ablations show that cross-agent attention and team-stage future-video supervision both improve coordinated control: without attention, a four-arm relay falls from 95.0% to 39.0%, and without this supervision, Place Food falls from 78.0% to 47.0%.

</details>


### [73] [Mapping the Wind to Find the Source: Online Physics-Informed Gas Source Localization with Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25067)
*Wanting Jin,Thomas Wiedemann,Han Fan,Achim Lilienthal,Alcherio Martinoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级物理信息概率框架，用于室内空间变化气流下的移动机器人气体源定位；在线重建风场并驱动前向扩散模型预测气体模式，更新源位置概率信念，结合信息路径规划实现高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人气体源定位面临风与气体测量稀疏、噪声大且受机器人运动影响，室内气流空间变化显著，而详细环境布局、边界条件或预计算流场常不可得，且危险源需尽快定位，穷举探索不现实。

Method: 机器人导航中持续采集风与气体测量并在线重建空间变化气流场；估计风场驱动前向扩散模型为每个候选源位置预测气体模式，与实测气体模式比较以更新源位置概率信念；考虑风场估计的空间非均匀可靠性及其对源估计的影响；利用信念图进行信息路径规划引导机器人前往信息丰富区域。

Result: 高保真仿真全面评估各组件及其在完整系统中的集成，物理室内实验评估系统在真实传感、运动与气流条件下的性能；结果表明融入轻量级物理知识显著提升空间复杂气流下的定位鲁棒性并实现更高效探索。

Conclusion: 轻量级物理信息概率框架能有效应对空间变化室内气流下的气体源定位，通过在线风场重建与概率信念更新结合信息路径规划，在仿真与真实实验中验证了鲁棒性与探索效率。

Abstract: Gas Source Localization (GSL) with mobile robots is challenging because wind and gas measurements are spatially sparse, noisy, and potentially affected by robot motion. Indoor airflow can vary substantially across space, while detailed environmental layouts, boundary conditions, or precomputed airflow fields are often unavailable. Moreover, hazardous sources must be localized as quickly as possible, making exhaustive exploration impractical. This paper presents a lightweight physics-informed probabilistic framework for efficient GSL under spatially varying indoor airflow. As the robot navigates, it continuously collects wind and gas measurements and reconstructs the spatially varying airflow field online. The estimated wind field drives a forward dispersion model that predicts the gas pattern for each candidate source location. These predictions are compared with the measured gas pattern to update a probabilistic belief over the source location. As the wind field is reconstructed incrementally, the framework also accounts for the spatially non-uniform reliability of wind field estimation and its influence on the source estimation.
