The resulting belief map is then used for informative path planning to guide the robot toward informative regions. High-fidelity simulations provide a comprehensive evaluation of the individual components and their integrations within the complete system, while physical indoor experiments assess the system performance under realistic sensing, motion, and airflow conditions. The results demonstrate that incorporating lightweight physical knowledge significantly improves localization robustness under spatially complex airflow and enables more efficient exploration.

</details>


### [74] [Inhabitant: An Agent Framework for Long-Horizon Human-Scene Interaction with Spatial Control and Quantitative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25005)
*Yao Zhang,Zhuchenyang Liu,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出训练-free智能体框架Inhabitant，通过空间控制与定量反馈修复长程人-场景交互，并构建Inhabit基准，最高任务完成率达98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程人-场景交互智能体缺乏对运动的精细空间控制和对执行结果的定量反馈，在拾取玩具并投入篮子等日常挑战场景中常无法完成交互序列。

Method: Inhabitant为无需训练的智能体框架：智能体以位置、路径和姿态指定每个运动，度量执行交互与意图的偏差，并据此修改控制规格；同时构建Inhabit基准，含公寓31种家具布局中的75个家务任务，每个任务串联1至3个可移动物体目标，由全身（不含手指）执行。

Result: 在Inhabit上，Inhabitant使用Claude Opus 5.5完成98.7%的任务，使用Claude Sonnet 5.5和Gemini 4 Argon分别完成93.3%和82.7%；消融实验表明，缺少空间控制时智能体在多数任务上失败，缺少定量反馈时运动精度下降。

Conclusion: 空间控制与定量反馈是长程人-场景交互智能体完成复杂日常任务的关键，Inhabitant与Inhabit为相关评估提供了有效框架与基准。

Abstract: Recent work on human-scene interaction (HSI) generation moves from single interactions to long-horizon interaction, where a language-model agent plans and executes a sequence of interactions in a 3D scene under one instruction. However, existing agents struggle in challenging everyday scenarios (e.g., picking up a toy and throwing it into a basket) because they lack spatial control over the motion and quantitative feedback on its execution, and they often fail to complete the interaction sequence. To address these gaps, we present Inhabitant, a training-free agent framework in which the agent specifies each motion through positions, paths and poses, measures how the executed interaction deviates from its intent, and revises the control specification accordingly. For an extensive evaluation in such scenarios, we build Inhabit, a benchmark of 75 household tasks in 31 furniture layouts of an apartment, in which every task chains one to three goals on movable objects and is carried out by the whole body, excluding fingers. On Inhabit, Inhabitant completes 98.7\% of the tasks with Claude Opus 5.5, and 93.3\% and 82.7\% with Claude Sonnet 5.5 and Gemini 4 Argon, respectively. Ablations confirm that without spatial control the agent fails most tasks, and without quantitative feedback its motion becomes less precise. Code is available on the project page.

</details>


### [75] [Reference-Free Humanoid Skateboarding via Termination-Shaped Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26054)
*Ganeshvar Soenessardien,Michael Przystupa,Kevin Sebastian Luck*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种不依赖示范数据的无模型深度强化学习框架，使K1人形机器人学会滑板运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人行走和奔跑研究进展迅速，但需要与被动外部物体协调的物体介导运动（如滑板）仍很困难；现有方法要么需要人类示范，要么依赖需要系统辨识的解析转向模型。

Method: 采用深度强化学习框架，结合任务奖励与阻断利用的终止条件，并引入多项约束（如失去踩板脚、双脚站板、过度扭转、与板分离、板运动不稳定），这些约束在训练中变化频率形成从姿态控制到持续机器人-滑板运动的隐式渐进。

Result: 机器人能够在无模型学习条件下学习滑板运动，约束的变化产生从站立控制到持续机板协同运动的渐进过程。

Conclusion: 无模型深度强化学习结合任务奖励与阻断利用终止条件，可在无先验数据情况下实现人形机器人滑板运动，为人形机器人物体介导运动提供可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion research has advanced rapidly on walking and running, but \emph {object-mediated} locomotion, in which a robot must coordinate its body with a passive external object, remains difficult. One challenging task is skateboarding, which requires simultaneous interaction with the ground and a passive but movable platform to achieve forward motion. Current methods for tackling skateboarding with humanoid robots either require human demonstrations or utilise analytic steering models that require system identification. In this paper, we introduce a deep reinforcement learning framework that does not rely on prior data such as demonstrations and enables robots such as the K1 humanoid to learn to skateboard using model-free learning. The method combines task rewards with exploit-blocking terminations. We introduce a number of constraints restricting behaviours such as losing the planted board foot, standing with both feet on the board, excessive twisting, separation from the board, and unstable board motion. Their changing prevalence during training produces an implicit progression from stance control towards sustained robot--board locomotion.

</details>


### [76] [From Self-Play RL to Constrained Control: Distilling DiffMPC Students with DAgger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25197)
*Luca M. Hartmann,Parinaz Naghizadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将PPO教师策略的动作头替换为带约束的DiffMPC，并用DAgger在自博弈模拟器中蒸馏，可在少量训练时间内为学生策略加入显式约束并提升泛化与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈强化学习得到的驾驶策略难以验证或解释，而DiffMPC能施加显式约束但通常依赖模仿日志数据或昂贵RL训练；目标是给已训练策略加约束并保持性能。

Method: 保留PPO教师的主干网络，将其动作头替换为强制执行道路边界半平面与智能体椭圆约束的DiffMPC，再在教师的自博弈模拟器中使用DAgger蒸馏学生策略。

Result: 在9个随机种子下，较小教师对应的约束学生策略在nuPlan上与教师持平，在CARLA和分布外Waymo场景上优于教师；较强教师对应的学生在nuPlan和Waymo上失败更少，在CARLA上持平；蒸馏时间不超过教师训练时间的30%。

Conclusion: 该方法能以较低训练成本为已训练自博弈策略注入显式约束，并在多数据集上保持或提升性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning (RL) produces strong driving policies, but these policies are hard to verify or interpret. Differentiable model predictive control (DiffMPC) can enforce explicit constraints, but it is usually trained by imitating logged data or with costly RL. To add constraints to an already trained policy, we keep a PPO teacher's backbone and replace its action head with a DiffMPC that enforces road boundary half-planes and agent ellipses. We then distill the student with DAgger in the teacher's self-play simulator. Over 9 seeds, the constrained student of a smaller teacher matches its teacher on nuPlan and beats it on CARLA and on out-of-distribution Waymo scenes. For a stronger teacher, the student has fewer failures on nuPlan and Waymo, while staying level on CARLA. Its distillation takes no more than 30% of the teacher's training time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [FLUID: Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26491)
*Yunqi Zhao,Run Peng,Ze Dong,Ryan Lu,Yinpei Dai,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLUID是一个用于多模态任务引导中灵活交互的建模、评估与实现的统一框架，利用自然人类引导数据将干预基于证据、必要性和时机灵活性，并构建实时引导智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将合成对话视为固定目标，忽略了实时任务引导的灵活性——何时说、说什么可有多种合适选择，有时沉默最佳。

Method: 基于WTaG和HoloAssist的自然人-人引导数据，FLUID将干预与其支持证据、必要性及时机灵活性关联，并利用该表示评估灵活引导并构建实时任务引导智能体。

Result: 该智能体在响应正确性、干预精确性、及时引导和响应延迟方面取得最强整体平衡，但在避免语音重叠方面仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: FLUID推动情境化任务引导从僵化的参考匹配走向更自然、自适应的自然人-AI交互。

Abstract: Real-time task guidance is inherently flexible: when an agent guides a user through a task, multiple responses and timings can be appropriate, and sometimes the best action is to remain silent. Existing benchmarks often miss this flexibility by treating synthesized dialogues as fixed targets for what to say and when to say it. We introduce FLUID (Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance), a unified framework for modeling, evaluating, and enabling flexible interaction in multimodal task guidance. Using naturally occurring human-human guidance from WTaG and HoloAssist, FLUID grounds interventions in their supporting evidence, necessity, and timing flexibility, and uses this representation both to evaluate flexible guidance and to build a real-time agent for task guidance. Our agent achieves the strongest overall balance of response correctness, intervention precision, timely guidance, and response latency among evaluated systems, while still leaving room for improvement in avoiding speech overlap. Together, FLUID moves situated task guidance beyond rigid reference matching toward more natural and adaptive human-AI interaction. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [ZeroVLN: Learning to Navigate without Supervised Fine-Tuning of the VLM Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26414)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Guli Zhang,Sunzhu Li,Hongyan Xie,Zehao Chen,Fuzhen Zhuang,Kaixuan Wang,Xiaozhi Chen,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroVLN 提出慢-快导航框架，无需对视觉语言模型大脑做导航域监督微调，而是通过语义工具调用接口将运动决策传给动作专家，并用闭环强化学习联合优化，在 VLN-CE 基准上超越基于 SFT 的分层方法且基本保留通用视觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法将视觉语言模型大脑与可学习动作头耦合，并在导航数据上微调，导致基础 VLM 权重被显著改变、通用视觉能力退化；作者希望在不做导航域 SFT 的前提下实现有效导航。

Method: 提出 ZeroVLN 慢-快框架：为 VLM 大脑配备语义工具调用接口，用自然语言把运动决策传给动作专家；先让动作专家学会执行这些运动请求，再通过闭环强化学习联合改进大脑的运动决策与动作专家的连续执行。

Result: 在相同导航回合上训练后，ZeroVLN 在标准 VLN-CE 基准上显著优于代表性的基于 SFT 的分层 VLN 方法，同时仅对大脑权重造成微小改动并大体保留其通用能力。

Conclusion: ZeroVLN 是首个对 VLM 大脑零导航域 SFT 的慢-快 VLN 框架，也是首个在 VLN-CE 中通过闭环智能体强化学习联合优化慢、快策略的工作。

Abstract: Representative vision-language navigation (VLN) methods typically couple a vision-language model (VLM) brain with a learnable action head and fine-tune the VLM brain on navigation-domain data to predict geometric goals. However, such adaptation can substantially alter base VLM weights and degrade general visual capabilities. We introduce ZeroVLN, a slow-fast navigation framework that achieves effective navigation with zero navigation-domain SFT of the VLM brain. Rather than teaching the brain to predict geometric goals, we equip it with a semantic tool-calling interface for conveying movement decisions to an action expert in natural language. We first adapt the action expert to carry out these movement requests, then jointly improve the brain's movement decisions and the action expert's continuous execution through closed-loop reinforcement learning (RL). Trained on the same navigation episodes, ZeroVLN substantially outperforms representative SFT-based hierarchical VLN methods on standard VLN-CE (continuous environments) benchmarks, while inducing only minor changes to the brain's weights and largely preserving its general capabilities. To our knowledge, ZeroVLN is the first slow-fast VLN framework with zero navigation-domain SFT of the VLM brain, and the first to jointly optimize its slow and fast policies through closed-loop agentic RL in VLN-CE.

</details>


### [79] [World Models Favor Learned Priors over Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26366)
*Han Xiao,Zhiyong Bao,Xiaotong Liu,Yanhao Chen,Qingqiang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出视觉校准任务与TailWorld基准，并设计RIFT方法从短时交互中推断物理规则，以提升跨环境且长尾分布下的运动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为每个环境单独训练世界模型难以扩展；跨环境预测需识别当前物理规则，而外观不足以揭示规则；不同物理条件的数据量差异导致长尾训练分布，使模型偏向常见规则。

Method: 构建TailWorld基准，独立控制训练不平衡与校准信息量，用同一初始状态的两条参考未来（演示规则与常见规则）衡量预测偏置；提出Rule Inference for Transfer（RIFT），学习规则对物体运动的影响、从校准交互推断当前规则并应用于新初始状态的预测，显式控制训练频率对规则推断的影响。

Result: 在TailWorld上，RIFT在不同训练不平衡和分布偏移下提升预测准确性与物理一致性，尤其在稀有动态预测上取得增益。

Conclusion: 视觉校准可作为跨环境世界模型的有效途径，RIFT能在长尾分布下利用充分信息支持稀有规则，提升跨初始状态的规则迁移预测性能。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used to simulate physical interactions, but training a separate model for each environment limits scalability. Cross-environment prediction requires identifying the current physical rule, which appearance alone may not reveal. We therefore study visual calibration: using object motion and interactions in a short video as direct evidence to infer the current rule and predict motion under that rule from a different initial state. However, differences in data availability across physical conditions can yield a long-tailed training distribution, biasing models toward common rules even when evidence supports rare ones. To study this challenge, we introduce TailWorld, which evaluates the transfer of demonstrated rules across initial states while independently controlling training imbalance and calibration informativeness. The benchmark compares predictions with two reference futures from the same prediction initial state, one following the demonstrated rule and the other a common rule, to measure prediction bias toward common dynamics. We further propose Rule Inference for Transfer (RIFT), which learns how different rules affect object motion, infers the current rule from calibration interactions, and applies it to prediction from a new initial state. RIFT explicitly controls the influence of training frequency on rule inference, allowing sufficiently informative observational evidence to support rare rules. Experiments on TailWorld show that RIFT improves prediction accuracy and physical consistency across varying training imbalances and distribution shifts, with gains in predicting rare dynamics.

</details>


### [80] [Actor-Predictor Architecture for Reinforcement Learning with Formal Specification Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26313)
*Josef Brozovich,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Actor-Predictor框架，不显式构造自动机，而是学习逻辑进度的代理表示来满足时序安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将逻辑公式转换为自动机进行状态增强和奖励塑形，但假设自动机构造可行且可处理，限制了其对小型规格和线性时序逻辑（LTL）的适用性。

Method: Actor-Predictor通过预测器持续估计部分轨迹未来是否满足形式规格，将学习到的特征作为演员的辅助输入，并用GRPO和RWR优化演员；理论上证明在足够预测精度下学习表示等价于自动机状态。

Result: 在网格世界和Isaac Lab机器人任务上，Actor-Predictor在LTL约束任务上优于基于自动机的基线，并在非正则形式规格（如(a^n b^n)）上表现良好。

Conclusion: Actor-Predictor无需显式构造自动机即可学习逻辑进度表示，扩展了强化学习处理形式规格的适用范围，尤其适用于自动机方法不可行的非正则规格。

Abstract: One fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning (RL) is enforcing temporal safety constraints, such as satisfying formal specifications. Existing methods often transform logic formulas into automata for state augmentation and reward shaping. However, this approach assumes that automaton construction is both feasible and tractable, which limits its applicability to small specifications and linear temporal logic (LTL). This paper proposes Actor-Predictor, a framework that learns a surrogate representation of logic progress without explicitly constructing an automaton. The predictor continuously estimates whether a partial trajectory will satisfy the formal specification in a later step, and its learned features are provided as auxiliary inputs to the actor, which is then optimized using GRPO and RWR. We show theoretically that, under sufficient predictive accuracy, the learned representation is equivalent to the automaton state. Experiments on grid world and robotic tasks in Isaac Lab demonstrate that Actor-Predictor outperforms automaton-based baselines on LTL-constrained tasks. It also performs well on non-regular formal specifications, such as $(a^n b^n)$, for which finite-automaton-based approaches are not applicable.

</details>


### [81] [SenseAlign: Learning Decision-Aligned Predictive Representations for Active Urban Sensing with UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26109)
*Weijiang Xiong,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SenseAlign 是一种基于强化学习的框架，通过训练特权教师和带预测辅助分支的学生，学习决策对齐的预测表示，用于协调城市传感无人机队进行主动感知，在巴塞罗那真实路网实验上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动城市感知需同时理解复杂时空演化并在部分观测下决策，而现有工作很少联合考虑动态交通状态下的感知效用。

Method: 提出 SenseAlign：先训练利用未来真实交通的特权教师；学生除 RL 的 actor/critic 外增加辅助分支从部分观测预测未来交通；训练时对齐教师与学生在真实及学生预测未来下的动作分布，塑造面向决策的潜空间；推理时学生无额外开销。

Result: 在巴塞罗那市中心含 1570 条路段的真实路网上进行实验，在多种效用公式下均一致优于基线。

Conclusion: 面向决策对齐的预测表示学习可有效支持大规模动态路网中的主动感知与无人机队协调。

Abstract: Active urban sensing orchestrates a mobile sensor fleet to proactively inspect a dynamic road network and gather critical information for system management. This problem simultaneously requires understanding the complex spatio-temporal evolution and making decisions with partial observations. In this work, we focus on active sensing in a large-scale urban road network with utilities grounded in dynamic traffic states, while prior work has rarely considered these factors jointly. We propose \emph{SenseAlign}, a Reinforcement Learning-based (RL) framework that learns decision-aligned predictive representations for coordinating an urban sensing drone fleet. SenseAlign first trains a privileged teacher agent that exploits future ground-truth traffic for optimal sensing choices. The student agent has an auxiliary branch that learns to predict future traffic with partial observations, in addition to the RL-trained actor and critic branches. During student learning, our framework matches the action distributions of the teacher under ground-truth and student-predicted future traffic, while keeping the teacher's observations unchanged. Such a design shapes the latent space for decision-making instead of prediction alone. At inference time, the student agent has no overhead as all this learning is done in training. We conduct comprehensive experiments with the real road network of central Barcelona containing 1570 road segments, and demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines on multiple utility formulations. The codebase will be made publicly available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [82] [DMT-Attack: Diffusion-Prior Texture Search for Closed-Loop Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25695)
*Jun Xia,Shangwen Wang,Jun Ma,Ruichao Ren,Zhouyang Jia,Xin Zhao,Huijun Liu,Jie Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DMT-Attack，一种无梯度灰盒攻击，利用扩散模型生成自然外观纹理，通过闭环反馈筛选可破坏VLA策略的候选，在LIBERO上实现更高攻击成功率且更隐蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略依赖全程视觉反馈，易受持续纹理攻击；但优化出的图案易与周围环境区分，使攻击载体易被察觉、检查或移除。

Method: 基于扩散模型的结构化图像分布搜索自然纹理，冻结的文本条件扩散模型定义搜索空间，八专家潜变量提议网络由评估的教师潜变量初始化并用rollout反馈精炼，构成无梯度灰盒攻击。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件上对pi_0.5和OpenVLA-OFT评估，宏平均干净条件攻击成功率57.9%（UPA-RFAS为44.7%，随机自然纹理为19.7%），场景相似度更高，干净校准检测器标记率45%（优化补丁基线为97.5-100%）。

Conclusion: 自然外观纹理可破坏VLA视觉反馈与闭环决策，需同时评估视觉外观与任务行为的防御方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely on visual feedback throughout manipulation, making them vulnerable to persistent texture attacks. However, optimized patterns can stand out from their surroundings, making an attack easier to notice and its carrier more likely to be inspected or removed. To address this limitation, our key insight is to search the structured image distribution of a diffusion model for natural-looking textures while using closed-loop feedback to identify task-disruptive candidates. Based on this idea, we propose DMT-Attack, a gradient-free gray-box attack that generates textures for ordinary scene objects such as tablecloths. A frozen text-conditioned diffusion model defines the search space, and an eight-expert latent proposal network is initialized from evaluated teacher latents and refined using rollout feedback. We evaluate DMT-Attack against pi_0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT across four LIBERO suites. DMT-Attack achieves a macro clean-conditioned attack success rate (cASR) of 57.9%, compared with 44.7% for UPA-RFAS and 19.7% for random natural textures. It also attains higher scene similarity and a lower clean-calibrated detector flag rate (45% versus 97.5-100% for optimized patch baselines). These results demonstrate that natural-looking textures can disrupt VLA visual feedback and closed-loop decision-making, motivating defenses that jointly assess visual appearance and task behavior.

</details>


### [83] [LATER: Learning Action-Conditioned Temporal Evidence Retrieval for Predictive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25671)
*Zihan Zhou,Wenxi Xu,Qilin Wang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LATER是一个基于CF-JEPA的动作条件世界模型，通过分离记忆存储与选择性读取来保留早期物理证据，在受干扰任务中显著提升终端成功率和物理读出精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期交互揭示的物理属性在后续观测中可能消失，但仍影响未来动作效果；受工作记忆与认知科学中保持与选择性读取区分的启发，需设计能存储并查询早期证据的世界模型。

Method: 提出LATER，基于CF-JEPA构建动作条件世界模型：将完整真实转移存入分区记忆并在候选推演时保持固定；响应桥用当前状态和候选动作查询记忆；多切辅助监督训练记忆写入器；并采用受干扰的两阶段PushT及六模型两随机种子对比和受控历史干预实验。

Result: 在受干扰Two-Stage PushT上终端成功率从15.5%提升至25.0%；六模型两随机种子对比中，LATER在干净和受干扰视图下均取得最低平均物理读出误差，受干扰下沿推方向MAE较CF-JEPA降低20.2%；受控历史干预表明预测依赖早期物理证据，且推阶段影响大于等待阶段。

Conclusion: 分离记忆保持与选择性读取能有效利用早期物理证据，提升世界模型在干扰环境下的动作效果预测与任务成功率。

Abstract: Early interactions can reveal physical properties that later observations no longer show. These properties can still gov- ern the effects of future actions. Inspired by the distinction between retention and selective readout in working mem- ory and cognitive science, we introduce LATER, an action- conditioned world model built on CF-JEPA. LATER stores completed real transitions in partitioned memories that remain fixed during candidate rollouts. A response bridge queries these memories using the current state and candidate action, while multi-cut auxiliary supervision trains the memory writ- ers. On distracted Two-Stage PushT, LATER raises terminal success from 15.5% to 25.0% over reconstructed CF-JEPA. In a separate comparison across six models and two scratch- training seeds, LATER achieves the lowest mean physical readout error in both clean and distracted views, reducing along-push mean absolute error (MAE) under distractors by 20.2% relative to CF-JEPA. Controlled history interventions show that later predictions depend on earlier physical evi- dence even when query observations and actions are fixed, with larger effects during pushing than waiting.

</details>


### [84] [Don't Lose the Future: Latent-Aware 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25614)
*Yichen Guo,Yanlong Chen,Xinyue Zhang,Mohan Liu,Song Zhang,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LAQ，一种面向世界动作模型的潜在感知4比特量化框架，通过保护预测未来信息的保真度，在W4A4KV4下接近16比特模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs的4比特量化器只保护生成的动作，逐步估计去噪敏感度，但无法保持预测未来的保真度；动作误差主要通过退化的未来信息引入而未被专门保护。

Method: LAQ在预测未来的写入、存储和读取环节进行保护：引入reader metric衡量未来误差对动作的影响，以及read count按读取该舍入误差的去噪步数加权，由此得到激活和缓存精度分配的闭式解；并提出gaze-guided refinement，在去噪循环和缓存中对动作关注的token进行第二次4比特处理，所有权重保持4比特，无高精度分支和逐任务重校准。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上，Fast-WAM和Cosmos-Policy平均成功率分别达76.4%和83.3%，与16比特模型差距在1.2个百分点内，且均优于使用16比特KV缓存的基线；模型压缩3.8倍，整个策略峰值GPU内存降低2.3倍，运行速度比16比特模型快1.66倍；在真实AgileX PiPER机械臂三个任务上平均成功率47.0%，16比特模型为52.0%，比QuaRot高30.0%。

Conclusion: LAQ通过保护预测未来信息的保真度，实现了高压缩、低内存、低延迟的4比特WAMs部署，精度接近16比特模型并显著优于现有量化基线。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict how a scene will evolve and condition their actions on this predicted future. Deploying them on a robot's onboard computer calls for 4-bit weights, activations and key-value caches (W4A4KV4). Existing 4-bit quantizers for WAMs preserve the generated action, and estimate sensitivity one denoising step at a time. However, they fail to preserve the fidelity of the predicted future: once the action pathway is protected, the remaining action error enters mostly through a degraded future that none of them deliberately protects. We propose LAQ, a latent-aware quantization framework that preserves the predicted future where it is written, stored and read. Specifically, we introduce a reader metric, which measures future error by its effect on the action, and a read count, which weights each rounding error by the number of denoising steps that read it. Together they give the allocation of precision over activations and caches a closed-form solution. We further develop gaze-guided refinement, which gives the tokens the action attends to a second 4-bit pass in the denoising loop and in the cache. All weights stay at 4 bits, with no high-precision branch and no per-task recalibration. On the LIBERO-Plus benchmark, LAQ reaches 76.4% and 83.3% average success on Fast-WAM and Cosmos-Policy, within 1.2 percentage points of the 16-bit models and all above the baselines with 16-bit KV caches. It compresses the model by 3.8x, reduces the peak GPU memory of the whole policy by 2.3x, and runs 1.66x faster than the 16-bit model. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, it reaches 47.0% average success over three tasks, against 52.0% for the 16-bit model and 30.0% higher than QuaRot.

</details>


### [85] [AeroBelief: Budgeted First-Action Verification for Evidence-Guided Aerial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25568)
*Tianren Li,Dongbo Li,Hongkai Chen,Jie Liu,Guoliang Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AeroBelief 以“下一个可执行动作”为验证单元，将不确定的空中上下文与当前观测结合，在预测、移动与图像处理的预算约束下选择动作和调度感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中感知需要同时处理不确定的俯视上下文、当前观测以及预测/移动/图像处理的有限预算，现有穷举式世界模型打分代价过高，需在保证任务完成率的同时大幅降低参考候选评估与图像处理开销。

Method: AeroBelief 按首个动作对候选视角分组，用低成本提议器扫描，并有选择地查询结构化世界模型；采用校准的分组边界支持严格承诺，六查询控制器将选择性预测与信念更新、接受检查及计划感知相连；同时在主机端流水线中对 2,685 张 20MP UAV 图像使用上下文引导的准入与反馈机制。

Result: 在 4 类布局、40 个世界、960 次闭环执行中，AeroBelief 完成任务率 75.42%，高于穷举 WM 打分的 73.33% 和视线几何的 65.83%，且参考候选评估减少 91.81%；六查询固定参考回放中，提议器缩放锚定边界将首动作证书可用率从 36.25% 提升至 54.69%，每状态平均查询减少 13.54%；图像流水线保留全部 8 个独立支持的目标确认，处理图像减少 76.05%，累计主动处理服务减少 70.02%，反馈相对静态上下文再省 5.17% 图像。

Conclusion: 该研究将预算约束下的动作选择与证据驱动的飞行图像处理相连接，在显著降低计算与图像处理成本的同时保持或提升任务完成率与目标确认能力。

Abstract: Aerial sensing must combine uncertain overhead context with current observations while budgeting prediction, movement and image processing. AeroBelief uses the next executable action as the unit of verification against reference scores. It groups candidate views by first action, scans them with an inexpensive proposer, and selectively queries a structured world model (WM). Calibrated group bounds support strict commitment; a six-query controller connects selective prediction with belief updates, acceptance checks and scheduled sensing. Across four layout families, 40 worlds and 960 closed-loop executions, the AeroBelief controller completes 75.42% of tasks, compared with 73.33% for exhaustive WM scoring and 65.83% for line-of-sight geometry, using 91.81% fewer reference-candidate evaluations than exhaustive scoring. In fixed-reference replay with a six-query cap, proposer-scaled anchored bounds raised first-action certificate availability from 36.25% to 54.69% and reduced mean queries per state by 13.54% relative to unit-scale anchored bounds. A complete ground-hosted pipeline processes 2,685 self-collected 20-megapixel unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) images from seven DJI Mavic 3M routes. Context-guided admission and feedback retain all eight independently supported target confirmations with 76.05% fewer processed images and 70.02% less accumulated active processing service than uncapped all-frame processing; feedback saves another 5.17% of images relative to static context. The studies connect budgeted action selection with evidence-driven processing of recorded flight imagery.

</details>


### [86] [Efficient Multimodal Agents for Auditing 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25543)
*Jingbo Yang,Ziyan Jiang,Li An,Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Tommi Jaakkola,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种混合审计器：小型视觉语言模型群负责导航与触发故障，前沿模型仅负责识别，在WorldAuditBench上以更低成本超越现有审计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体构建的3D世界常含悬浮物、可穿墙等故障，自动审计需同时准确且廉价，但现有审计器要么准确但慢且贵，要么廉价但不可靠。

Method: 将审计分解为导航、触发、识别三项能力；用经贝叶斯搜索引导的前沿教师模型验证轨迹，端到端微调4B视觉语言模型学习导航与触发，六个探索器组成群体；识别环节交由前沿模型审查小模型记录并报告。

Result: 在Unreal Engine 5和该http URL世界的213个任务上成功率达55.9%，比最强基线高13.6个百分点，成本不到其四分之一；与前沿模型单独探索相比成本低4.6倍、延迟约减半；无需额外训练即可回答真实扫描的具身问题，匹配前沿模型并超其他四个前沿模型6.4至16.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 小型模型可承担审计中的导航与触发，识别仍需前沿模型；该混合群体审计方案在准确性与成本间取得更优平衡，并具零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Multimodal agents can now build interactive 3D worlds and games, yet these worlds, like those built by people, often contain glitches such as floating objects, walls that can be walked through, or objects that change when no one is looking. An automatic auditor could check such worlds at scale and provide rewards for agents that build them, but only if it is both accurate and cheap. Auditing requires three abilities, namely navigating to the objects that may contain a glitch, triggering glitches that appear only after a specific behavior, and recognizing what is wrong. Current auditors are either accurate but slow and expensive or cheap but unreliable. We study how much of the audit small models can take on. A 4B vision language model fine-tuned end to end on verified trajectories of a frontier teacher guided by Bayesian search learns to navigate and trigger, and six such explorers together match a frontier model on both, but recognition stays far behind. We therefore build an auditor in which a swarm of small explorers navigates and triggers, and a frontier model reviews what they record and report. On WorldAuditBench, our auditor succeeds on 55.9% of 213 tasks in Unreal Engine 5 and this http URL worlds, 13.6 points above the strongest auditor reported, at less than a quarter of its cost. It matches the frontier reviewer exploring on its own at 4.6 times lower cost and about half the latency. Without further training, it also answers embodied questions about real-world scans, matching the frontier reviewer exploring on its own and surpassing four other frontier models by 6.4 to 16.6 points.

</details>


### [87] [LoongForge-Embodied: A Unified Framework for Training Heterogeneous Embodied Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25460)
*LoongForge Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoongForge-Embodied 是一个原生 PyTorch 框架，统一并加速异构具身模型（VLA 与 WAM）的训练，在代表性模型上实现 1.47x-4.38x 端到端训练吞吐提升和 4.1x-23.2x 评估加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型和世界-动作模型（WAMs）计算代价高昂，且异构架构与碎片化训练栈导致系统优化难以跨模型复用，亟需统一且高效的训练基础设施。

Method: 提出公共抽象层将模型特定实现与共享训练基础设施解耦，实现跨模型复用而不约束架构；结合可复用加速机制与模型特定、硬件感知的优化策略，覆盖计算、执行、内存、数据 I/O 和分布式通信（含 DDP 与 FSDP 的 FP8 通信优化）；集成统一数据基础设施与从自我中心数据合成机器人数据的流水线，以及支持并发与模拟器优化计算的高效评估框架。

Result: 在代表性 VLA 和 WAM 模型上，端到端训练吞吐相比对应开源基线提升 1.47x-4.38x，相同协议下评估速度提升 4.1x-23.2x。

Conclusion: LoongForge-Embodied 通过统一抽象与多层次优化，有效解决了异构具身模型训练系统优化复用难的问题，显著提升了训练与评估效率。

Abstract: Training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and World-Action Models (WAMs) is computationally expensive, while their heterogeneous architectures and fragmented training stacks make system optimizations difficult to reuse across models. To address these challenges, we present LoongForge-Embodied, a native-PyTorch framework that unifies and accelerates the training of heterogeneous embodied models. At its core, LoongForge-Embodied introduces a common abstraction layer that decouples model-specific implementations from shared training infrastructure, enabling cross-model reuse without constraining individual architectures or execution requirements. Buildingon this unified infrastructure, the framework combines reusable acceleration mechanisms with model-specific and hardware-aware optimization strategies across computation, execution, memory, data I/O, and distributed communication, including FP8-based communication optimizations for DDP and FSDP. Beyond training acceleration, LoongForge-Embodied integrates a unified data infrastructure with a pipeline for synthesizing robot data from egocentric data, and an efficient evaluation framework that supports concurrency and optimized computation in simulator. Experiments across representative VLA and WAM models demonstrate 1.47x-4.38x end-to-end training throughput improvements over their corresponding open-source baselines, as well as 4.1x-23.2x faster evaluation under the same protocols.

</details>


### [88] [Controlling the Humanoid Robot H1-2 With a Simulated Biologically Plausible Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26074)
*Fatima Atwi,Constant Roux,Christian Bergaud,Olivier Stasse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出生物启发脉冲神经网络，通过不同学习方案训练并测试于Unitree H1-2人形机器人，发现e-prop达到100%存活率与BPTT持平，而固定储备池和奖励调制STDP分别仅约43.7%和11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络能模拟生物神经培养物活动，但其能否同时保持生物合理性与鲁棒性能来训练人形机器人运动仍是未解问题。

Method: 设计生物启发脉冲网络，比较三种递归权重训练方案：固定储备池（仅训练线性读出）、奖励调制STDP与资格迹传播（e-prop）、以及非生物基线BPTT；所有方案均基于强化学习专家策略蒸馏与数据集聚合，并在H1-2 Unitree人形机器人上测试。

Result: e-prop达到100%存活率，与BPTT相当；固定储备池和奖励调制STDP虽最接近生物培养物，但性能大幅停滞，存活率分别约为43.7%和11%；并进一步分析了真实神经培养物目前能或不能产生的活动趋势，最终在全尺寸H1-2人形机器人上实现了短暂双足行走。

Conclusion: e-prop可在保持一定生物合理性的同时实现与BPTT相当的人形运动性能，而更贴近生物培养物的方案目前性能受限，表明生物合理性与鲁棒性能之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Artificial Spiking Neural Networks have gained remarkable attention in the last few years due to their proven ability in simulating biological neural cultures and producing similar activity and trends. Nevertheless, whether they can be trained for humanoid locomotion while retaining biological plausibility and robust performance at once remains an open question. We study this problem by proposing a biologically inspired spiking network, trained by different learning schemes and tested on the H1-2 Unitree Humanoid. These schemes differ in how recurrent weights are trained. Ordered from most to least biologically faithful, they are: a fixed reservoir mimicking an in-vitro neural culture, where recurrent weights are kept frozen and only a linear readout is trained, the same network but with its synapses trained by reward-modulated spike timing dependent plasticity and eligibility propagation, and finally backpropagation through time as a non biological baseline for comparison. All schemes relied on distillation of a reinforcement learning expert policy with dataset aggregation. We show that e-prop reached 100% survival rate matching backpropagation through time, whereas both the fixed reservoir and the reward-modulated STDP, though the closest to biological cultures, plateaued at a much lower performance (~43.7% and ~11% survival). We explore in further detail which trends a real culture could and could not produce until now. Finally we demonstrate brief bipedal stepping on a full size humanoid H1-2.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Neural Conditional Gaussian Model: Efficient Data Assimilation with Sequential Uncertainty Quantification for Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26463)
*Chuanqi Chen,Nan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经条件高斯模型（Neural CGM），将未观测物理状态映射到隐空间并令其服从条件高斯动力学，从而在隐空间实现闭式数据同化更新，兼顾非线性表示能力与在线同化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性数据同化需反复估计隐状态及其不确定性，传统非线性滤波计算代价高且依赖显式动力学模型，因此需要一种数据驱动、低成本且能同时给出状态与不确定性的方法。

Method: 构建编码器—解码器结构：编码器将物理状态映射到隐空间，隐状态服从条件高斯动力学以支持闭式同化更新；高斯解码器将隐后验均值与协方差通过局部矩传播与学习到的非线性修正映射回物理空间，物理空间后验统计量同时进入高斯负对数似然训练目标。

Result: 在基准非线性动力系统上的数值实验表明，该方法能准确进行状态估计与预测，且相对传统非线性数据同化方法显著降低在线同化计算成本。

Conclusion: Neural CGM适用于仅部分观测条件下的重复状态估计、不确定性量化与预测，并可在无需显式控制方程的情况下用于状态预测与仿真。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) in partially observed nonlinear systems requires repeated estimation of both hidden states and their uncertainty. Conventional nonlinear filtering can be computationally expensive and often relies on an explicit dynamical model. In this paper, we develop the Neural Conditional Gaussian Model (Neural CGM), a data-driven dynamical model that maps unobserved physical states into a learned latent space, where the dynamics of the latent states follow a conditional Gaussian structure. This structure allows closed-form DA updates in the latent space, while nonlinear encoder-decoder mappings retain the flexibility needed to represent complex dynamics in physical space. A key feature of Neural CGM is its treatment of uncertainty in DA. The latent posterior mean and covariance are mapped to the physical variables through a Gaussian decoder that combines local moment propagation with learned nonlinear corrections. The resulting physical-space posterior statistics depend on the sequentially updated latent distribution rather than on a separate post-processing estimate. The same statistics enter a Gaussian negative log-likelihood during training, so both the posterior mean and uncertainty affect the learned model. Because Neural CGM learns the dynamical model itself, it can also be used for state forecasting and simulation without explicit knowledge of the governing equations. Numerical experiments on benchmark nonlinear dynamical systems show accurate state estimation and forecasting while substantially reducing online assimilation cost relative to conventional nonlinear DA methods. Neural CGM is therefore suited to repeated state estimation, uncertainty quantification, and prediction from partial observations.

</details>


### [90] [Policy Improvement-Guided Replay for Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25989)
*Qin-Wen Luo,Haoxuan Gao,Sheng Ren,Xiang Chen,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从策略改进视角研究视觉语言模型的持续强化学习，提出对比似然回放（CoLiR）方法，通过对比正确与错误回答的似然变化来选择历史提示进行回放，在MRCL基准上取得最高平均最终准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习侧重稳定性与知识保留，本文希望从强化学习策略改进原则出发，利用历史任务上的优势来保留已获得的性能增益，并解决有限回放内存的分配问题。

Method: 利用性能差异引理刻画历史性能变化为当前策略在历史任务分布上的期望优势，提出CoLiR：对最终任务策略在正确回答上的似然增益与初始策略在错误回答上的似然下降进行对比打分，在任务边界选择提示并后续均匀回放，KL正则仅作用于回放提示；内存构建仅需少量跨策略打分，无需额外生成回答。

Result: 在MRCL上，三个Qwen3-VL模型规模下，CoLiR在对比方法中取得最高平均最终准确率和平均任务准确率，同时保持有竞争力的新任务表现。

Conclusion: 将持续强化学习重新表述为策略改进并据此设计回放选择策略是有效的，CoLiR在有限内存下兼顾了历史性能保持与新任务学习。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) requires models to acquire new capabilities while retaining performance on previous tasks. A stability-oriented perspective common in conventional continual learning emphasizes knowledge preservation and interference mitigation. Complementing this perspective, we revisit CRL for vision-language models through policy improvement, a fundamental principle of RL, to preserve acquired performance gains. Using the performance difference lemma, we characterize historical performance changes through the current policy's expected advantages on historical task distributions, motivating replay of historical prompts with the current policy. To allocate limited replay memory, we propose Contrastive Likelihood Replay (CoLiR), which contrasts likelihood gains on correct responses from the final task policy with likelihood reductions on incorrect responses from the initial policy. Our analysis links correct-response likelihood gains to reference-policy advantages under an idealized KL-regularized optimal update and provides an endogenous-reward interpretation for general policy updates. CoLiR estimates scores from finite training rollouts and cached log-likelihoods, selects prompts at task boundaries, and replays them uniformly during subsequent training, with KL regularization restricted to replayed prompts. Memory construction only requires a small amount of additional cross-policy scoring but no additional response generation. Across three Qwen3-VL model sizes on MRCL, CoLiR achieves the highest mean final accuracy and mean task accuracy among the compared methods while maintaining competitive new-task performance.

</details>


### [91] [Bradley-Terry Training Can Distort Outcome Probabilities of Initially Accurate Language World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25594)
*Xinze Shi,Litian Zhang,Zhenjie Su,Zelong Yu,Yuting Zhang,Qihua Feng,Zifeng Kang,Chaozhuo Li,Zhengxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在Bradley-Terry目标下用比较反馈训练语言世界模型，会把概率质量转移到任何已枚举分支都不产生的结果上，从而扭曲真实结果概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较反馈是训练语言世界模型的一种实用方法，但作者要检验：以Bradley-Terry目标拟合这种反馈时，它是否真的能学到真实结果概率，还是会把原本准确的结果分布带偏。

Method: 在模拟机器人推动任务中，枚举结果分布；用Bradley-Terry目标拟合比较反馈，并在训练前计算该目标的最优解；检验概率转移在未见条件、五种候选律和三倍稠密枚举下是否稳健；另用一个已知评判规则的似然模型作对照；再审计读出、响应与决策，追踪从概率到动作的影响。

Result: 该目标把未见布局上原本准确（风险误差2.4个百分点）的模型约33至43个百分点的概率转移到任何已枚举分支都不产生的结果上；训练前计算的目标最优解能预测模型最终去向；知道评判规则的似然模型在同一批比较上学习，没有出现这种转移；该转移在未见条件、五种候选律和三倍稠密枚举下反复出现；去掉物理不可能事件后仍约29个百分点；存在这些事件时，风险概率被抬高，沿用反馈前模型的阈值会因误拒使每个决策损失约3个百分点效用（训练条件）和1至2个百分点（未见条件）；没有这些事件时，六个种子上效用损失平均低于1个百分点，但动作选择仍有小幅遗憾。

Conclusion: 比较反馈本身不是问题，问题在于Bradley-Terry目标：其最优解会把概率推向枚举分支不产生的结果，并可能损害决策效用；用与评判规则一致的似然目标可以在相同比较数据上避免这种偏移。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, in which a judge says which of two candidate outcomes is closer to what happened, is a practical way to train language world models that assign probabilities to an action's outcomes. We show that, fitted with a Bradley-Terry objective, it need not teach the true outcome probabilities and can distort accurate ones. On a simulated robot push with enumerated outcome distributions, this objective takes a model that is accurate on unseen layouts (hazard error 2.4 percentage points, pp) and moves 33 to 43 pp of its probability onto outcomes that none of a condition's enumerated branches produces. The cause is the objective, not the comparisons: its optimum, computed before training, predicts where the model ends up, and a likelihood that knows the judge's rule learns from the same comparisons without the shift. The shift is robust: it recurs on unseen conditions, under five candidate laws and a three-fold denser enumeration, and about 29 pp remain without the physically impossible events. Its cost is conditional: with those events it inflates hazard probabilities, and a threshold inherited from the pre-feedback model costs about 3 pp of utility per decision on trained and 1 to 2 pp on unseen conditions, through false rejects; without them the utility loss averages below 1 pp over six seeds, though a small regret in action choice remains. Audits of readout, response and decision trace the effect from probabilities to actions.

</details>


### [92] [Drifting, Discriminating, and Directioning for Action Coordination in Higher-dimensional Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25385)
*Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量策略模型及三步学习算法，用于缓解强化学习中高维动作空间处理困难，并通过漂移模型隐式表示复杂分布、单次前向推理动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在高维动作空间中难以有效处理，且仅通过Q梯度反向传播训练漂移模型无法充分捕获期望的动作协调性。

Method: 构建三步学习算法促进动作协调：1）将漂移模型的源分布与对角策略模型联合优化，使漂移模型以残差形式表达对角分布无法表达的动作协调；2）引入漂移前后判别器并进行对抗训练作为正则项，主动促进提取区分两分布的动作协调；3）在rollout动作选择中应用best-of-N采样，为协调提取不足/发现充分的区域提供方向性。

Result: 在三个高维动作空间运动任务上，该方法相比传统对角分布建模持续提升样本效率和最终控制性能；分析表明性能提升源于降低某些动作维度的方差，并有效将其与其他动作耦合为动作协调。

Conclusion: 所提轻量策略模型与三步学习算法能有效建模高维动作空间中的动作协调，显著改善强化学习的样本效率和控制性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight policy model to mitigate the difficulty of handling higher-dimensional action spaces in reinforcement learning (RL), alongside an algorithm to effectively train it. To address this, we focus on drifting models, which can implicitly represent complex distributions and infer actions in a single forward pass. However, while drifting models can be straightforwardly trained via backpropagation of the Q-gradient, this alone cannot be expected to appropriately capture the desired action coordination. Therefore, this paper constructs a three-step learning algorithm to encourage the action coordination. First, the source distribution of the drifting model is co-optimized as a diagonal policy model. This conditions the drifting model to attach the coordination, which cannot be expressed by a diagonal distribution, as residuals. Second, a discriminator model before and after drifting is introduced with adversarial training for a regularization term. This actively promotes the extraction of the coordination that fundamentally separates the two distributions. Third, best-of-N sampling is applied for action selection during rollouts in order to give directionality toward/beyond the regions where the coordination is insufficiently extracted/sufficiently discovered. The proposed method consistently improves the sample efficiency and the final control performance over the conventional diagonal distribution modeling across three locomotion tasks with high-dimensional action spaces. Furthermore, the analysis indicates that this improvement is achieved by reducing variance in certain action dimensions, effectively coupling them with other actions as action coordination.

</details>


### [93] [Improving Imitation Policies for Multi-Phase Tasks through Residual Learning Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25320)
*Clinton Enwerem,Saleh Kemal,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出完成增益（completion gain）以区分基础策略失败阶段与纠正最有效阶段，并据此分配残差强化学习的交互预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差强化学习需将有限环境交互预算分配给多阶段任务各阶段，而基础策略失败最多的阶段未必是纠正后最能提升全任务完成率的阶段。

Method: 将完成增益形式化为单阶段用状态反馈控制器替换基础策略动作分量时全任务完成概率的变化，分解为到达该阶段的概率与阶段内条件完成改进，并用配对回合估计；正的增益估计决定残差策略纠正哪些动作分量及训练回合在各阶段的分配。

Result: 在随机点质量对接实验中，多数失败发生在最终对接阶段，但最大完成增益出现在前一通道阶段；基于增益的分配优于按失败计数比例分配，在更长接近的变体中于一半预算下优于均匀分配。在仿真双臂机器人装配中，完成增益估计仅在插入和释放阶段为正，尽管释放阶段失败很少；在另一装配配置中，释放阶段训练的残差策略使从释放开始处留出状态的完成率提升16.9%。

Conclusion: 按完成增益而非失败频次分配残差强化学习的预算与纠正目标，能更有效地提升多阶段任务的整体完成率。

Abstract: Residual reinforcement learning corrects an imitation-trained base policy using a limited budget of environment interaction. In a task with several phases, this budget must be divided among the phases, and the phase in which the base policy fails is not necessarily the phase in which a correction most improves task completion. We formalize this distinction through the completion gain: the change in full-task completion probability when, during a single phase, a state-feedback controller replaces selected components of the base policy's action. We decompose the completion gain into the probability of reaching the phase and the conditional improvement in completion from the start of the phase, and we estimate it from paired episodes. The positive estimates determine which action components the residual policy corrects and how training episodes are distributed across phases. In stochastic point-mass docking experiments, most failures occurred in the final docking phase, whereas the largest completion gain occurred in the preceding channel phase; gain-based allocation improved completion over allocation proportional to failure counts and, in a variant with a longer approach, over uniform allocation at half of the budget. In simulated bimanual robotic assembly, the completion-gain estimates were positive only for insertion and release, although few failures occurred during release; in a second assembly configuration, a residual policy trained in the release phase improved completion from held-out states at the start of release by 16.9%.

</details>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [HumanCOIN: A 12,500-Hour Dataset for Human Intent in Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26434)
*Shihan Wu,You Liu,Chunyu He,Minghui Qin,Longhan Jiao,Jinbang Guo,Shaoxuan Xie,Kaixi Tian,Wenjie Chen,Jingrui Pang,Yuanhang Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Miao Liu,Tengfei Pan,Yance Jiao,Yunyan Zhang,Yihang Li,Yongjian Shen,Li Liu,Kai Zhu,Chen Fang,Jianxing Chen,Sihan Liu,Xiaofeng Wang,Jie Zhao,Jing Li,Yueqi Wang,Yichen Tang,Wei Li,Zhexuan Yang,Yue Cao,Ji Zhao,Kai Wang,Dongsheng Jiang,Yongwei Wang,Wei Mu,Jiangmiao Pang,Maoqing Yao,Wenchao Ding,Zheng Zhu,Zhongpu Xia,Dalong Du,Jianwei Niu,Dahui Gao,Zhiwei Yu,Bo Lei,Huan Yang,Yonghua Lin,Tiejun Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Guocai Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HumanCOIN第一人称操作数据集，以人类意图为核心，用三层代理记录从决策到动作的意图通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称数据集把观察-动作对作为原子单元，将动作的生成与执行压缩进策略网络权重，导致动作背后的意图状态不可观测；而人类运动控制研究表明意图介于观察与动作之间，并沿意图通路留下可观测痕迹。

Method: 构建HumanCOIN数据集，包含38个场景类别共12,500小时数据，分三层代理：L1注意力层（10,000小时）从头带摄像头视频推断注视；L2用力层（2,000小时）用臂带记录表面肌电；L3认知层（500小时）记录头皮脑电；每层继承下层全部代理，并提供多视角RGB、深度图与点云、6自由度头与腕位姿、步骤级动作与物体标注，L3受控环境还直接测量注视、手部位姿与手指运动学，并发布时钟与校准数据以支持重对齐。

Result: 三层数据覆盖从视觉定向到皮层准备的三个阶段，使视觉-动作对背后的过程可在数据集规模上建模。

Conclusion: 该数据集将意图通路以多层级代理形式规模化记录，弥补了传统观察-动作对数据集中意图不可观测的缺陷。

Abstract: First-person manipulation datasets treat the observation-action pair as the atomic unit and compress the generation and execution of an action into a policy network's weights, rendering the intent state behind the action unobservable. Human motor control research shows that intent mediates between observation and action and leaves observable traces along the intent pathway from decision to effect: gaze reaches the target before the hand, muscle load builds before contact, and cortical activity precedes the motor command. To record this pathway at scale, we present HumanCOIN, a first-person manipulation dataset built around human intent: 12,500 hours across 38 scene classes with three tiers of proxies. At the output end, the L1 attention tier (10,000 h) infers gaze from headband camera video, exposing the operator's next object of attention and reach before the hand moves. Midway, the L2 exertion tier (2,000 h) records surface EMG from armbands, making load expectations readable before execution is complete. At the origin, the L3 cognition tier (500 h) records scalp EEG, giving early readouts of task decisions and monitoring. Each tier inherits all proxies from the tiers below. The dataset further provides multi-view RGB images, depth maps and point clouds, 6-DoF head and wrist pose, and step-level action and object annotations. The controlled L3 setting additionally measures gaze, hand pose and finger kinematics directly. Clock and calibration data are released with the dataset so that any read rate can be re-aligned. The three tiers cover three stations, from visual orienting to cortical preparation, making the process behind visual-action pairs available for modeling at dataset scale. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [95] [Plan4D: Generative Plannable 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26516)
*Hangwei Zhang,Rujing Dang,Ruimin Lin,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: plan4d 是一个可规划的生成式 4D 世界模型，能从场景上下文和文本动作预测未来 RGB 视频及稠密 4D 几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型偏重视觉美观，缺乏显式 4D 建模，物理基础薄弱且时间几何一致性有限，且配对动作与稠密 4D 标注的训练数据稀缺。

Method: 提出模型拼接架构，将 Depth Anything 3 与 LongCat-Video 耦合，并增加辅助 DPT 头预测场景流，利用基础视频生成器与 3D 重建器的互补先验。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上实现生成式 4D 环境反应建模的最先进性能，显著提升生成未来视频的视觉保真度与几何精度，支持复杂长时程细粒度动作规划。

Conclusion: plan4d 是迈向可生成、可规划 4D 世界模型的下一个里程碑，验证了显式 4D 建模与模型拼接策略的有效性。

Abstract: Most world models for future prediction prioritize aestetic quality of generated videos, but offer only weak physical grounding and limited geometric consistency over time, due to the lack of explicit 4D modeling. In this paper, we introduce plan4d, a possible next milestone towards building a generative plannable 4D world model that predicts future RGB videos together with dense 4D geometry, including depth, cameras, point clouds, and scene flow from an observed scene context and text-specified actions. Since training data with paired actions and dense 4D annotations is inherently scarce, plan4d leverages the complementary priors of foundational video generators and 3D reconstructors. Specifically, we propose a model-stitching architecture that couples Depth Anything 3 with LongCat-Video and augments it with an auxiliary DPT head for scene-flow prediction. The resulting model allows us to plan and generate complex, long-horizon, and fine-grained actions in a dynamic 3D scenes, while substantially improving both visual fidelity and geometric accuracy of the generated future videos. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that plan4d achieves state-of-the-art performance in generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [96] [Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26438)
*Zixuan Liu,Hashem Haghbayan,Zizhan Zheng,Siavash Khajavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R2-VLA是一个风险控制的检索增强VLA框架，通过学习不确定性预测器与任务级风险校准，在满足用户指定任务成功率的前提下实现可靠测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强VLA即使在检索到相关示范时，也可能无法有效利用这些信息而导致任务失败，因此需要一种能可靠判定何时自主行动、何时请求专家协助的机制。

Method: R2-VLA学习一个不确定性预测器，估计在给定检索示范时VLA预测动作的不确定性，以指导自主行动或请求专家协助；并引入任务级风险控制流程，校准不确定性阈值以满足用户指定的任务失败风险要求；同时持续将专家纠正纳入检索记忆以改进未来自适应并保持风险保证。

Result: 在三个VLA骨干网络和四个LIBERO基准套件上的实验表明，R2-VLA平均任务成功率提升9.2至11.2个百分点，且以更少的专家请求达到目标成功率。

Conclusion: 将检索示范的有用性与任务级风险控制相结合，能够有效支持成功的机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models enable efficient test-time adaptation by leveraging expert demonstrations as additional context without updating model parameters. However, even when retrieved demonstrations are relevant to the current task, the VLA may fail to effectively utilize the retrieved information, ultimately leading to task failure. To address this challenge, we propose R2-VLA, a risk-controlled retrieval-augmented VLA framework that enables reliable test-time adaptation by satisfying a user-specified task success rate requirement with minimal expert assistance. Specifically, R2-VLA learns an uncertainty predictor that estimates the uncertainty of the VLA's predicted actions given the retrieved demonstrations, guiding when to act autonomously or request expert assistance. We further introduce a task-level risk-control procedure that calibrates the uncertainty threshold to satisfy a user-specified task-failure risk requirement. Moreover, R2-VLA continually incorporates expert corrections into its retrieval memory to improve future adaptation while preserving the prescribed task-level risk guarantee. Experiments across three VLA backbones and four LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that R2-VLA improves average task success by 9.2--11.2 percentage points, achieving the desired target with fewer expert requests. These results highlight the effectiveness of connecting the usefulness of retrieved demonstrations with task-level risk control to enable successful robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [97] [ViDecide: Teaching Video Diffusion Models to Reason and Plan Step by Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26389)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Yiming Gao,Zeqi Xiao,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以决策为中心的ViDecide框架训练视频扩散模型进行因果状态转移预测并决定停止时机，配合Corrective Fine-tuning纠正失败决策，在多步推理与规划任务上大幅提升严格成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领先视频生成模型虽能合成高度逼真视频，但在多步推理与规划中仍频繁违反物理与逻辑约束，即使使用大规模推理数据或现有扩散强化学习方法训练后依然如此；作者将其归因于传统视频训练范式把解视为固定长度片段而非决策链。

Method: 提出ViDecide决策中心框架，训练视频扩散模型因果地预测状态转移并决定何时停止，采用面向决策的训练调度；进一步提出Corrective Fine-tuning（CFT），通过将失败决策的噪声映射到模型自身成功的延续上来纠正失败决策，无需策略梯度优化即可稳定提升。

Result: 在抽象游戏、规划任务及新的多步机器人规划基准上，ViDecide平均严格成功率达83.8%，远高于标准微调的23.3%和最强先前方法的67.8%，并可泛化到更大规模谜题与未见布局。

Conclusion: 该框架释放了视频生成模型作为视觉世界中可靠推理者与规划者的更大潜力。

Abstract: Current leading video generation models can synthesize highly realistic videos, while still frequently violating physical and logical constraints in multi-step reasoning and planning, even after training with large-scale reasoning data or existing diffusion RL methods. We attribute these failures to the conventional video training paradigm, which treats a solution as a fixed-length clip rather than a chain of decisions. We present ViDecide, a decision-centric framework that trains video diffusion models to predict state transitions causally and decide when to stop, with a decision-focused training schedule. We further propose Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT), which corrects failed decisions by mapping their noise to the model's own successful continuations, achieving stable improvement without policy-gradient optimization. Across abstract games, planning tasks, and a new benchmark of multi-step robot planning, ViDecide achieves an average strict success rate of 83.8%, far above 23.3% for standard fine-tuning and 67.8% for the strongest prior method, and generalizes to larger puzzles and unseen layouts. Our framework therefore unlocks greater potential for video generation models as reliable reasoners and planners in the visual world. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [98] [BADAS-3.0: Real-Time Driving Perception and Planning from One Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26294)
*Roni Goldshmidt,Hamish Scott,Lorenzo Niccolini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BADAS-3.0提出仅用一个前视相机和实时延迟（<50ms）的1B参数视频编码器加四个小头，从2秒视频预测当前速度、已行驶的度量路径、碰撞风险分数及6.4秒内六个候选未来；在未训练过的相机配置下，其minADE₆在五个轨迹基准上比34B的Alpamayo 2 Super低44%，共享配置下二者持平，并在两个新增开环安全基准上通过率高于所测已发表规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仅用一个相机和实时延迟时，驾驶模型能达到何种性能水平，并弥补现有基准多集中于常规驾驶、缺乏安全评估的问题。

Method: 构建BADAS-3.0：1B参数视频编码器加四个小头，输入2秒单前视相机视频，预测当前速度、度量路径、碰撞风险分数和6.4秒内六个候选未来，推理延迟低于50ms；并新增两个开环安全基准（真实行车记录仪碰撞与险情、Euro NCAP场景）。

Result: 在未训练过的相机配置下，五个轨迹基准上minADE₆比34B Alpamayo 2 Super低44%（后者用六相机且慢100倍以上）；在共享配置下二者相当；两个安全基准上通过率高于所有测试的已发表规划器。

Conclusion: 单相机实时延迟的BADAS-3.0可取得有竞争力的轨迹预测与更高的安全通过率，且现有标称驾驶基准不足，需引入安全导向基准评估规划器。

Abstract: We ask how far a driving model can get with one camera and real-time latency. BADAS-3.0 is a 1B-parameter video encoder with four small heads. From two seconds of one forward camera, it predicts the current speed, the metric path just driven, a collision-risk score and six candidate futures over 6.4 seconds, in under 50 ms. Given the car's current speed, BADAS-3.0 has 44% lower minADE$_6$ over five trajectory benchmarks than the 34B Alpamayo 2 Super, which reads six cameras and runs over 100x slower. The gain comes from camera rigs that neither model was trained on. On the rig they share, the two are on par. Existing benchmarks are mostly nominal driving, so we add two open-loop safety benchmarks, one of real dashcam collisions and near-misses and one of Euro NCAP scenarios. On both, BADAS-3.0 has a higher pass rate than every published planner we tested.

</details>


### [99] [MixReact: Physics-Based Reactive Humanoid Control from Multimodal Cues via a Discrete-Continuous Mixture of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26218)
*Bin Li,Xing Zhu,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MixReact统一框架，从文本、音频、视频线索生成物理可执行的人形反应，结合DisCo-MoT状态预测与HAT动作执行，在保真度、多样性和物理一致性上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线索驱动反应生成只支持有限线索模态且局限于纯运动学生成，结果易出现物理伪影，无法像人一样对多样线索做出语义恰当且物理可执行的反应。

Method: 构建首个物理基人形反应控制统一框架：DisCo-MoT在MoT架构中通过单向混合注意力将离散掩码建模分支与连续自回归扩散分支耦合，进行状态预测；HAT将预测的下一状态与执行历史映射为可执行动作，实现物理仿真中的闭环控制。

Result: 在文本、音频、视频三种模态上均较基线在保真度、多样性、物理一致性方面取得一致提升，消融实验验证了各组件互补贡献。

Conclusion: MixReact证明了离散-连续混合建模与历史感知动作变换相结合可有效实现多模态线索驱动的物理基人形反应生成。

Abstract: Humanoid agents in everyday environments are expected to react to diverse cues as humans do, with semantically appropriate and physically executable behaviors. Existing cue-driven reaction generation methods support only a narrow range of cue modalities and are limited to purely kinematic generation, leaving the resulting reactions susceptible to physical artifacts. To this end, we present MixReact, the first unified framework for physics-based reactive humanoid control from text, audio, and video cues, integrating a Discrete--Continuous Mixture of Transformers (DisCo-MoT) for state prediction with a History-aware Action Transformer (HAT) for physical execution. DisCo-MoT couples a discrete masked-modeling branch with a continuous autoregressive diffusion branch via one-way mixture attention within a MoT architecture. Guidance from discrete context mitigates collapse into averaged behaviors in one-to-many reaction generation, while direct prediction in continuous state space bypasses the decoding bottleneck of discrete representations. HAT then maps the predicted next state and observed execution history to executable actions, enabling robust closed-loop control in physics-based simulation. Experiments across all three modalities demonstrate consistent improvements over the baselines in fidelity, diversity, and physical consistency, while ablations validate the complementary contributions of the components. Our code will be released to facilitate future research. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [100] [SpecWM: Turning Verification into Refinement for Streaming World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26211)
*Jiaming Li,Lingzhen Li,Xiaze Yang,Chenyu Zhu,Kaiqi Liu,Xiaotong Zhao,Songlian Li,Toyota Li,Alan Zhao,Haobin Zhong,Yue Ma,Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpecWM是一个无需训练的统一推理框架，利用目标模型的潜在速度场验证草稿块，并通过可复用的校正与双阈值机制实现接受、精炼或拒绝，在流式视频世界模型中取得质量与速度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测方法仅依赖外部评分器验证解码帧，评估维度有限且增加解码与评分开销；评分不提供校正，被拒草稿需昂贵的目标重生成；需在保持质量的同时提升推理速度。

Method: 提出SpecWM：以目标模型的潜在速度场验证草稿块，验证同时产生可复用校正；用两个阈值选择接受、精炼或拒绝；一步精炼复用校正提升感知质量，三步精炼增强相机跟随与轨迹一致性，进而引入过渡感知精炼。

Result: 在WBench Navigation上，SpecWM相对目标模型实现1.63倍加速并提升视觉质量，NavScore从草稿的75.99升至85.63，取得最佳质量-速度-控制权衡；LongSpecBench显示长多轮生成中质量与控制持续；Hy-Nomas实验验证跨骨干与去噪预算的泛化性。

Conclusion: SpecWM是一种无需训练、统一的流式世界模型推理框架，通过潜在速度场验证与可复用校正实现高效精炼，在多项基准上同时提升速度、视觉质量与控制一致性。

Abstract: Recently, video world models have advanced interactive generation, with large models offering higher quality and smaller models enabling faster inference. Existing speculative methods combine their strengths through fast draft generation and selective target regeneration guided by rewards on decoded frames. However, verification covers only the external scorer's evaluation dimensions and adds decoding and scoring costs. Moreover, these scores provide no correction, requiring costly target regeneration for rejected drafts. To address these limitations, we propose Speculative World Model (SpecWM), a unified, training-free inference framework for streaming world models. It verifies draft chunks using the target's latent velocity field. Verification also yields a reusable correction; two thresholds select Accept, Refine, or Reject. Specifically, one-step refinement reuses it to improve perceptual quality, while three-step refinement strengthens camera following and trajectory consistency. These findings motivate transition-aware refinement. On WBench Navigation, SpecWM improves visual quality at 1.63$\times$ speedup over the target and raises NavScore from the draft's 75.99 to 85.63, achieving the best quality-speed-control trade-off. Additionally, our LongSpecBench shows sustained quality and control over long, multi-turn rollouts, while experiments on Hy-Nomas validate SpecWM across backbones and denoising budgets.

</details>


### [101] [SpatialScout-V2: Can VLM Agents Actively Explore Real-World 3D Scenes and Localize Objects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26177)
*Zhaoxi Zhang,Hao Liang,Linxuan Wu,Yiming Xu,Yifu Yuan,Kun Liang,Chenming Tang,Meiyi Qiang,Wentao Zhang,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialScout-V2基准，将主动建图任务从仿真扩展到真实住宅场景，评估7个前沿视觉语言模型在对象中心定位上的表现，发现所有模型均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能研究多依赖仿真环境，缺乏对真实场景中主动探索与三维建图能力的评估基准。

Method: 基于Realsee3D中100个真实住宅构建基准，覆盖29类共3,171个目标对象；智能体在固定交互预算内自主选择记录的视点与视角，整合标定RGB观测并迭代估计和修正对象中心；采用mean IoU、IoU阈值命中率和目标覆盖率评估定位精度与完整性。

Result: 7个前沿视觉语言模型中有6个全目标mean IoU低于20%，最佳模型Claude Opus 5.5仅达22.52%，仅18.36%的目标IoU超过0.5；分析显示广泛提交覆盖率可与有限定位精度并存，目标遗漏进一步降低总分。

Conclusion: 通过真实场景主动探索构建准确且完整的对象中心图仍面临重大挑战，现有视觉语言模型在该任务上能力显著不足。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires agents to acquire visual evidence and integrate partial observations into coherent three-dimensional representations. We introduce SpatialScout-V2, which extends SpatialScout's active mapping task from simulation to real residential scenes through three advances: real visual observations, preservation of original scene content, and interactive exploration of recorded views. Built from 100 homes in Realsee3D, the benchmark covers 3,171 target objects across 29 categories. Given a fixed target inventory, agents autonomously select recorded viewpoints and viewing directions, integrate calibrated RGB observations, and iteratively estimate and revise object centers within a fixed interaction budget. We evaluate the localization accuracy and completeness of object-center maps using mean IoU, IoU-threshold hit rates, and target coverage. Evaluating seven frontier vision-language models (VLMs), we find that six achieve an all-target mean IoU below 20\%. Even the best-performing model, Claude Opus 5.5, reaches only 22.52\%, with just 18.36\% of targets exceeding an IoU of 0.5. Further analysis shows that broad submission coverage can coexist with limited localization accuracy, while target omissions further reduce overall scores. These results highlight substantial challenges in constructing accurate and complete object-center maps through active exploration of real scenes.

</details>


### [102] [StreamEgo: Streaming Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26165)
*Tianyi Xu,Boyang Lyu,Yunkai Tang,Kairan Ding,Jiahang Li,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Shuchen Weng,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamEgo 提出了一个实时、无界且可泛化的外中心到内中心（Exo2Ego）视频生成框架，通过流式双分支先验、区域感知损失课程和分块自回归蒸馏，在单张 H100 GPU 上达到 24.3 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Exo2Ego 方法在操作区域质量下降、过拟合、仅支持固定长度片段且延迟高，无法满足实时流式应用需求。

Method: 设计流式双分支先验为外观和骨骼运动学提供像素对齐引导；引入区域感知损失课程以提升泛化并稳定多目标扩散训练；将模型蒸馏为分块自回归模型，并辅以区域感知损坏机制和首块课程。

Result: 在单张 H100 GPU 上实现 24.3 FPS 的高质量、可泛化实时流式 Exo2Ego 视频生成。

Conclusion: StreamEgo 有效解决了现有方法的局限，实现了无界、可泛化的实时流式 Exo2Ego 视频生成，为实时应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Exocentric-to-egocentric (Exo2Ego) video generation transforms third-person recordings into first-person viewpoints, unlocking immersive AR/VR experiences and scalable demonstration synthesis for embodied robotics. Extending it to a streaming setting is expected to further enable live applications. However, existing methods suffer from degraded manipulation regions, overfitting and confinement to fixed-length clips with prohibitive latency. To address these limitations, we present StreamEgo, a real-time framework for unbounded, generalizable Exo2Ego video generation. To resolve intricate manipulation dynamics while supporting the streaming pipeline, a streaming dual-branch prior provides pixel-aligned guidance for translating appearance and skeletal kinematics. We also introduce a region-aware loss curriculum to improve generalization and stabilize multi-objective diffusion training. Furthermore, we distill the model into a chunk-wise autoregressive model, augmented with a region-aware corruption mechanism and a first-chunk curriculum, enabling high-quality, generalizable real-time streaming Exo2Ego generation at 24.3 FPS on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [103] [Domain-Aware Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy Spacecraft Pose Estimation under Sim-to-Real Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26065)
*Nidhal Eddine Chenni,Arunkumar Rathinam,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对航天器自主交会与近距离操作中的位姿估计可信度问题，提出在合成图像翻译为真实外观后进行共形校准，从而恢复覆盖保证并大幅减少静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测能为位姿估计提供带有形式化保证的不确定性集合，但其前提是校准数据与测试数据同分布；在轨道感知中，标注校准图像仅来自合成渲染而实际图像为真实图像，分布不一致导致共形保证失效，且失败往往是静默的（预测集合看似有效却遗漏真实位姿）。

Method: 在SPEED+数据集上先用合成图像进行共形位姿估计校准以复现覆盖失效，再将合成图像翻译为真实图像外观后进行校准；由于翻译只改变外观而保持几何不变，合成位姿标签仍然有效，且无需真实标注图像。实验采用两种翻译模型验证。

Result: 在误覆盖水平0.1及以上时，基于翻译图像的校准使覆盖率恢复到规定水平以上，并将静默失败从最高43.5%的测试图像降至最多3.3%。

Conclusion: 通过在合成图像外观翻译后进行共形校准，可在无需真实标注图像的情况下恢复共形位姿估计的覆盖保证并显著减少静默失败，推动共形位姿估计更接近在太空中的可信部署。

Abstract: Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations require not only an accurate estimate of the pose of the target spacecraft, but also a reliable indication of when this estimate can be trusted. Conformal prediction provides such an indication with a formal guarantee, by producing pose uncertainty sets that contain the true pose with a specified probability. Such a guarantee, however, assumes that calibration and test data follow the same distribution, an assumption that is violated in orbital perception, where labelled calibration images are available only from synthetic renderings while operational images are real. In this work, we show on the SPEED+ dataset that conformal pose estimation calibrated on synthetic images loses its coverage guarantee on real images, and that many of the resulting failures are silent: the predicted pose set appears valid but excludes the true pose. To address this, we calibrate on synthetic images translated to the appearance of real images. Since translation alters appearance while preserving geometry, the synthetic pose labels remain valid and no labelled real images are required. Experiments with two translation models show that this calibration restores coverage above the specified level for miscoverage levels of 0.1 and above, and reduces silent failures from up to 43.5% to at most 3.3% of the test images, bringing conformal pose estimation a step closer to trustworthy deployment in space.

</details>


### [104] [Less Than Meets the Eye: Action-Set Identifiability and Principled Abstention for 3D Physical Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26056)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Abrar Samin,Sammam Mahdi,Syeda Maliha Tabassum,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Intervene3D框架，通过有界相机运动区分走廊、镜面反射与显示屏图像等不同深度解释，并在运动无法区分时主动弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 走廊、其镜面反射和显示屏图像可能外观相同但物理表面深度不同，需要判断观察到的图像对应哪种解释，并避免在不可分辨时给出错误置信答案。

Method: Intervene3D输入图像和初始几何估计，对每个假设和候选运动预测视图，以最大最小可分性选择运动；仅当可用运动能区分剩余假设时报告机制和第一物理表面，否则弃权；并设 adequacy 检验拒绝无假设可解释的观察。

Result: 合成基准上，禁用弃权时76.4%不可分辨场景给出置信答案，启用弃权后无置信答案；在回答场景100%准确率下，maximin选择回答61.0%，summed选择回答43.1%；adequacy检验将强曲镜面场景错误答案从56.8%降至12.4%；真实显示屏实验一次运动使十二个网络汇总中位表面误差降低31.5%；33.4%幻觉基准图像上面板内误差大于面板外，36.9% Mirror3D帧将第一物理表面置于玻璃后方。

Conclusion: 有界相机运动结合弃权与adequacy检验能有效区分深度解释、减少不可分辨场景的错误置信答案，并改善真实场景中的表面深度估计。

Abstract: A corridor, its mirror reflection, and its image on a display can look identical, yet their physical surfaces lie at different depths. We test whether bounded camera motions distinguish these explanations. Intervene3D takes an image and an initial geometry estimate, predicts the view under each hypothesis and candidate motion, and selects a motion by maximin separability. It reports the mechanism and first physical surface only if the available motions separate the hypotheses still in contention. Otherwise it abstains. An adequacy test also rejects observations that no hypothesis explains. On a matched synthetic benchmark with exact landmarks, disabling abstention gives confident answers on 76.4% of unresolvable scenes. With abstention, none receives a confident answer under exact or noisy landmarks. At 100% accuracy on answered scenes, maximin selection answers 61.0% of scenes and summed selection 43.1%. The adequacy test lowers wrong answers on strongly curved mirror scenes from 56.8% to 12.4%. Real display experiments compare two hypotheses using one frozen depth-network estimate. The second image supplies photometric evidence and is never passed through the depth network. One executed motion reduces the pooled median surface error of twelve networks by 31.5%. These networks have larger error inside the annotated panel than outside on 33.4% of illusion-benchmark images. They place the first physical surface behind the glass on 36.9% of Mirror3D frames.

</details>


### [105] [MorphTwin: Enriching Deformable Material Responses from Videos for Morphable and Interactive Physical Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25963)
*Haoqin Hong,Ding Fan,Fubin Dou,Tianyu Huang,Yuzhuo Chen,Zhemeng Zhang,Hanxiao Jiang,Congcong Zhu,Gerhard Petrus Hancke,Jingrun Chen,Rynson WH Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorphTwin通过增强局部变形模式并独立控制体积与形状响应，从视频重建可交互物理孪生体，提升了材料参数估计和未来跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弹簧基物理孪生体（如PhysTwin）将体积与形状响应耦合，导致从视频重放的运动无法保证对新力的正确响应；同时需要支持可解释的材料编辑与迁移。

Method: 提出MorphTwin，丰富局部变形模式以独立控制体积与形状响应，保留标准弹簧力和显式仿真；通过离线校准将共享的相对响应变化映射到各重建图上的刚度更新；并构建涵盖不同刚度物体的自我中心立体与RGB-D基准。

Result: 在合成场景相同逆设置下，相较PhysTwin，泊松比平均绝对误差降低75.8%，杨氏模量平均相对误差降低22.1%；真实实验中重建精度提升，未来跟踪误差最多降低14.4%，且保持交互速度。

Conclusion: MorphTwin在保持高效显式仿真的同时，解耦了体积与形状响应，显著提高了从视频重建物理孪生体的材料参数准确性和长期跟踪性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive physical twins from video requires accurately capturing how objects deform while maintaining efficient simulation. We observe that reproducing motion in a video does not guarantee correct responses to new forces when the physical representation restricts how an object can deform. In spring-based twins, coupling between volume and shape responses creates such a limitation. We present MorphTwin, which enriches local deformation modes to capture and independently control these responses while retaining standard spring forces and explicit simulation. To support interpretable material editing and transfer, we use offline calibration to map shared relative response changes to stiffness updates on each reconstructed graph. Under identical inverse settings on synthetic scenes, MorphTwin reduces the mean absolute error of Poisson's ratio by 75.8% and the mean relative error of Young's modulus by 22.1% compared to PhysTwin. We introduce an egocentric stereo and RGB-D benchmark covering interactions with objects of varying stiffness. Real-world experiments demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy and up to 14.4% lower future tracking error than PhysTwin at interactive speeds.

</details>


### [106] [Point Dynamics as Proxy for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25946)
*Dong An,Tianyu Liu,Haoran Ding,Wen Yang,Anqing Duan,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将机器人世界模型中的动力学预测与视觉渲染分离，以3D点动力学作为中介，提升物体运动预测精度而不损失图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能紧密跟随给定动作，但被操作物体的运动常与动作不一致（塌陷或漂移），原因在于动作或机器人渲染仅指定机器人去向而未指定场景如何响应，单一视频模型需同时隐式推断交互动力学和渲染外观。

Method: 将首帧提升为3D点，用学习到的点动力学模型模拟点对机器人运动的响应，再将模拟点渲染成像素对齐视频供任意视频模型读取；在DROID数据集上用两种不同架构的视频渲染器进行受控实验，并在真实双臂机器人上验证。

Result: 物体运动误差分别降低8.6%和6.2%且不降低图像质量，在真实双臂机器人上降低8.1%；视频跟随模拟结果，动力学越准确物体运动越准确，编辑点可引导视频生成。

Conclusion: 将动力学预测与视觉渲染解耦并以3D点动力学为中介，可显著提升机器人世界模型的运动保真度，表明动力学建模的进步能转化为更可信的机器人世界模型。

Abstract: Robotic world models predict future video frames from the current observation and a sequence of robot actions. While recent models follow the given actions closely, manipulated objects often collapse or move inconsistently with the action. We attribute this to the conditioning: an action, or a rendering of the robot, specifies where the robot goes but not how the scene responds, leaving a single video model to infer the interaction dynamics implicitly while also rendering appearance. We therefore separate dynamics prediction from visual rendering, with 3D point dynamics as the proxy between them. Specifically, we lift the first frame into 3D points, simulate how they respond to the robot's motion with a learned point dynamics model, and render the simulated points back into pixel-aligned videos that any video model can read. In controlled experiments on the DROID dataset with two video renderers of different architectures, this point dynamics proxy reduces object motion error by 8.6% and 6.2% without degrading image quality, and by 8.1% on a real bimanual robot. Moreover, the video follows the simulation: more accurate dynamics yield more accurate object motion, and editing the points steers the video. This suggests that advances in dynamics modeling can translate into more faithful robotic world models.

</details>


### [107] [SCCV-6D: Sparse Canonical Coordinate Voting for Holistic Multi-View RGB-D 6D Pose Estimation in Robotic Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25819)
*Nico Leuze,Samed Dogan,Maximilian Hoh,Henry Schaub,Alfred Schöttl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCCV-6D是一种全稀疏多视图RGB-D位姿估计器，单次前向推理即可同时预测任意数量物体的类别、平移偏移和旋转，无需后处理即可实现高精度6D位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业料箱抓取需要在杂乱、部分遮挡及反光无纹理零件下仍准确的位姿估计，但现有高精度多阶段流水线内存和运行时开销大，难以满足实际部署约束；而整体式流水线通过无差别下采样或降分辨率控制成本，精度受限。

Method: 提出SCCV-6D全稀疏多视图估计器：以毫米级重建3D空间，通过分阶段热图机制剪除背景，在高分辨率下同时预测类别、平移偏移和旋转；旋转建模为规范坐标投票，点以置信度投票其规范物体坐标，结合对称感知加权Procrustes求解闭式恢复位姿；偏移投票诱导簇内点恢复，去除耗时后处理；复用热图任务监督的注意力先验，设计注意力门控蒸馏目标重加权师生特征对齐。

Result: 在IPD测试集上达到0.826 AP，每场景1.13秒，显著快于领先方法；在MV-YCB上达到96.1 ADD-S；在密集堆叠的ROBI料箱上仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: SCCV-6D在保持竞争性精度的同时大幅提升推理速度，验证了全稀疏单次多视图位姿估计在工业部署中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Industrial bin picking requires pose estimates that are accurate under clutter, partial occlusion, and the reflective, texture-less nature of manufactured parts. Most recent approaches to 6D pose estimation follow multi-stage pipelines. These achieve high accuracy, but incur memory and runtime footprints that do not cope with the operational constraints of many real-world industrial deployments. Holistic pipelines estimate poses in a single pass, with cost controlled through indiscriminate subsampling or lowered resolution. Continuing this line, we present SCCV-6D, a fully sparse multi-view RGB-D estimator. We reconstruct the 3D space at millimeter precision, prune background via a staged heatmap mechanism, and predict class, translation offset and rotation at highest resolution for an arbitrary number of objects simultaneously. Rotation is formulated as canonical coordinate voting. Points vote for their canonical object coordinates with an associated confidence, enabling closed-form pose recovery with a symmetry-aware weighted Procrustes solve. Along an in-cluster point recovery induced by offset votes, this removes time-consuming post-processing stages. We reuse the task-supervised attention priors from the heatmap formulation for a novel attention-gated distillation objective that reweights teacher-student feature alignment. On the IPD test split we report competitive 0.826 AP at 1.13 s per scene, significantly faster than the leading entries. On MV-YCB our approach reaches 96.1 ADD-S, and remains competitive even on the densely packed bins of ROBI.

</details>


### [108] [GeoPillars: A Large-Kernel Pillar Detector for Single-Sweep LiDAR Under Point Sparsity, and a Decomposition of Its Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25787)
*Chaitanya Dumbre,Shubham Sarkar,Akash Mohapatra,Ghanasham Patil,Sheetal Girase*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoPillars是一个在每0.4米柱体仅8点预算下的单帧LiDAR 3D检测器，其性能提升主要来自检测头分辨率提升，而非新组件，且零样本迁移效果差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的点预算下（每0.4米柱体仅8点，丢弃46.6%范围内点），现有方法性能受限，作者试图通过多种新组件提升单帧LiDAR检测性能。

Method: 提出GeoPillars，包含几何柱体特征、柱内分组向量注意力、BEV网格上学习的绝对位置先验、大核骨干、辅助表面法线监督和全分辨率检测头；在123k合成CARLA扫描上训练，评估汽车类别。

Result: 达到81.33 BEV AP@0.7，同预算下比CenterPoint高13.76点，但在其原生更密预算下仅高1.91点；逐级消融显示检测头分辨率单独恢复10.19点，编码器/骨干/颈/位置先验联合增加3.37点，法线监督仅增0.20点（在运行间波动内）；朝向误差七模型相似，GeoPillars反转朝向频率约低一半但速度最慢；零样本在6,743个真实nuScenes关键帧上无模型迁移成功，仅保留3.7%–12.2%的CARLA精度。

Conclusion: 性能增益主要归因于检测头分辨率提升而非新组件；验证集非场景分离，结果属分布内；零样本迁移失败，且数据收集缺陷导致强度恒定，模型仅从几何学习。

Abstract: GeoPillars is a pillar-based 3D detector for single-sweep LiDAR under a hard budget of eight points per 0.4 m pillar, a budget that discards 46.6% of in-range points. It adds geometric pillar features, grouped vector attention within each pillar, a learned absolute positional prior on the bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid, a large-kernel backbone, auxiliary surface-normal supervision and a full-resolution detection head. Trained on 123k synthetic CARLA scans and evaluated on cars, it reaches 81.33 BEV AP@0.7, 13.76 points above CenterPoint at the same input budget and 1.91 points above CenterPoint at its native, denser budget. These are in-distribution figures, because the validation split is per-frame rather than scene-disjoint. A rung-by-rung decomposition shows that the new components explain little of the gain. Raising the detection-head resolution alone recovers 10.19 of the 13.76 points, the joint change of encoder, backbone, neck and positional prior adds 3.37, and the normal-supervision branch adds 0.20, which is within run-to-run variation. With heading reversals folded out, orientation error is similar for all seven models, although GeoPillars reverses headings about half as often as a resolution-matched baseline. It is also the slowest of the seven models. Evaluated zero-shot on 6,743 real nuScenes keyframes, no model transfers, and each retains 3.7% to 12.2% of its CARLA accuracy. A collection defect left LiDAR intensity constant, so every model learned from geometry alone. Code and checkpoints are publicly available.

</details>


### [109] [DACA: Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25722)
*Jiaming Liu,Linghe Kong,Guixu Zhang,Huaxi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双锚协作智能体DACA，将3D视觉定位重构为语义-几何双锚协作推理范式，通过双向信息交换实现闭环优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-VG智能体采用单向流水线式工具调用架构，将语义理解与几何重建分离，导致误差累积且无法自主从失败中恢复。

Method: DACA由两个互补专用工具组成：语义锚工具持有目标的视觉语义特征，通过光流与VLM局部验证实现目标跟踪；几何锚工具持有目标的3D空间几何特征，通过体素化与多关键点投影进行全局空间推理；两者通过标准化工具调用协议双向交换信息，自主决定下一步感知-动作步骤形成闭环优化。

Result: 零样本DACA在ScanRefer上达到73.6%的Acc@0.25，在Nr3D上达到70.4%的总体准确率，远超最新零样本3D-VG方法。

Conclusion: DACA通过语义-几何双锚协作与闭环优化有效解决了单向流水线架构的误差累积问题，显著提升了零样本3D视觉定位性能。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding (3D-VG) is a task that bridges the natural language and 3D space, serving as a foundational capability for embodied intelligence and human-computer interaction. Although recent 3D-VG agents overcome the reliance on pre-processed 3D point clouds, they essentially employ a unidirectional, pipeline-style tool-calling architecture. This architecture separates semantic understanding from geometric reconstruction, leading to error accumulation and an inability to autonomously recover from failures. To address this flaw, this paper proposes the Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent (DACA), which reinterprets 3D-VG as a semantic-geometric dual-anchor collaborative reasoning paradigm.
