DACA comprises two complementary specialized tools: the semantic anchor tool holds the visual semantic features of the target and achieves efficient target tracking via optical flow and VLM local verification; the geometric anchor tool holds the 3D spatial geometric features of the target and performs global spatial reasoning via voxelization and multi-keypoint projection. The two components exchange information bidirectionally via a standardized tool call protocol, autonomously deciding on the next perception-action step to form a closed-loop optimization. Experiments on the ScanRefer and Nr3D benchmarks demonstrate that zero-shot DACA achieves an overall Acc@0.25 of 73.6\% on ScanRefer and 70.4\% overall accuracy on Nr3D, far surpassing the latest zero-shot 3D-VG methods. The code and models are available at this https URL

</details>


### [110] [SkillLens: Action-Guided Dense Visual Dynamics for Proficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25712)
*Deokhyun Ahn,Hyukjin Kim,Jae-Ho Han,Bumsub Ham,Heeseung Choi,Ig-Jae Kim,Haksub Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillLens 利用原子动作描述作为时间引导，定位与熟练度相关的窗口并提取密集视觉动态特征，通过原型边际评分和 CORAL 序数校准融合自我中心/外中心线索，实现人类熟练度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熟练度是高层且主观的属性，无法仅由动作身份捕捉；现有基于多模态观测（如自我-外中心视频、注视、姿态、惯性信号）的方法难以弥合传感器级证据与抽象熟练度标签之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 SkillLens 框架：使用原子动作描述作为时间引导（而非直接语义监督）定位熟练度相关窗口进行密集视觉动态提取；将每个窗口汇总为动作动态特征，并用同活动-动作类别中更高和更低熟练度执行的高-低原型边际进行评分；通过 CORAL 序数校准将自我中心/外中心 PDM 线索与全局自我-外中心-注视基线融合。

Result: 在 Ego-Exo4D 数据集上达到 0.542 的准确率。

Conclusion: 以原子动作描述作为时间引导并结合原型边际评分与序数校准，可有效桥接传感器证据与抽象熟练度标签，提升人类熟练度估计性能。

Abstract: Human proficiency estimation is challenging because skill is a high-level and often subjective property that is not captured by action identity alone. Existing approaches leverage multimodal observations such as ego-exo video, gaze, pose, and inertial signals, but the gap between sensor-level evidence and abstract proficiency labels remains difficult to bridge. We present SkillLens, a proficiency estimation framework that uses atomic action descriptions as temporal guidance, rather than direct semantic supervision, to localize proficiency-relevant windows for dense visual-dynamics extraction. Each window is summarized into an action-dynamics feature and scored by a high-low prototype margin against higher- and lower-proficiency executions from the same activity-action category. Fusing these ego/exo PDM cues with a global ego-exo-gaze baseline through CORAL ordinal calibration, our method achieves 0.542 accuracy on Ego-Exo4D. Our project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [111] [TwoPass: Draft-Guided Few-Step Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25553)
*Yijun Liu,Shijia Ge,Tongtong Feng,Xin Wang,Yuan Meng,Yuwei Liu,Zhi Wang,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TwoPass自条件视频预测框架，仅需4次函数评估即可达到SOTA，远少于现有方法的50-59次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频预测器对每个未来从噪声去噪时不使用自身估计，导致预测质量受限且需大量网络调用。

Method: 保持标准流匹配目标，训练时用共享网络生成的去噪干净视频估计作为条件（自生成草稿训练），并加入仅细化后期适配器；推理时草稿复用迭代细化，每次速度预测同时得到求解器更新与下一草稿。

Result: 在Bridge和Language Table上以4 NFE取得SOTA，显著优于现有预测器的50-59次调用。

Conclusion: TwoPass通过自条件训练与草稿复用推理，在极少函数评估下提升了动作条件视频预测质量与效率。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video prediction lets robots anticipate the visual consequences of their actions for planning and policy evaluation. Existing predictors denoise each future from noise without using their own estimate of it, which limits the quality of the predicted future, in particular of the manipulated objects, and requires many network calls. We present TwoPass, a self-conditioned training-and-inference framework that keeps the standard flow-matching objective. Self-Generated Draft Training supplies a detached clean-video estimate from the shared network as conditioning at the same noisy state. A Refinement-Only Late Adapter applies a dedicated residual transformation when a draft is present. At inference, Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement obtains both a solver update and the next draft from each velocity prediction, requiring as few as 4 function evaluations (NFE) per video chunk. Training samples discrete solver-query noise levels and updates the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on Bridge and Language Table show that TwoPass achieves state-of-the-art results with 4 NFE, far fewer than the 50-59 calls of existing predictors. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [112] [PRIO: Pre-trained Representation for Interactive Objects in 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25500)
*Shixun Huang,Lifu Wei,Yue Chang,Chen Geng,Heng Zhang,Jinhyung Ahn,Caoliwen Wang,Siyuan Chen,Donglai Xiang,Jiajun Wu,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出预训练表示PRIO，为静态3D物体预测单次前向传播的构型空间，实现实时交互式4D物理仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景重建与生成虽已规模化，但物体保持静止，无法压缩或摆动，缺乏实时响应物理动作的4D世界模型表示。

Method: PRIO以静态几何为输入，单次前向传播预测物体特定的构型空间，提供拉格朗日力学的广义坐标；构型在坐标中线性，可预计算动力学量，随后以交互速率运行动力学而无需额外网络推理；预训练于多样4D轨迹，支持网格、点云和3D高斯。

Result: 在真实到仿真工作流和机器人操作中验证；相比现有4D生成方法，PRIO生成动力学更快且更忠实遵循用户动作。

Conclusion: PRIO将重建与生成场景中的静态物体转化为可交互对象，为交互式4D世界模型提供高效、可泛化的物理表示。

Abstract: 3D worlds can now be captured, reconstructed, and generated at scale, yet the objects in them stay still: a pressed pillow does not compress, and a pulled door does not swing. Bringing these objects into interactive 4D world models requires a representation of how they can move, deform, and respond physically to user actions in real time. We present PRIO, a pre-trained representation for interactive objects. Given static geometry, PRIO predicts an object-specific configuration space in a single forward pass. This space provides generalized coordinates for Lagrangian mechanics, allowing physical laws and user actions to govern motion. Configurations are linear in these coordinates, allowing the quantities needed by the dynamics to be precomputed. Dynamics then run at interactive rates without further network inference. Pre-trained on diverse 4D trajectories, PRIO learns priors over object configurations that generalize to unseen shapes. It supports meshes, point clouds, and 3D Gaussians, bringing objects in reconstructed and generated scenes into interaction. We demonstrate its use in real-to-sim workflows and robot manipulation. In addition, compared with existing 4D generation methods, PRIO generates dynamics faster and follows user actions more faithfully.

</details>


### [113] [Search or Scoring? Diagnosing Why Visual World-Model Planners Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25347)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Chenhao Wu,Zixiang Ni,Yingguang Yang,Feng Zhang,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Jifeng Zhu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文区分视觉世界模型规划失败的两种原因：搜索失败与评分失败，并提出诊断方法及基于演示窗口的自适应利用策略DemoProp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型规划器可能因优化器未探索到好动作（搜索失败）或因预测代价错误排序（评分失败）而失败，二者需不同补救，但规划器不记录失败类型。

Method: 通过执行优化器候选动作、用规划器自身代价给已知解打分、比较预测与真实结果代价来区分两类失败；建立误差模型解释失败成因，并据此决定演示窗口用法：预测误差小时热启动搜索，误差大时直接平均演示窗口而不经模型。

Result: 在四个仿真环境与三个世界模型中，短任务上PushT和Wall主要因搜索失败，Metaworld和RoboCasa因评分失败（代价在真实结果上AUROC 0.94，在预测上仅0.54）；四倍搜索仅对搜索失败有效；评分失败时直接平均窗口在Metaworld新任务上解决127/168，而模型选择最多87；PushT中仅退化模型即可翻转优劣，冻结规则跟随翻转解决678/960新回合，最佳固定用法为530。

Conclusion: 区分搜索失败与评分失败可指导补救选择，且基于预测误差的自适应演示利用规则优于固定策略。

Abstract: Visual world models such as DINO-WM and JEPA-WM plan with an optimizer that searches for actions whose predicted outcome has the lowest cost, its distance to a goal image. A plan can thus fail in two ways: the optimizer never tries a good action that the cost would prefer (a search failure), or the cost, computed on predictions, ranks a bad action first (a scoring failure). The two need different remedies, more search or a better model, but a planner does not record which one occurred. We tell them apart by executing the optimizer's candidates, scoring a known solution with the planner's own cost, and comparing costs on predicted and real outcomes. An error model explains when each arises and so how to use retrieved demonstration windows (DemoProp): start the search from them (a warm start) when prediction errors are small, and average them without the model when large errors make the optimizer pick overrated actions. Across four simulated environments and three world models, on short tasks whose goals the planner can represent, PushT and Wall fail mostly by search, while Metaworld and RoboCasa fail by scoring because the cost, sound on real outcomes (AUROC 0.94 on Metaworld), is at chance on predictions (0.54). The diagnosis tells which remedy pays off: a four times larger search lowers the real-outcome cost only where failures come from search (clearly for DINO-WM), and where they come from scoring, averaging the windows without the model solves 127 of 168 new Metaworld tasks, against at most 87 when the model chooses among them. Within PushT, degrading only the model flips which use is better, and our rule, frozen in advance, follows the flip and solves 678 of 960 new episodes, against 530 for the best fixed use.

</details>


### [114] [Accord: Universal Online Metric Localization and Dense Mapping from one Camera and an IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25279)
*Jaafar Mahmoud,Arthur Movsesyan,Cuong Huynh,Mikhail Iumanov,Sergey Zagoruyko,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Accord从单目相机加IMU构建在线稠密度量地图，通过VIO位姿锚定深度基础模型预测，并利用多视图双向深度一致性校正逐图尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO能提供度量运动但几何稀疏，前馈深度模型能预测稠密结构却缺乏度量尺度，二者单独都无法得到稠密度量地图。

Method: VIO滤波器输出度量位姿；以此为条件，深度基础模型在短关键帧窗口内预测深度；用滤波器位姿三角化的度量3D点锚定每个关键帧窗口；利用共视图像间的双向稠密深度一致性估计并校正逐图尺度误差，同时保持锚定建立的度量尺度；引入上下文关键帧提升深度预测；最后用因子图融合子图。

Result: 在五个公开相机-IMU配置和一个新的Phone-rig/GoPro数据集上，使用单一建图配置、不针对数据集调参、且每张地图仅用Accord自身轨迹对齐，Accord在每个平台5 cm阈值下均取得最优或次优平均F-score。

Conclusion: 多视图一致性（Accord）可作为SLAM中的几何自洽来源，实现跨平台一致的高精度在线稠密度量建图，并开源代码与手机支架数据集以促进研究。

Abstract: Dense metric mapping from a single camera and IMU remains challenging: visual-inertial odometry (VIO) provides metric motion but sparse geometry, whereas feed-forward depth models predict dense structure without ensuring metric scale. We present Accord, which constructs an accurate online dense metric map from any camera with an IMU. A VIO filter publishes metric poses, a depth foundation model conditioned on them predicts depth for short keyframe windows, and a factor graph fuses the resulting submaps. Recent work has explored several approaches to this problem. What is essential is the multi-view agreement (Accord), which is considered in SLAM as a source of geometric self-consistency. Accord anchors each keyframe window to metric 3D points triangulated from the filter poses, then exploits bidirectional dense depth agreement between co-visible images to estimate and correct per-image scale errors while preserving the metric scale established by the anchors. Context keyframes further improve depth prediction, and the resulting submaps are fused in a factor graph. We evaluate a single mapping configuration across five public camera-IMU setups and a new Phone-rig/GoPro dataset. With nothing tuned per dataset/setup and every map aligned only by Accord's own trajectory, Accord achieves consistently best/second-best mean F-score at 5 cm on every platform. Accord is open-source along with the phone-rig dataset for the benefit of the research community

</details>


### [115] [Do Deep Stereo Models Learn Shape Priors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25113)
*Linbo Tang,Aneel Damaraju,Todd Zickler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究深度立体模型在局部线索微弱时边界预测的局限性，并提出联合预测视差与距离场的生成式立体模型以改进边界定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体边界定位对具身视觉系统至关重要，微小边界误差会改变物理表面的几何推断；作者想探究当前立体模型在弱局部线索下是否遵循简单形状先验。

Method: 构建一个由高度受限形状族组成的合成立体数据集（随机化深度、倾斜、颜色和纹理），测试多种先进立体模型；随后提出一个简单生成式立体模型，联合预测视差和表示物体边界的距离场。

Result: 多个先进立体模型即使在该简单数据集上微调或从头训练，仍存在系统性边界误差；所提生成式模型改善了边界定位，生成的轮廓更贴合底层形状空间。

Conclusion: 当前立体架构在局部证据微弱时边界预测存在局限，需进一步研究生成式模型能否更好地结合区域分组证据与全局几何结构。

Abstract: Localizing object boundaries is important for embodied visual systems, since small boundary errors can change the inferred geometry of physical surfaces. We study whether deep stereo models produce boundaries consistent with simple shape priors when local image and disparity cues are weak. We create a synthetic stereo dataset consisting of objects drawn from a highly constrained family of shapes, with randomized depth, slant, color and texture. Despite the simplicity of this shape space, we find that several state-of-the-art stereo models retain systematic boundary errors, even when finetuned or trained from scratch on the dataset. We then introduce a simple generative stereo model that jointly predicts disparity and a distance field representing object boundaries. The model improves boundary localization and produces contours that adhere more closely to the underlying shape space. These results expose limitations in the boundary predictions of current stereo architectures when local evidence is weak, and motivate further study of whether generative models can better combine regional grouping evidence with global geometric structure.

</details>


### [116] [EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer's Passage in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24995)
*Hongyu Wang,Chuhan Li,Congjin Xu,Xuandong Zhao,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoPass-Bench基准，评估多模态基础模型在真实第一人称视频中对观察者-实体交互的理解，发现运动感知与交互理解之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多模态基础模型的评估多关注观察者定位、运动轨迹和动作可行性，但对不断演化的观察者-实体交互的发现与解释评估不足。

Method: 构建EgoPass-Bench，包含用Ray-Ban Meta（Gen 2）智能眼镜录制的284段人工审核评估片段，以及覆盖五项任务（运动感知、指定实体的交互判断、无目标线索的相关实体发现）的2,201个问题；评估八种基于帧的配置和六种原生视频配置。

Result: 最强模型GPT-6 Astra在同一目标的394次比较中正确识别物体运动达91.37%，但仅66.50%正确识别通行约束变化；在574个实体发现问题中落后双人人类基线23.65±7.45个百分点（配对95% CI），但至少高出始终为空基线（36.76%）18.9个百分点；模型常纳入既无合理物理冲突也不约束通行的实体。

Conclusion: 准确的运动识别并不能保证可靠的相关实体发现，当前多模态基础模型在观察者-实体交互理解方面仍有明显不足。

Abstract: Understanding an egocentric environment requires reasoning about how surrounding entities affect the observer's actions. Existing evaluations of multimodal foundation models (MFMs) examine observer localization, motion trajectories, and action feasibility, but leave the discovery and interpretation of evolving observer-entity interactions insufficiently assessed. We introduce EgoPass-Bench, a benchmark for observer-relative interaction understanding in real-world egocentric video. EgoPass-Bench comprises 284 manually reviewed evaluation clips recorded using Ray-Ban Meta (Gen 2) smart glasses and 2,201 distinct questions across five tasks assessing motion perception, interaction judgments about specified entities, and relevant-entity discovery without target cues. Our evaluation of eight frame-based and six native-video configurations reveals a pronounced discrepancy between motion perception and interaction understanding. The strongest evaluated model, GPT-6 Astra, correctly recognizes object motion in 91.37% of 394 comparisons involving the same targets, but correctly identifies changes in passage constraints in only 66.50%. Across all 574 entity-discovery questions, it trails the two-person human baseline by $23.65 \pm 7.45$ percentage points (paired 95% CI), while exceeding the always-empty baseline (36.76%) by at least 18.9 percentage points. Further analysis reveals frequent inclusion of entities that neither pose a plausible physical conflict nor constrain passage, showing that accurate motion recognition does not ensure reliable relevant-entity discovery.

</details>


### [117] [SteerTrace: Tracing How Target Attacks Steer World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25719)
*Yukun Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SteerTrace，一种对世界动作模型（WAM）的通用定向对抗攻击，并追踪其内部效应来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM同时预测未来视频与动作，其想象的未来常被视为安全信号；作者要检验这种安全性是否可靠，并定位攻击效应在模型内部的作用位置。

Method: 为每个模型学习一个ℓ∞≤4/255、跨帧/任务/视角共享的通用扰动，将执行动作推向攻击者指定目标；在LIBERO与RoboTwin上测试四个家族共八个WAM；通过恢复未来token、当前帧token及视频专家第10–29层中0.36%的FFN神经元等干预，用带符号定向效应量化各组件对攻击效果的贡献。

Result: 攻击使任务成功率接近零并显著偏移动作；同预算噪声与BadWAM几乎不产生定向偏移。恢复未来token在五个动作专家关注未来token的模型中消除61–94%效应；约束未来预测接近干净值时，三个模型中恢复当前帧token消除87–99%（未来token仅9–15%）；六个模型中恢复0.36%的FFN神经元消除72–96%；残差方向2–16仅含11–18%偏移能量，但移除后大部分定向效应被抵消。

Conclusion: WAM的想象未来不足以作为可靠安全信号，定向攻击效应可集中在少量未来/当前帧token与极少数FFN神经元及低能量残差方向上，为WAM的安全评估与防御提供可解释的追踪依据。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict future videos and actions with one backbone, and their imagined future is often seen as a safety signal. We introduce SteerTrace, a targeted universal attack on WAMs that also traces its effect inside the model. One $\ell_\infty$-bounded (4/255) perturbation per model, shared across frames, tasks, and camera views, pushes the executed action toward an attacker-chosen target. On eight WAMs from four families (FastWAM, Faster-WAM, OpenWAM and LingBot-VA) on LIBERO and RoboTwin, it drives task success close to zero and moves the attacked action toward the target. Same-budget noise and BadWAM produce little action shift in the target direction. We use the signed targeted effect to measure how much of the effect each intervention removes or reproduces. Under the standard attack, restoring future tokens removes 61-94% of the targeted effect in the five models whose action experts attend to them. When the attack is constrained to keep the predicted future close to the clean prediction, the targeted effect can become more dependent on current-frame tokens. In three of these five models, restoring current-frame tokens removes 87-99% of the effect, compared with 9-15% for future tokens. In six models, restoring just 0.36% of the FFN neurons in layers 10-29 of the video expert removes 72-96% of the targeted effect. Residual directions 2-16 hold only 11-18% of the shift energy, yet removing them cancels most of the targeted effect.

</details>


### [118] [Fast and Accurate Body Estimation in Pixels, World Space and Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25801)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAB直接预测人体骨架关节与稠密表面点，联合提升推理效率与身体重建保真度，可实时运行并支持世界空间运动估计与人到人形机器人的重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只预测参数化网格模型参数，推理效率与重建保真度难以兼顾，且缺乏与场景接触及人形机器人重定向的高效统一方案。

Method: FAB预测稠密2D与3D身体点及MHR头部参数，再用一次学习式拟合加两步高斯-牛顿优化得到SMPL；其ViT-L学生模型FAB-L从微调的SAM 3D Body蒸馏而来；同一网络预测足跟、脚趾、手和膝盖接触，并估计世界空间运动、融合可见人周围静态场景深度、利用接触对齐身体与场景；还引入G1头部与FAB-Retarget运动级重定向器，将稠密点或拟合网格映射到Unitree G1。

Result: 在RTX 4070 Ti SUPER上单人实时运行，从检测到拟合身体的速度为SAM 3D Body eager-PyTorch仅身体路径的4.6倍，并在多数相机空间基准上提升精度；FAB-Retarget生成的参考比SOMA、GMR、UMR、OmniRetarget等专用重定向器更贴合人体，且显著优于用序列IK重定向的Fast SAM 3D Body。

Conclusion: FAB以直接预测稠密点与接触的方式，在实时效率与重建精度上取得联合提升，并有效支持场景对齐与人到人形机器人的真实到仿真运动迁移。

Abstract: We present FAB (Fast and Accurate Body estimation), a human body estimator that jointly improves inference efficiency and body-recovery fidelity. Rather than predicting only parametric mesh-model parameters, FAB directly predicts human skeleton joints and dense surface points. In images, it predicts dense 2D and 3D body points alongside a parametric Momentum Human Rig (MHR) head, then fits SMPL in one learned pass followed by two Gauss-Newton steps. Its ViT-L student, FAB-L, distilled from a fine-tuned SAM 3D Body, runs in real time for one person on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, from detection to a fitted body, at 4.6x the speed of SAM 3D Body's eager-PyTorch body-only path, while improving accuracy on most camera-space benchmarks. The same network predicts heel, toe, hand, and knee contacts, which connect the body to the scene. FAB also estimates world-space motion, fuses static-scene depth around the visible person, and aligns body and scene using predicted contacts. For human-to-humanoid real-to-sim transfer, an in-model G1 head and FAB-Retarget, a motion-level retargeter that reads dense points or the fitted mesh, map motion to a Unitree G1. FAB-Retarget produces references that follow the person more closely than specialized retargeters including SOMA, GMR, UMR, and OmniRetarget, and substantially more closely than Fast SAM 3D Body retargeted with our sequence IK. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [119] [LoopedWAM: Looped World Action Models for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25595)
*Anh Dao,Xinxin Liu,Hang Yu,Jeffrey A Chan Santiago,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopedWAM是一种紧凑的世界动作模型，通过循环共享六块核心在视频和动作专家中执行30层转换器但仅存储12块，并采用弹性自蒸馏LoopedWAM-E实现多精度-延迟工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型耦合生成式视频建模与机器人动作预测，但通常需要大型视频和动作转换器，限制了部署效率。

Method: 提出LoopedWAM：每个专家循环应用共享的六块核心，执行30层转换器但仅存储12块；视频与动作专家可不同深度（视觉特征每查询计算一次，动作每采样步重复）；进一步提出弹性自蒸馏训练单一弹性检查点LoopedWAM-E，覆盖动作深度和采样步预算。

Result: 仅0.58B策略参数且无机器人预训练，LoopedWAM在LIBERO达96.1%成功率、RoboTwin 2.0达91.9%、RoboCasaGR1达52.2%，峰值GPU内存比Fast-WAM少7.4倍；LoopedWAM-E在LIBERO上27.6ms达96.0%或24.7ms达95.2%，两个采样步和四个动作循环下三个基准均与LoopedWAM差距0.5点内；消融显示权重共享深度保持竞争力，激进减少动作深度需预算感知训练；真实AgileX PiPER机械臂80次试验四任务成功率71.3%。

Conclusion: LoopedWAM以紧凑架构和弹性自蒸馏在多个基准上实现高成功率与低内存，并提供单一检查点的多精度-延迟权衡，验证了权重共享深度和预算感知训练的有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) couple generative video modeling with robotic action prediction but typically require large video and action transformers, limiting deployment efficiency. We present LoopedWAM, a compact WAM that recurrently applies a shared six-block core in each expert, executing 30 transformer layers while storing only 12 blocks per expert. To account for their asymmetric inference costs, the video and action experts can operate at different depths: visual features are computed once per query, whereas action computation repeats at every sampling step. We further propose elastic self-distillation, which trains a single elastic checkpoint, LoopedWAM-E, across action-depth and sampling-step budgets. With only 0.58B policy parameters and no robot pretraining, LoopedWAM achieves 96.1% success on LIBERO, 91.9% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 52.2% on RoboCasaGR1, while using $7.4\times$ less peak GPU memory than Fast-WAM under matched inference measurements. On LIBERO, LoopedWAM-E achieves 96.0% success in 27.6 ms or 95.2% in 24.7 ms on an H100, providing multiple accuracy-latency operating points from a single checkpoint; with two sampling steps and four action loops, it stays within 0.5 points of LoopedWAM on all three benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate that weight-shared depth preserves competitive performance, while aggressive action-depth reduction requires explicit budget-aware training. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, LoopedWAM succeeds in 71.3% of 80 trials across four tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Mesh-Free Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25513)
*Junghoon Seo,Hakjin Lee,Jaehoon Sim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MORSEPose，利用标准位姿与尺寸标注构造容忍膨胀包络来筛选可见点作为重建目标，用于类别级6D位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖将观测点与注册实例表面的距离来选取重建目标点，需要额外注册表面；本文希望直接从标准位姿和度量尺寸标注导出该支撑集。

Method: 将观测3D点映射到归一化物体坐标，保留落在容忍膨胀的位姿-尺寸包络内的点；该构造跨相机帧与度量尺度一致，并与多级RGB-D特征、自适应关键点和可学习类别上下文集成。

Result: 在REAL275和HouseCat6D上，包络监督在点级和任务级均接近基于表面的监督，对边界扰动和视角变化稳定，并取得有竞争力的类别级位姿精度。

Conclusion: 基于位姿与尺寸标注的膨胀包络可作为表面距离选择器的保守替代，稳定有效地支持类别级6D位姿估计。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose estimation relates partial RGB-D observations of unseen instances to a canonical frame shared across a category. Object-aware reconstruction helps adaptive keypoints retain the geometry of the visible surface. Common reconstruction targets select observed points by their distance to a registered instance surface. We present MORSEPose, which derives this support directly from standard pose and metric-size annotations. We map observed 3D points to normalized object coordinates and retain those inside a tolerancedilated pose-and-size envelope. This construction is consistent across camera frames and metric scales. When the annotated extent bounds the object surface, the envelope conservatively contains the support of a conventional surface-distance selector. We integrate this target with multi-level RGB-D features, adaptive keypoints, and learnable category context. Experiments on REAL275 and HouseCat6D show that the envelope closely matches surface-based supervision at both point and task levels, remains stable under boundary perturbations and viewpoint changes, and supports competitive category-level pose accuracy across benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [TuringPAI: A Foundation for On-Device Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25474)
*Qiman Wu,Hanlin Chen,Yuhua Wei,Mingyuan Wang,Shaman Tang,Minghao Li,Rui Xin,Xingye Liu,Zhichao Yang,Yiqi Yu,Hongyan Pei,Xiaowen Tian,YingYing Liu,Shiwen Liu,Jiahui Hu,Yizhao Wang,Yuheng Zhang,Anhua Liu,Yue He,Shuang Su,Da Zhu,Hua Zhou,Pengfei Diao,Haojie Yang,Weichao Huang,Hang Zhang,Honggou Yang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了面向端侧物理AI的TuringPAI模型家族，包含视觉语言理解、自动驾驶与机器人操作三个模型，在有限算力下实现了高效推理与良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI需要在有限的车载/端侧算力下理解空间关系与动态环境并将观测转化为动作，现有模型难以兼顾通用理解与下游任务的高效部署。

Method: 构建0.65B的TuringPAI-VL，基于HybridViT-MLQ融合卷积局部聚合与Transformer全局建模，并用多尺度潜在查询（MLQ）整合空间特征与观测历史作为语言模型的紧凑接口；训练结合通用视觉语言数据与几何、跨视角、时序监督，并以此为共享初始化派生TuringPAI-Drive（自动驾驶）和TuringPAI-Embodied（机器人操作）两个VLA模型。

Result: TuringPAI-VL在定量几何感知上优于Qwen2-VL-2B；TuringPAI-Drive所有ADE/FDE指标与全token HybridViT基线差距在1.2%以内，内部仿真综合得分相当，并在单颗NVIDIA Orin-X上通过XNRT以12Hz生成轨迹；TuringPAI-Embodied在四个LIBERO套件上平均成功率达96.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，共享的视觉语言基础模型可在实际端侧约束下同时支撑自动驾驶与机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Physical AI requires models that understand spatial relationships and dynamic environments and translate observations into actions under limited onboard compute. We present TuringPAI, a family of models for on-device Physical AI comprising TuringPAI-VL for shared vision-language understanding, TuringPAI-Drive for autonomous driving, and TuringPAI-Embodied for robotic manipulation. TuringPAI-VL is a 0.65B model built on HybridViT-MLQ, which combines convolutional local aggregation with Transformer-based global modeling. Multi-scale Latent Queries integrate spatial features and observation history within the same visual Transformer, providing a compact, structured interface to the language model. Training combines general vision-language data with geometric, cross-view, and temporal supervision. TuringPAI-VL supports general multimodal understanding and improves quantitative geometric perception over Qwen2-VL-2B, while providing a shared initialization for the two downstream VLA models. For autonomous driving, TuringPAI-Drive maintains all evaluated ADE/FDE metrics within 1.2\% of a full-token HybridViT baseline using the MLQ interface and achieves comparable aggregate scores in an in-house simulation evaluation. Using XPeng Native Runtime (XNRT) on a single NVIDIA Orin-X SoC, TuringPAI-Drive generates trajectories at 12\,Hz in on-vehicle operation. For robotic manipulation, TuringPAI-Embodied achieves a 96.8\% average success rate across the four LIBERO suites. These results demonstrate a shared vision-language foundation for driving and robotic manipulation under practical on-device constraints.

</details>


### [122] [MIR-Depth: Multi-Range Refinement for Single-View RGB-D Depth Completion of Transparent Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25417)
*Xinzhe Fu,Qi Shao,Yuanfei Xie,Taoyu Wu,Zhihao Cao,Long Hu,Hong Liang,Xianzhi Li,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang,Rongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIR-Depth 通过多范围假设与选择性循环细化，从单张 RGB-D 图像恢复透明物体可见表面深度，提升抓取位姿预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明物体因镜面反射导致深度缺失、折射导致背景深度污染，原始深度不可靠；单视角下固定采样预算的深度细化难以兼顾大折射偏移与局部精细分辨率。

Method: 构建坐标感知多范围假设：基于先验初始深度的全局逆深度体与深度轴滤波的逐像素局部体；采用选择性循环细化，引入越界查找掩码与有效性掩码 softmax 融合，通过多尺度 ConvGRU 迭代更新逆深度；最后用空间门控校正对全分辨率残差进行逐像素门控，保留初始几何且不硬约束原始传感器读数。

Result: 在 TransCG、TODD、TRansPose、ClearPose、HouseCat6D 上误差低于 ReMake、DFNet、BP-Net、PSD；真实机器人实验验证了用于 6-DoF 抓取位姿预测的准确表面几何。

Conclusion: MIR-Depth 有效解决单视角透明物体深度恢复中的折射与反射问题，在多个数据集和真实抓取任务中表现优越。

Abstract: Recovering accurate visible-surface depth from a single RGB-D image and an object mask is essential for robotic grasping and manipulation of transparent objects. Specular reflections cause missing depth, while refraction corrupts nonzero returns with background depth, making raw measurements unreliable as surface anchors. Under a fixed sampling budget, refining an initial single-view prediction within a single search interval either misses large refraction offsets or sacrifices fine local resolution. To resolve this single-view trade-off, MIR-Depth constructs Coordinate-Aware Multi-Range Hypotheses: a 3D-aggregated global inverse-depth volume and depth-axis-filtered pixel-wise local volumes around a prior-guided initial depth. Selective Recurrent Refinement then introduces out-of-bounds lookup masking and validity-masked softmax fusion to iteratively update inverse depth from these volumes using a multi-scale ConvGRU. Finally, Spatially Gated Correction applies a learned per-pixel spatial gate to the full-resolution residual, preserving accurate initial geometry without imposing raw sensor readings as hard constraints. Experiments on TransCG, TODD, TRansPose, ClearPose, and HouseCat6D show that MIR-Depth achieves lower error than ReMake, DFNet, BP-Net, and PSD. Real-robot evaluations confirm accurate surface geometry for 6-DoF grasp pose prediction. Code and pretrained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [123] [Phantom Success, Silent Failure: Auditing the Success Checkers of Simulated Manipulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25179)
*Zirui Song,Jiadong Zhang,Zheng Lu,Xiaosong Ma,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimAudit是一个基于大语言模型的流水线，通过分析任务指令与成功检查器代码的潜在分歧、在仿真中重建这些场景并多视角验证，审计仿真操作基准中成功检查器的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写成功检查器仅证明能接受专家演示的一种完成方式，而学习策略可能部分完成或用不同方式完成任务，导致检查器误判为成功（幻影成功）或误判失败（静默失败）。

Method: SimAudit先分析任务指令与检查器代码以识别二者可能不一致的场景，在仿真中重建这些场景并运行原始检查器，再用多视角渲染验证观察到的行为是否满足指令。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、RoboCasa365和RoboDojo的183个仿真测试用例中均产生预测的检查器判定；经视觉验证和去重后在49个任务中发现61个不同缺陷，包括40个幻影成功和21个静默失败，其中RoboCasa365多为接受不完整执行、RoboDojo多为拒绝有效执行、RoboTwin 2.0两类错误均常见。

Conclusion: 验证成功检查器需同时测试不完整执行和专家演示之外的合法替代方案，并推动未来基准采用更严格的评估协议。

Abstract: Simulated manipulation benchmarks measure policy performance using hand-written success checkers that label each episode as a success or failure. Passing an expert demonstration shows that a checker accepts one way of completing the task. Learned policies, however, may stop after partial progress or complete the task in a different way. A checker may then accept an incomplete execution (phantom success) or reject an execution that satisfies the instruction (silent failure). In this paper, we introduce SimAudit, an LLM-based pipeline that audits success checkers against task instructions. SimAudit analyzes the instruction and checker code to identify scenarios where the two may disagree, recreates those scenarios in simulation, and runs the original checker. Multi-view renderings are then used to verify whether the observed behavior satisfies the instruction. Across RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa365, and RoboDojo, 183 simulated test cases produced the predicted checker verdict. Visual verification and deduplication yielded 61 distinct defects across 49 tasks, including 40 phantom successes and 21 silent failures. Most defects in RoboCasa365 accepted incomplete executions, most in RoboDojo rejected valid executions, and both error types were common in RoboTwin 2.0. These findings suggest that validating success checkers requires testing both incomplete executions and valid alternatives to expert demonstrations, and motivate more rigorous evaluation protocols for future benchmarks.

</details>


### [124] [One Prior, Two Dynamics: Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Self-Supervised Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26617)
*Yuxin Jiang,Wenqiang Sun,Jiaqi Li,Yuchao Gu,Lvmin Zhang,Ivor Tsang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: O$^{2}$LaF利用预训练视频生成器作为先验，同时学习逆动力学（从视觉变化推断动作）与正动力学（预测动作后果），在少样本动作探测和零样本动作迁移上达到新最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频能反映世界变化但缺少导致变化的动作标注；现有潜在动作学习几乎从零学习动态，未充分利用预训练视频模型的先验知识。

Method: 基于“一个先验、两种动态”原则，从同一预训练视频DiT初始化两个模型：带可学习动作查询的前馈过渡编码器（逆动力学）和因果的、动作条件的视频生成器（正动力学）；通过控制信息访问和紧凑瓶颈端到端联合优化两种动态，从无标注视频中学习可迁移动作。

Result: 在导航和机器人操作任务上，O$^{2}$LaF在少样本动作探测上刷新最优，并在跨视觉上下文与跨具身场景中实现更强的零样本动作迁移；框架还可扩展到V-JEPA2等预测性表示先验并取得有竞争力表现。

Conclusion: 将预训练视频模型重新用于发现可迁移动作并预测其后果，为潜在动作学习提供了一种更广泛的范式。

Abstract: Videos reveal how the world changes, but rarely the actions that cause those changes. Latent action learning recovers such supervision from visual transitions, yet often learns the underlying dynamics largely from scratch. We ask whether a pretrained video generator can instead provide the prior for both inverse and forward dynamics. We introduce O$^{2}$LaF, built on a simple principle: one prior, two dynamics. We initialize both models from the same pretrained video DiT and adapt them to their distinct roles: a feed-forward transition encoder with learnable action queries for inverse dynamics, and a causal, action-conditioned video generator for forward dynamics. By controlling information access and optimizing both dynamics end-to-end through a compact bottleneck, O$^{2}$LaF learns transferable actions from unlabeled videos. Across navigation and robotic manipulation, O$^{2}$LaF sets a new state of the art in few-shot action probing and enables stronger zero-shot action transfer across visual contexts and embodiments. Beyond generative video priors, we further extend our framework to predictive representation priors such as V-JEPA2, achieving competitive performance. Our findings suggest a broader paradigm for latent-action learning: repurposing pretrained video models to discover transferable actions and predict their consequences.

</details>


### [125] [Backbone Matters: Benchmarking Encoder-Decoder Networks for Semantic Segmentation of Driving Scenes on BDD100K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26191)
*Ehsan Rassekh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该系统比较了BDD100K驾驶数据集上4种解码器与13种骨干网络的组合，最佳为FPN+EfficientNet-B4，达62.6% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割是自动驾驶场景理解的核心，但现有网络常随意选择骨干网络而缺乏系统比较，因此需要研究骨干与解码器组合对分割性能的影响。

Method: 在BDD100K数据集上，将4种解码器（U-Net、FPN、LinkNet、PSPNet）与来自6个家族的13种骨干网络（ResNet、VGG、DenseNet、Inception、MobileNet、EfficientNet）组合，共52个模型采用统一训练协议进行训练和评估。

Result: 最佳模型为FPN解码器搭配EfficientNet-B4骨干，取得62.6% mIoU、89.8%准确率和0.092损失；52个模型中仅两个（均为FPN+EfficientNet）超过BDD100K基准的56.9% mIoU；骨干效果强烈依赖解码器，同一EfficientNet-B4在PSPNet下仅42.6% mIoU而在FPN下达62.6%，最佳与最差骨干的差距从12.0个百分点（U-Net）到42.5个百分点（FPN）不等。

Conclusion: 骨干网络的选择对分割性能影响显著且与解码器强相关，FPN+EfficientNet组合表现最优，研究结果与适用范围需进一步讨论。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation gives an autonomous vehicle a dense, pixel-level description of its surroundings and is a core part of scene understanding. Most segmentation networks consist of an encoder (the backbone) and a decoder, yet the backbone is often chosen without a systematic comparison. This work studies this choice on the BDD100K driving dataset. Four widely used decoders (U-Net, FPN, LinkNet and PSPNet) are combined with 13 backbones from six families (ResNet, VGG, DenseNet, Inception, MobileNet and EfficientNet), and all 52 models are trained with one common protocol. The best model, an FPN decoder with an EfficientNet-B4 backbone, reaches 62.6% mean intersection over union (mIoU), 89.8% accuracy and a loss of 0.092. Only two of the 52 models, both FPN with EfficientNet, exceed the 56.9% mIoU reported with the BDD100K benchmark. The effect of the backbone depends strongly on the decoder: the same EfficientNet-B4 gives 42.6% mIoU with PSPNet but 62.6% with FPN, and the gap between the best and the worst backbone ranges from 12.0 points (U-Net) to 42.5 points (FPN). The results and the scope of this study are discussed.

</details>


### [126] [Fixing the Twin, Not the Detector: Sim-to-Real Roadside LiDAR Detection from Digital-Twin Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26035)
*KuanLin Chen,Feng-jui Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套用于路边LiDAR检测器的Sim2Real流程，在不使用真实标签的情况下，仅用合成数据训练CenterPoint模型，在UrbanTwin挑战赛LUMPI赛道获得第一名、V2X-Real赛道第五名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路边LiDAR检测器在真实标签上训练成本高昂，数字孪生有望替代真实标签，但孪生数据与真实场景在传感器、目标和标注约定上存在差异，导致直接迁移效果差。

Method: 仅用合成数据在单张8GB笔记本GPU上训练CenterPoint模型；对齐孪生数据的坐标、点密度和框约定；生成孪生缺失的行人和骑行者，并加入街道杂物作为无标签负样本；按真实尺寸和点密度重建孪生目标；采用非学习式重采样使每格点密度匹配无标签真实帧；最后使用无标签后处理，利用固定路边传感器的静态结构重复性和真实目标的点统计特征。

Result: 非学习式重采样将LUMPI真实度从0.589提升至0.947（真实帧之间为0.949）；LUMPI赛道综合得分0.5541排名第一，V2X-Real赛道得分0.8102排名第五；系统性Sim2Real差异的修正在测试集上保留了验证增益的四分之一到四分之三，而针对少量验证目标拟合的选择无法迁移。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要来自修正孪生与真实站点之间的系统性差异，而非检测器本身；系统性Sim2Real修正能够有效迁移到测试集，而依赖验证集少量目标的拟合选择不具备迁移性。

Abstract: Roadside LiDAR detectors are expensive to train on real labels, and digital twins promise to replace these labels, but a detector trained on a twin meets a different sensor, different objects and different annotation conventions on the real site. We describe the pipeline that placed first in the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge (IEEE BigData 2026 Cup, 0.5541 combined score) and fifth on its V2X-Real track (0.8102). Every detector is a CenterPoint model trained only on synthetic data, on a single 8 GB laptop GPU. Most of the gain came from correcting differences between the twin and the real site rather than from the detector. We align the twin's coordinates, point density and box convention with the test sensor, generate the pedestrians and riders that the twins lack, plus street clutter as unlabeled negatives, and rebuild twin objects at real size and real point density. A non-learned resampling to the per-cell point density of unlabeled real frames lifts LUMPI realism from 0.589 to 0.947, against 0.949 between real frames. Label-free post-processing then exploits what a fixed roadside sensor guarantees: static structures recur in every frame, and real objects have characteristic point statistics. Reporting the validation and test gain of each component side by side, we find that corrections of systematic sim-to-real differences transfer to the test set, typically keeping a quarter to three quarters of their validation gain, while choices that fit the few objects of a validation set do not.

</details>


### [127] [Driver Gaze Target Identification via Gaze Estimation and Structured Driving Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25771)
*Xinze Cao,Ke Wang,Deyong Guan,Yuanquan Li,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个模块化框架，从同步的车内驾驶员图像和前视道路图像中识别物体或区域级注视目标；实验室实验Top-1准确率达96.27%，LBW数据集上任务相关物体为79.92%、道路类为85.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注视区域分类和连续注意定位只能提供有限信息，无法确定所注视场景目标的具体身份，因此需要物体级或区域级的注视目标识别为智能车辆视觉注意力监测提供支持。

Method: 框架由四部分组成：MFGaze（结合MAE预训练Vision Transformer与通过FiLM进行头部姿态条件化）、全景场景理解构建结构化候选集、多项式岭回归经个体化校准将注视特征映射到2D场景图像坐标、以及熵驱动目标推理自适应融合边界距离几何与类别级语义先验。

Result: 实验室实验整体Top-1注视目标识别准确率96.27%；序列特定校准后LBW上任务相关物体Top-1为79.92%、道路类为85.58%；MFGaze在MPIIFaceGaze、ETH-XGaze和Gaze360上平均角度误差分别为4.06°、4.23°和10.52°，为对比方法中最低且具强跨数据集泛化能力；敏感性分析显示超参数扰动下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架可作为驾驶员监测中物体级注视信息的来源。

Abstract: Identifying a driver's gaze target provides object-level information for visual attention monitoring in intelligent vehicles. Gaze-zone classification and continuous attention localization provide limited information about the identity of the attended scene target. This paper proposes a modular framework that identifies object- or region-level gaze targets from synchronized in-cabin driver images and forward-view road images. The framework comprises four components: MFGaze, which combines a Vision Transformer pretrained with a masked autoencoder (MAE) and head-pose conditioning through feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM); panoptic scene understanding, which constructs a structured candidate set; polynomial ridge regression, which maps gaze features to 2D scene-image coordinates through individualized calibration; and entropy-driven target inference, which adaptively fuses boundary-distance geometry with class-level semantic priors. Experiments evaluate the framework using three public gaze datasets, a laboratory test using Cityscapes road scenes and self-collected gaze data, and the Look Both Ways (LBW) dataset. The complete framework achieves an overall Top-1 gaze target identification accuracy of 96.27% in the laboratory experiment. After sequence-specific calibration, it achieves Top-1 accuracies of 79.92% for task-relevant objects and 85.58% for the road class on LBW. MFGaze achieves the lowest mean angular errors among the compared methods, with 4.06$^\circ$, 4.23$^\circ$, and 10.52$^\circ$ on MPIIFaceGaze, ETH-XGaze, and Gaze360, respectively, and demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. Sensitivity analyses show stable performance under hyperparameter perturbations. These results support the framework as a source of object-level gaze information for driver monitoring.

</details>


### [128] [What One Sample Cannot See: Why Fewer Denoising Steps Can Score Higher on Physics Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25632)
*Yutao Li,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出Physics-IQ等视频世界模型评测因只与单一真实录像比较，可能奖励预测多样性坍缩；提出Kernel Physics-IQ以期望上消除该激励，仅需每条件两个独立样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成世界模型评测将生成视频与唯一真实录像对比，但场景可有多种合理未来，导致少步采样虽使视频模糊却可能提高物理得分这一悖论。

Method: 通过精确分解揭示Physics-IQ得分可奖励生成未来间变异减小；提出Kernel Physics-IQ，用核方法在期望上移除该激励，每条件仅需两个独立样本，并在五个图生视频模型及已知结果概率的受控物理系统中验证。

Result: 两步采样在全部五个图生视频模型中降低Kernel Physics-IQ；CogVideoX官方得分上升0.055而新指标下降0.131；改变采样计划揭示官方得分遗漏的差异；受控系统中新指标偏好真实分布而非坍缩预测。

Conclusion: 世界模型不应只以单个视频与已发生事件的匹配程度评判，还应以其预测对可能发生情况的表征忠实度来评判。

Abstract: When a storm can head north or south, a forecast down the middle may score better under squared error than either plausible path. The same paradox appears when video generators are tested as world models: fewer denoising steps can raise physics scores even as videos blur or stop moving early. Why? Physics-IQ compares each generated video with one recording, although a scene can have multiple valid futures. An exact decomposition shows its scores can reward reduced variation among generated futures, even when their distribution becomes less accurate. We introduce Kernel Physics-IQ to remove this incentive in expectation, needing only two independent samples per condition. Two-step sampling lowers Kernel Physics-IQ in all five image-to-video models. For CogVideoX, the official score rises by 0.055 while ours falls by 0.131. Changing the sampling schedule reveals further differences official scores miss. In controlled physical systems with known outcome probabilities, our metric favors the true distribution over collapsed predictions. In short, a world model should be judged not only by how closely one video matches what happened, but by how faithfully its predictions represent what could have happened.

</details>


### [129] [Physical Laws without Physics Loss? Probing Learned Dynamics in Video World Models through Forced Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25626)
*Yutao Li,Chen Wang,Wangmeng Zuo,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过非线性受迫响应任务检验视频世界模型是否真正学习物理规律而非仅模仿运动，发现模型能再现线性响应与高阶非线性动力学，但学习到的动力学在状态估计或传播出错时可能失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有整体运动与外观评分无法判断视频世界模型是否在没有物理损失的情况下学习物理定律，还是仅模仿运动，因此需要一个可量化的物理测试框架来探究其能力边界。

Method: 引入双视觉-数值条件，适配六个图像到视频骨干网络，仅用流匹配损失预测受迫振动运动；设计基于动力学与几何泰勒展开的三层测试（线性响应、高阶动力学、非线性软化），并扩展到双摆与摇摆块。

Result: Wan能准确预测运动并泛化到未见激励、物体、姿态和场景；反事实测试表明其能力超越训练视频回放；六个模型均再现线性响应（含共振），五个捕获高阶动力学（含非线性软化），而软化在训练中占比仅0.74%；双摆与摇摆块显示持续动力学比短暂冲击学得更忠实。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型可在无物理损失下学习部分物理规律，但学习到的动力学在状态估计或传播失败时可能不显现；这些测试与发现可转化为改进视频训练与生成的策略。

Abstract: Do video world models learn physical laws without a physics loss, or merely imitate motion? Overall motion and appearance scores cannot settle it. We investigate this through nonlinear forced response: an excitation shakes the support of a suspended object. We introduce dual visual-numeric conditioning and adapt six image-to-video backbones to predict this motion using only the flow-matching loss. Wan predicts motion accurately and generalizes to unseen excitations, objects, poses, and scenes. Counterfactual tests suggest capabilities beyond replaying training videos. But what laws have they learned? We design three tiers of tests using Taylor expansions of the dynamics and geometry. All six reproduce the linear response, including resonance; five capture higher-order dynamics, including nonlinear softening. Remarkably, softening is barely present in training: only 0.74% of the swing's squared displacement. Extensions to a double pendulum and a rocking block show sustained dynamics are learned more faithfully than brief impacts. Yet learned dynamics may fail to appear when state estimation or propagation goes wrong. We turn these tests and findings into strategies to improve video training and generation.

</details>


### [130] [ActMem: From Latent Memory Reasoning to Grounded Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25461)
*Linfeng Dong,Jianwei Niu,Jiuyi Zhao,Ruibing Feng,Xinghao Wu,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Mu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActMem是一个在单一视觉-语言主干内将记忆推理与感知接地解耦的上下文记忆框架，通过潜在记忆思维链和感知注意力提升长时程操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA要么仅检索过去观测而不推理其含义，要么在共享上下文中对全部历史推理从而挤占当前感知；认知科学将记忆与感知视为不同系统，前者重构过去经验以决定做什么，后者指导如何行动。

Method: 基于原生视频理解的视觉-语言模型，ActMem从带时间戳的交互历史推断当前子任务，并将推理中形成的隐藏状态保留为潜在记忆思维链（Memory CoT），用于条件化流匹配动作专家；感知注意力使该专家直接关注当前视图；高效Memory CoT推理在连续决策间复用计算且不改变输出。

Result: 在RoboMemArena上达到71.1%任务成功率和80.8%完成成功率，分别超过最强基线7.5和8.7个百分点；在MIKASA-Robo上平均成功率73.8%，为所有对比方法中最佳；真实世界中在外生、内生和无痕记忆任务上平均成功率68.8%，比最强记忆增强VLA高10.0个百分点。

Conclusion: ActMem通过解耦记忆推理与感知接地，在仿真和真实长时程记忆任务上均优于现有方法，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in real-world long-horizon manipulation, where memory plays a crucial role. However, existing memory-augmented VLAs either retrieve past observations without reasoning about what they imply, or reason over the full history in a shared context where it crowds out current perception. Cognitive science treats them as distinct systems: memory reconstructs past experience to decide what to do, while perception guides how to act. We therefore propose ActMem, an in-context memory framework that decouples memory reasoning from perceptual grounding within a single vision-language backbone. Built on a vision-language model with native video understanding, ActMem infers the current subtask from a timestamped interaction history and keeps the hidden states formed during this inference as a Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought (Memory CoT), which conditions a flow-matching action expert. Perception Attention further lets the expert attend directly to the current view. To keep inference fast under long histories, Efficient Memory CoT Inference reuses computation across consecutive decisions without changing the output. ActMem achieves 71.1% task success and 80.8% completion success on RoboMemArena, surpassing the strongest baseline by 7.5 and 8.7 percentage points, and 73.8% average success on MIKASA-Robo, the best among all compared methods. In the real world, it reaches 68.8% average success on exogenous, endogenous, and traceless memory tasks, 10.0 percentage points above the strongest memory-augmented VLA. Code and checkpoints will be released at this https URL and this https URL .

</details>
